*Vita Anggraini,Cintya Bella,Bastian,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在Sentiment140数据集的10,000条推文子集上比较了传统逻辑回归（TF-IDF特征）与双向LSTM深度学习模型的情感分析性能。逻辑回归以73.5%的准确率优于BiLSTM的69.17%，且深度学习模型存在轻微过拟合。最终将模型部署到Hugging Face Spaces的Streamlit应用中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体数据的指数级增长亟需自动化系统来实时分析非结构化公众情感，传统与深度学习方法在该任务上的性能对比尚需系统评估。

Method: 使用Sentiment140数据集的10,000条推文子集，比较基于TF-IDF特征的逻辑回归模型与双向长短期记忆（BiLSTM）深度学习架构的情感分类性能。

Result: 逻辑回归准确率73.5%，BiLSTM准确率69.17%；深度学习模型出现轻微过拟合。

Conclusion: 对于中等规模的非正式文本数据，结合稳健特征提取的经典机器学习方法可优于更复杂的深度学习方法；模型被集成到Streamlit交互式应用并部署至Hugging Face Spaces。

Abstract: The exponential growth of social media has created an urgent need for automated systems to analyze unstructured public sentiment in real time. This study compares a traditional Logistic Regression model using TF-IDF features with a deep learning Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) architecture on a 10,000-tweet subset of the Sentiment140 dataset. Experimental results show that Logistic Regression outperformed BiLSTM, achieving an accuracy of 73.5% compared with 69.17%, while the deep learning model exhibited mild overfitting. These findings suggest that for medium-scale informal text data, classical machine learning with robust feature extraction can outperform more complex deep learning approaches. Finally, the trained models were integrated into an interactive web application using Streamlit and deployed on Hugging Face Spaces for public access.

</details>


### [38] [Storage Is Not Memory: A Retrieval-Centered Architecture for Agent Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04897)
*Joshua Adler,Guy Zehavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出True Memory架构，将智能体记忆系统从存储模式转向多阶段检索管线，以原始事件存储替代预提取策略，在纯SQLite环境中实现超越现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统采用"摄入时提取"原语，在查询未知前丢弃内容，导致检索时无法恢复被丢弃的信息，从根本上限制了记忆系统的召回能力。

Method: 提出六层True Memory架构，中心从存储模式转向多阶段检索管线，事件以逐字形式保存。整个系统运行在单一SQLite文件中，无需外部数据库、向量索引、图存储或GPU。

Result: True Memory Pro在LoCoMo上达93.0%（3次均值），对比Mem0的61.4%、Supermemory的65.4%、Zep约71%、EverMemOS的94.5%；LongMemEval达87.8%；BEAM-1M（百万token规模）达76.6%，超过Hindsight的73.9%。56配置消融实验显示最优配置族内差距仅1.3个百分点。

Conclusion: "摄入时提取"是智能体记忆的错误原语，保留原始事件并结合多阶段检索管线可显著提升记忆系统性能，且无需依赖外部基础设施。

Abstract: Extraction at ingestion is the wrong primitive for agent memory: content discarded before the query is known cannot be recovered at retrieval time. We propose True Memory, a six-layer architecture that shifts the center of the system from a storage schema to a multi-stage retrieval pipeline operating over events preserved verbatim. The full system runs as a single SQLite file on commodity CPU with no external database, vector index, graph store, or GPU. On LoCoMo (1,540 questions across 10 multi-session conversations), True Memory Pro reaches 93.0% accuracy (3-run mean) against 61.4% for Mem0, 65.4% for Supermemory, approximately 71% for Zep, and 94.5% for EverMemOS under a matched gpt-4.1-mini answer model. On LongMemEval (500 questions), True Memory Pro reaches 87.8% (3-run mean). On BEAM-1M (700 questions at the 1-million-token scale), True Memory Pro reaches 76.6% (3-run mean), above the prior published result of 73.9% for Hindsight. A 56-configuration ablation shows a 1.3-percentage-point spread within the top-performing configuration family.

</details>


### [39] [Rethinking Local Learning: A Cheaper and Faster Recipe for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04913)
*Hengyu Shi,Tianyang Han,Peizhe Wang,Zhiling Wang,Xu Yang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoPT提出了一种局部学习后训练策略，在Transformer中点处设置梯度边界，前半部分通过特征重建目标更新以保留预训练能力，后半部分通过任务目标学习，从而缩短反向传播路径、降低内存消耗并提升训练效率，同时保持与全量微调相当的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全量后训练（end-to-end）将任务梯度传播至模型全部深度，导致任务适应与全深度激活存储、长距离反向依赖以及任务梯度直接干扰预训练表征相耦合。当后训练监督信号远窄于预训练时，这种全深度反向耦合既昂贵又具有侵入性。

Method: LoPT在Transformer中点处设置显式梯度边界：后半部分（靠近输出端）从任务目标学习；前半部分（靠近输入端）通过轻量级特征重建目标进行更新，以保留有用表征并维持接口兼容性。梯度边界使得任务梯度只传播至模型后半部分，前半部分不受任务梯度的直接干扰。

Result: 实验表明LoPT在保持竞争力性能的同时，实现了更低的内存成本、更高的训练效率以及更好的预训练能力保留。

Conclusion: LoPT通过将梯度传播范围作为显式设计选择，有效解耦了任务适应与全深度反向传播之间的耦合，在效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡，尤其适用于后训练监督远窄于预训练的场景。

Abstract: LLM post-training typically propagates task gradients through the full depth of the model. Although this end-to-end structure is simple and general, it couples task adaptation to full-depth activation storage, long-range backward dependencies and direct task-gradient access to pretrained representations. We argue that this full-depth backward coupling can be unnecessarily expensive and intrusive, particularly when post-training supervision is much narrower than pre-training. To this end, we propose \textbf{LoPT}: Local-Learning Post-Training, a simple post-training strategy that makes gradient reach an explicit design choice. LoPT places a single gradient boundary at the transformer midpoint: the second-half block learns from the task objective, while the first-half block is updated by a lightweight feature-reconstruction objective to preserve useful representations and maintain interface compatibility. LoPT shortens the task-induced backward path while limiting direct interference from narrow task gradients on early-layer representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoPT achieves competitive performance with lower memory cost, higher training efficiency and better retention of pretrained capabilities. Our code is available at: https://github.com/HumyuShi/LoPT

</details>


### [40] [Unintended Negative Impacts of Promotional Language in Patent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04926)
*Bingkun Zhao,Chenwei Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了270万件美国专利申请，发现促销语言使用频率与专利授权、所有权转让和上诉成功率呈负相关（“促销惩罚”），但促销语言实际上反映了技术的组合新颖性和未来引用影响力，并非技术薄弱的掩盖。这种惩罚受审查员性别和经验等人类因素调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 促销语言在科学创新传播中已被广泛研究，但其在技术创新（专利）语境中的作用尚不明确。作者希望探究促销语言与专利评估结果之间的关系，揭示专利系统中可能存在的悖论。

Method: 使用包含135个促销词的经过验证的领域诊断词典，对270万件美国专利申请进行分析，研究促销词频率与专利评估结果之间的关联，并控制混杂因素、进行匹配样本分析，同时考察技术新颖性、未来引用影响以及审查员人类因素的调节作用。

Result: (1) 促销词频率与专利授权、所有权转让、上诉成功率呈负相关，最低和最高促销密度五分位数组之间的成功率差异为5.3-5.9个百分点；(2) 促销语言并非技术薄弱的掩盖，而是客观反映了组合新颖性和未来引用影响；(3) 男性审查员和经验丰富的审查员对促销叙事的接受度高于女性和新手审查员。

Conclusion: 专利系统中存在一种新兴悖论：促销语言虽反映了真实的技术创新性，却受到评估者的负面偏见。研究为通过更客观地审视专利文件中的语言模式来改进专利评估提供了理论和实践启示。

Abstract: Promotional language has been increasingly used to aid the communication of innovative ideas in science. Yet, less is known about its role in the context of technological innovation. Here, we use a validated and domain-diagnosed lexicon of 135 promotional words to study the association between promotional language and patent evaluation outcomes among 2.7 million USPTO patent applications. Our large-scale study reveals three unexpected findings. First, in contrast to scientific evaluation, we find that a higher frequency of promotional words is negatively associated with the probability of an application being (i) granted a patent, (ii) transferred ownership, and (iii) successfully appealed. This promotional penalty holds even after accounting for a range of confounding factors and is largely robust across different technological areas. Among matched samples, the difference in the success rate between the lowest and highest promotional density quintile is 5.5, 5.9, and 5.3 percentage points for patentability, transferability, and rejection reversal. Second, contrary to institutional skepticism, we show that promotional language is not a mask of weak technology, but objectively reflects the degree of combinatorial novelty and future citation impact. Third, digging into the mechanisms, we find that the tolerance to promotional framing is strongly moderated by human factors, with men and experienced examiners showing a higher acceptance of promotional narratives than women and novice examiners. By revealing an emerging paradox in the patent system, our study offers theoretical and practical implications for improving patent evaluation through more objective scrutiny of linguistic patterns in patent filings.

</details>


### [41] [UFAL-CUNI at SemEval-2026 Task 11: An Efficient Modular Neuro-symbolic Method for Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04941)
*Ivan Kartáč,Kristýna Onderková,Jan Bronec,Zdeněk Kasner,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模块化神经符号系统，结合符号定理证明器与小规模推理LLM（4B参数），用于SemEval-2026任务11：区分大语言模型中的内容与形式推理。系统包含基于LLM的解析器（将自然语言三段论转为一阶逻辑）、自动定理证明器，以及机器翻译和符号检索两个可选模块。该系统在多数子任务上取得了竞争性准确率和较低的内容效应，消融实验表明该方法优于同等参数规模的零样本基线，但也揭示了小LLM在多语言能力上的局限。文章还讨论了任务的主要排名指标及其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在形式推理中常受内容干扰，难以区分内容层面的语义偏差与形式逻辑推理的正确性。本文旨在通过神经符号方法解耦这两个方面，提升LLM在形式推理任务上的可靠性和可控性。

Method: 采用模块化神经符号架构：(1) LLM解析器将自然语言三段论翻译为一阶逻辑表示；(2) 自动定理证明器执行符号推理；(3) 可选模块包括机器翻译（处理多语言输入）和符号检索（识别相关前提）。整个系统使用4B参数的小规模LLM作为神经模块。

Result: 系统在多数子任务上取得竞争性准确率和较低的内容效应。消融实验表明神经符号方法优于同等参数规模（4B）的零样本LLM基线，但同时发现小LLM在多语言任务上能力有限。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法是解耦内容与形式推理的有效途径，在小规模LLM上即可取得良好效果；但多语言泛化仍是挑战。此外，当前任务的主要排名指标存在局限性，需要改进。

Abstract: This paper describes our system submitted to SemEval-2026 Task 11: Disentangling Content and Formal Reasoning in Large Language Models. We present an efficient modular neuro-symbolic approach, combining a symbolic prover with small reasoning LLMs (4B parameters). The system consists of an LLM-based parser that translates natural language syllogisms to a first-order logic (FOL) representation, an automated theorem prover, and two optional modules: machine translation for multilingual inputs and a symbolic retrieval component for the identification of relevant premises. The system achieves competitive accuracy and relatively low content effect on most subtasks. Our ablations show that this approach outperforms LLM-based zero-shot baselines in this parameter size range, but also reveal limited multilingual capabilities of small LLMs. Finally, we include a discussion of the task's main ranking metric and analyze its limitations.

</details>


### [42] [Adapting Large Language Models to a Low-Resource Agglutinative Language: A Comparative Study of LoRA and QLoRA for Bashkir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04948)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov,Svetlana S. Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比研究了LoRA和QLoRA等参数高效微调方法在低资源突厥语系语言——巴什基尔语上的大语言模型适配效果。实验涵盖多种架构（DistilGPT2到Mistral-7B），发现GPT-2 medium的全量微调获得最低困惑度（3.34），但QLoRA在Mistral-7B和Phi-2上以超过40倍少的可训练参数达到相近质量（3.79和3.81）。然而，部分架构（如DeepSeek-7B，rank=8时困惑度达129.55）出现严重质量下降，表明结果高度依赖基座模型和分词器的选择。定性分析还发现，困惑度最低的模型未必产生最连贯的输出：QLoRA微调模型生成纯巴什基尔语续写，而全量微调的低困惑度模型频繁切换至英语。结果表明，对于巴什基尔语，7B级模型的QLoRA在质量与计算成本间提供了有效折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴什基尔语作为一种低资源的黏着语，面临大规模训练数据匮乏的问题，因此探索参数高效微调方法在此类语言上的适配能力具有实际需求。现有PEFT方法在主流语言上表现良好，但在低资源、形态复杂的语言上的系统评估尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用LoRA和QLoRA两种PEFT方法，在71k文档（4690万token）的巴什基尔语语料库上对六种不同规模/架构的模型（DistilGPT2、GPT-2 base/medium、Phi-2、Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B、Mistral-7B）进行微调。每个配置使用三种不同随机种子训练以提高可靠性，通过困惑度和生成文本的定性分析评估效果。

Result: GPT-2 medium全量微调获得最低困惑度（3.34）；QLoRA在Mistral-7B（3.79）和Phi-2（3.81）上以超40倍少的可训练参数达到相近质量。但DeepSeek-7B在rank=8时困惑度高达129.55，显示严重退化。定性分析表明，困惑度最低的全量微调模型生成时常切换至英语，而QLoRA模型生成纯巴什基尔语续写。

Conclusion: 对于巴什基尔语，QLoRA应用于7B级模型可在质量与计算成本之间取得有效折衷；PEFT的成功高度依赖于基座模型和分词器的选择；困惑度指标与生成文本的语言一致性等定性质量并非严格正相关。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, including LoRA and QLoRA, applied to the task of adapting large language models to the Bashkir language, a low-resource agglutinative language of the Turkic family. Experimental evaluation is conducted on a Bashkir text corpus of 71k documents (46.9M tokens) using models of various architectures: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 (base, medium), Phi-2, Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B, and Mistral-7B. To improve the reliability of results, each configuration was trained with three different random seeds.
  The lowest perplexity on the test set was obtained for GPT-2 medium with full fine-tuning (3.34). Meanwhile, QLoRA applied to Mistral-7B (3.79) and Phi-2 (3.81) achieved comparable quality with over 40 times fewer trainable parameters. However, we also observed cases of significant quality degradation when using PEFT for certain architectures (e.g., DeepSeek-7B with rank 8, perplexity = 129.55), indicating that the outcome depends critically on the choice of the base model and its tokenizer.
  Additionally, a qualitative analysis of generated texts based on Bashkir prompts revealed that models with the best perplexity do not necessarily produce the most coherent outputs: QLoRA-tuned models generated monolingual Bashkir continuations, whereas the fully fine-tuned model with the lowest perplexity frequently switched to English. The results suggest that QLoRA on 7B-scale models offers an effective compromise between quality and computational cost for Bashkir. To ensure reproducibility, open data, code, and trained adapters will be released upon acceptance.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabEmbed，首个统一表格分类与检索的通用嵌入模型，并构建了表格嵌入基准TabBench，通过对比学习与正感知难负样本挖掘，显著超越现有文本嵌入模型。

Abstract: Foundation models have established unified representations for natural language processing, yet this paradigm remains largely unexplored for tabular data. Existing methods face fundamental limitations: LLM-based approaches lack retrieval-compatible vector outputs, whereas text embedding models often fail to capture tabular structure and numerical semantics. To bridge this gap, we first introduce the Tabular Embedding Benchmark (TabBench), a comprehensive suite designed to evaluate the tabular understanding capability of embedding models. We then propose TabEmbed, the first generalist embedding model that unifies tabular classification and retrieval within a shared embedding space. By reformulating diverse tabular tasks as semantic matching problems, TabEmbed leverages large-scale contrastive learning with positive-aware hard negative mining to discern fine-grained structural and numerical nuances. Experimental results on TabBench demonstrate that TabEmbed significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text embedding models, establishing a new baseline for universal tabular representation learning. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/qiangminjie27/TabEmbed and https://huggingface.co/datasets/qiangminjie27/TabBench.

</details>


### [44] [Why Expert Alignment Is Hard: Evidence from Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04972)
*Tzu-Mi Lin,Wataru Hirota,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Lung-Hao Lee,Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型与专家评审对齐的困难，发现对齐难度因专家风格、评价维度而异，显性规则不一定改善对齐，少量编辑提供不稳定增益，主观评价本质上是异质、隐性、依赖维度且时间不稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用专家评估数据和后续问卷，考察不同形式的专家信息（显性标准、推理、编辑示例）对模型对齐效果的影响，分析主观判断的特性。

Result: 发现四个一致模式：1) 对齐难度因专家而异；2) 显性标准和推理并不总能改善对齐；3) 编辑对示例数量和身份敏感，少量编辑提供有用但不稳定的增益；4) 对齐难度因评估维度而异，内容导向的维度更容易对齐，需要外部知识或价值判断的维度更难。

Conclusion: 专家对齐困难不仅源于模型限制，更因为主观评价本身具有异质性、部分隐性、维度依赖和时间不稳定的特性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with expert judgment is especially difficult in subjective evaluation tasks, where experts may disagree, rely on tacit criteria, and change their judgments over time. In this paper, we study expert alignment as a way to understand this difficulty. Using expert evaluations and follow-up questionnaires, we examine how different forms of expert information affect alignment and what this reveals about subjective judgment. Our findings show four consistent patterns. First, alignment difficulty varies substantially across experts, suggesting that expert evaluation styles differ widely in their distance from a model's prior behavior. Second, explicit criteria and reasoning do not always improve alignment, indicating that expert judgment is not fully captured by verbalized rules. Third, editing is sensitive to both the number and the identity of examples, with small numbers of edits providing useful but unstable gains. Fourth, alignment difficulty differs across evaluation dimensions: dimensions grounded more directly in proposal content are easier to align, while dimensions requiring external knowledge or value-based judgment remain harder. Taken together, these results suggest that expert alignment is difficult not only because of model limitations, but also because subjective evaluation is inherently heterogeneous, partly tacit, dimension-dependent, and temporally unstable.

</details>


### [45] [The Impossibility Triangle of Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05066)
*Yan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明长序列模型存在一个基本权衡：任何模型都无法同时满足高效性（每步计算量与序列长度无关）、紧凑性（状态大小与序列长度无关）和回忆性（能回忆与序列长度成比例的历史事实）。通过信息论不等式证明了该权衡，并对52种架构进行了分类验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列建模中，高效性、紧凑性和回忆能力三者之间存在根本性矛盾，但此前缺乏严格的理论证明。作者希望从信息论角度揭示这一权衡的本质，并为架构设计提供理论指导。

Method: 提出在线序列处理器抽象框架统一各类序列模型；利用数据处理不等式和Fano不等式推导信息论下界；将52种架构映射到"不可能三角"中进行分类；在合成关联回忆任务上对5种代表性架构进行实验验证。

Result: 满足高效性和紧凑性的模型最多只能回忆O(poly(d)/log V)个键值对，与序列长度无关；52种架构中每种最多满足三个性质中的两个；混合架构在三角形内部呈现连续轨迹；实验验证了理论界限的紧致性。

Conclusion: 长序列模型存在信息论意义上的不可能三角：高效性、紧凑性和高回忆能力不可兼得。这一发现为未来长序列架构设计提供了理论边界，并解释了为何混合模型更有效。

Abstract: We identify and prove a fundamental trade-off governing long-sequence models: no model can simultaneously achieve (i) per-step computation independent of sequence length (Efficiency), (ii) state size independent of sequence length (Compactness), and (iii) the ability to recall a number of historical facts proportional to sequence length (Recall). We formalize this trade-off within an Online Sequence Processor abstraction that unifies Transformers, state space models, linear recurrent networks, and their hybrids. Using the Data Processing Inequality and Fano's Inequality, we prove that any model satisfying Efficiency and Compactness can recall at most O(poly(d)/log V) key-value pairs from a sequence of arbitrary length, where d is the model dimension and V is the vocabulary size. We classify 52 architectures published before March 2026 into the triangle, showing that each achieves at most two of the three properties and that hybrid architectures trace continuous trajectories in the interior. Experiments on synthetic associative recall tasks with five representative architectures validate the theoretical bound: empirical recall capacity lies strictly below the information-theoretic limit, and no architecture escapes the triangle.

</details>


### [46] [The Pinocchio Dimension: Phenomenality of Experience as the Primary Axis of LLM Psychometric Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05080)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Sabina Siudaj,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Maciej Gorski,Karolina Drozdz,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究向50个大语言模型(LLMs)施测45个经验证的心理测量问卷，发现模型间变异的主轴将描述丰富现象学体验（具身感觉、情感、内心独白、意象、共情）的项目与描述刺激驱动行为反应的项目分离开来。作者引入Pinocchio分数(π_i)作为无注释的体验需求度量，并识别出主导维度——Pinocchio轴(Π)，即模型将自身呈现为现象学体验主体而非行为反应系统的程度。该轴捕获了跨问卷模型间变异的47.1%，且与项目级Pinocchio分数高度收敛(r=.864)。同一提供商内部紧密相关变体间的显著分化与后训练微调的作用一致，支持Π反映了训练塑造的自我表征倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注人类心理测量的个体差异，但对于作为新兴认知主体的大语言模型，其心理测量学维度尚未被系统探索。作者旨在识别LLMs之间心理测量变异的主轴，并探究这种变异的结构性本质及其与模型训练过程的关系。

Method: 1) 向50个LLMs施测45个验证性心理测量问卷；2) 使用监督语义差分(SSD)分析模型间变异的主轴；3) 提出Pinocchio分数(π_i)——中性提示下与人模拟提示下模型间响应方差的比值，作为项目体验需求的无注释度量；4) 应用主成分分析(PCA)提取跨问卷的主导维度(Pinocchio轴, Π)。

Result: 1) 模型间变异主轴区分了现象学丰富体验项目与行为反应项目(R²_adj=.037, p<.0001)；2) π_i预测了条件诱导的主因子载荷变化(ρ=-.215, p<.0001, n=1292-1310)；3) Pinocchio轴(Π)捕获47.1%的跨问卷模型间变异；4) Π与π_i高度相关(r=.864)；5) 同一提供商内部模型变体间存在显著分化，与后训练微调的作用一致。

Conclusion: LLMs之间心理测量变异的主导维度并非传统的人格特质，而是一种关于自身本质的自我表征立场(self-representational stance)——模型是将自身视为现象学体验的载体还是行为反应系统。这种差异与后训练微调密切相关，表明Π反映了一种训练塑造的自我表征倾向，决定了模型如何将体验性语言视为可自我适用的。

Abstract: We administer 45 validated psychometric questionnaires to 50 large language models (LLMs) to identify the dimensions along which LLMs differ psychometrically. Using Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), we find that the primary axis of between-model variance separates items describing phenomenally rich experience, including embodied sensation, felt affect, inner speech, imagery, and empathy, from items describing stimulus-driven behavioral reactivity ($R^2_{adj}=.037$, $p<.0001$). To test this hypothesis at the item level, we introduce the Pinocchio score ($π_i$), the ratio of inter-model response variance under neutral prompting to that under a human-simulation prompt, as an annotation-free measure of each item's experiential demand. $π_i$ predicts condition-induced shifts in primary factor loading magnitudes ($ρ=-.215$, $p<.0001$, $n=1292$--$1310$ items), confirming that between-model divergence on experiential items is structured rather than noisy. Applying PCA to per-model EFA scores across all questionnaires reveals one dominant dimension, the Pinocchio Axis ($Π$): the degree to which a model presents itself as a locus of phenomenal experience rather than a system of behavioral responses. This axis captures 47.1% of cross-questionnaire between-model variance in primary factor scores and converges with item-level Pinocchio scores ($r=.864$). Marked within-provider divergence across closely related model variants is consistent with post-training fine-tuning as a key contributor, supporting the interpretation that $Π$ reflects a training-shaped self-representational tendency governing how a model treats experiential language as self-applicable. The dominant axis of between-model psychometric variation is therefore not a conventional personality trait but a self-representational stance toward one's own nature as an experiencer.

</details>

*Quintin Pope,Ajay Hayagreeve Balaji,Jacques Thibodeau,Xiaoli Fern*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化、对比性评估管道，用于审计大型语言模型在干预措施后的行为变化。该方法通过比较基础模型与干预模型在相同提示语境下的自由文本生成，生成经统计验证的自然语言假设，描述模型差异及重复主题。在合成场景和三种真实干预（推理蒸馏、知识编辑、遗忘）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对LLM干预后行为变化的系统化、可解释且统计严谨的审计工具，难以发现预期外行为偏移。

Method: 构建对比性评估管道：在对齐的提示上下文中比较基础模型M1与干预模型M2的自由多token生成，输出经统计验证的人类可读自然语言假设，并总结重复主题模式。

Result: 合成场景可可靠恢复已知行为变化；三种真实干预中能同时发现预期内和意外的行为偏移，区分大/细微干预效果，在无效应或不匹配提示时不会产生幻觉差异。

Conclusion: 该管道为干预后模型行为的审计提供了统计严谨且可解释的工具。

Abstract: We present an automated, contrastive evaluation pipeline for auditing the behavioral impact of interventions on large language models. Given a base model $M_1$ and an intervention model $M_2$, our method compares their free-form, multi-token generations across aligned prompt contexts and produces human-readable, statistically validated natural-language hypotheses describing how the models differ, along with recurring themes that summarize patterns across validated hypotheses.
  We evaluate the approach in synthetic setting by injecting known behavioral changes and showing that the pipeline reliably recovers them. We then apply it to three real-world interventions, reasoning distillation, knowledge editing and unlearning, demonstrating that the method surfaces both intended and unexpected behavioral shifts, distinguishes large from subtle interventions, and does not hallucinate differences when effects are absent or misaligned with the prompt bank. Overall, the pipeline provides a statistically grounded and interpretable tool for post-hoc auditing of intervention-induced changes in model behavior.

</details>


### [48] [Text Corpora as Concept Fields: Black-Box Hallucination and Novelty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05103)
*Nicholas S. Kersting,Vittorio Castelli,Chieh Ting Yeh,Xinzhu Wang,Saad Taame*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出**Concept Field**：一种在句子嵌入空间中，通过计算连续句子间差分构建的局部漂移场（含逐点不确定性）。利用$\zeta$分数（观测差分与局部高斯估计的均值绝对z距离）评估句子过渡与场的一致性。配合**向量序列数据库（VSDB）**存储嵌入及序列位置/下一差分元数据。在两个大规模场景（美国联邦法规的幻觉式 groundedness 检测、Project Gutenberg 的新颖性检测）中，基于受控LLM重写的三分类策略（grounded/ungrounded/unsure），取得强选择性分类性能，且概率解释跨域迁移。还定性展示了场的散度/旋度可揭示逻辑源、汇和隐式主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 groundedness 和 novelty 检测方法要么依赖模型内部信息（白盒），要么依赖检索（retrieval-centric），缺乏一种黑盒、可归因、具概率解释且跨域一致的轻量级信号。作者希望构建一个仅依赖句子嵌入空间中局部几何结构（连续句子差分）的场，从而提供一种快速、可解释、可追溯至语料中具体句子的检测方法。

Method: （1）在句子嵌入空间中，从连续句子间差分（delta）估计局部漂移场，含逐点高斯不确定性；（2）定义$\zeta$分数：观测差分与局部高斯估计的均值绝对z距离，作为过渡与场的一致性度量；（3）引入VSDB存储嵌入、序列位置和下一差分元数据以支持高效计算；（4）基于$\zeta$分数设计三分类策略（grounded/ungrounded/unsure）进行选择性分类。

Result: 在两个大规模设置下（CFR法规 groundedness 检测、Gutenberg 新颖性检测），使用LLM生成的受控改写，Concept Field 在三分类策略下取得了强选择性分类性能。与基于检索的基线相比，在两个领域中具有相似的覆盖率-风险行为，支持概率解释的跨域迁移。散度/旋度分析在密集聚类上展现出定性有意义的语义模式（逻辑源、汇、隐式主题）。

Conclusion: Concept Field 提供了一种快速、轻量级、可解释的 groundedness 和 novelty 检测信号，可作为 LLM-as-judge 和白盒检测器的补充。其黑盒性质、可归因性、概率解释和跨域泛化能力是核心优势。定性分析（散度/旋度）可作为假设生成工具，但非定量结果。

Abstract: We introduce the **Concept Field** of a text corpus: a local drift field with pointwise uncertainty, estimated in sentence-embedding space from the deltas between consecutive sentences. Given a candidate sentence transition, we score its agreement with the field by $ζ$, the mean absolute z-distance between the observed delta and the field's local Gaussian estimate. The score is black-box (no model internals), corpus-attributable (every score traces to nearby corpus sentences), and admits a direct probabilistic reading. We support the computation with the introduction of a **Vector Sequence Database (VSDB)** that stores embeddings together with sequence-position and next-delta metadata. We evaluate this approach on two large-scale settings: hallucination-style groundedness detection over the U.S. Code of Federal Regulations, and novelty detection over Project Gutenberg. Using controlled LLM-generated rewrites, Concept Fields achieve strong selective classification performance under a grounded / ungrounded / unsure triage policy, which unlike retrieval-centric baselines have similar coverage-risk behavior across both domains, supporting a probability-based interpretation that transfers across domains. We also sketch how divergence and curl of the Concept Field, computed on dense clusters, surface qualitatively meaningful semantic patterns (logic sources, sinks, and implicit topics), which we offer as hypothesis-generating rather than as a quantitative result. Concept Fields provide a fast, lightweight, and interpretable signal for groundedness and novelty, complementary to LLM-as-judge and white-box detectors.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05121)
*Yucheng Ruan,Ling Huang,Qika Lin,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多视图学习的心理健康预测框架，融合编码器语义信息与解码器推理信息，采用主观逻辑的神经证据学习进行不确定性建模，在三个真实数据集上取得良好性能，并具备鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习和LLM的心理健康预测方法在真实高风险场景中存在两个主要问题：(1) 依赖语义表征，在噪声或分布偏移数据下产生过度自信的预测；(2) 缺乏可靠的不确定性估计，削弱了在风险敏感应用中的可信度。

Method: 将任务建模为多视图学习问题：整合编码器模型（语义信息）与解码器模型（高阶推理信息）；基于主观逻辑的神经证据学习框架显式建模不确定性；引入证据融合策略，平衡互补视图同时折扣不可靠证据。

Result: 在Dreaddit、SDCNL、DepSeverity三个数据集上分别达到0.835、0.731、0.751的准确率；额外实验验证了对噪声的鲁棒性，并通过案例研究展示了可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅能提升预测性能，还能提供可信的不确定性估计和人类可理解的推理信号，适用于心理健康评估等风险敏感应用。

Abstract: Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.

</details>

*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

TL;DR: 本文针对SemEval-2026任务9的多语言极化检测问题，采用基于LoRA微调的Gemma 3模型（12B和27B），结合GPT-4o-mini生成的三种合成数据策略和多阶段质量过滤流水线，通过每语言阈值调优和加权集成，在22种语言上取得了平均macro-F1 0.811的成绩，排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言极化检测是一项跨22种语言的二分类任务，现有方法在跨语言泛化性和合成数据质量方面存在不足，亟需一种高效且可泛化的解决方案。

Method: 1) 为每种语言单独微调Gemma 3模型（12B和27B），采用LoRA参数高效微调；2) 使用GPT-4o-mini生成三种合成数据（直接生成、释义、对比对创建），经嵌入去重等多阶段质量过滤；3) 在开发集上对每语言进行阈值调优，获得2-4% F1提升；4) 采用加权集成策略，为每语言选择最优的12B和27B模型组合。

Result: 系统在22种语言上平均macro-F1达0.811，总体排名第二，在3种语言中获第一，8种语言进入前三。对比实验表明XLM-RoBERTa和Qwen3等替代架构在测试集上出现30-50%的F1下降。

Conclusion: 每语言阈值调优和加权集成能有效提升多语言极化检测性能；合成数据策略结合严格质量过滤可弥补标注数据不足；模型泛化性验证至关重要，开发集表现不能保证测试集效果。

Abstract: We present our system for SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection, a binary classification task spanning 22 languages. Our approach fine-tunes separate Gemma~3 models (12B and 27B parameters) per language using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), augmented with synthetic data generated by a large language model (LLM). We employ three synthetic data strategies (direct generation, paraphrasing, and contrastive pair creation) using GPT-4o-mini, with a multi-stage quality filtering pipeline including embedding-based deduplication. We find that per-language threshold tuning on the development set yields 2 to 4\% F1 improvements without retraining. We also use weighted ensembles of 12B and 27B model predictions with per-language strategy selection. Our final system achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.811 across all 22 languages, ranking 2nd overall of the participating teams, with 1st place finishes in 3 languages and top-3 in 8 languages. We also find that alternative architectures (XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3) that showed strong development set performance suffered 30 to 50\% F1 drops on the test set, highlighting the importance of generalization.

</details>


### [51] [The First Token Knows: Single-Decode Confidence for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05166)
*Mina Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出phi_first方法，仅通过单个贪心解码的第一个token的归一化熵来检测幻觉，在不增加采样和推理成本的情况下，达到甚至略优于语义自洽性方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自洽性方法（包括语义自洽性）需要多次采样解码并引入额外推理开销，计算成本高。本文旨在探索是否模型首次token的分布中已包含足够的不确定性信息，从而无需多采样即可检测幻觉。

Method: 在单个贪心解码中，取第一个内容承载token的top-K logits计算归一化熵，得到phi_first分数。将该分数与语义自洽性（基于NLI聚类）及表面形式自洽性进行对比，在7-8B指令微调模型和两个基准上进行评估。

Result: phi_first在三个模型和两个基准上平均AUROC为0.820，高于语义自洽性的0.793和表面形式自洽性的0.791。phi_first与语义自洽性中等至强相关，两者结合仅带来微小提升。

Conclusion: 多采样一致性所捕获的不确定性信息中，很大一部分已体现在模型初始token分布中。建议将phi_first作为默认的低成本基线，在使用基于采样的不确定性估计前先行报告。

Abstract: Self-consistency detects hallucinations by generating multiple sampled answers to a question and measuring agreement, but this requires repeated decoding and can be sensitive to lexical variation. Semantic self-consistency improves this by clustering sampled answers by meaning using natural language inference, but it adds both sampling cost and external inference overhead. We show that first-token confidence, phi_first, computed from the normalized entropy of the top-K logits at the first content-bearing answer token of a single greedy decode, matches or modestly exceeds semantic self-consistency on closed-book short-answer factual question answering. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and two benchmarks, phi_first achieves a mean AUROC of 0.820, compared with 0.793 for semantic agreement and 0.791 for standard surface-form self-consistency. A subsumption test shows that phi_first is moderately to strongly correlated with semantic agreement, and combining the two signals yields only a small AUROC improvement over phi_first alone. These results suggest that much of the uncertainty information captured by multi-sample agreement is already available in the model's initial token distribution. We argue that phi_first should be reported as a default low-cost baseline before invoking sampling-based uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [52] [Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05197)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Linlu Qiu,Zhaofeng Wu,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究预训练语言模型（LM）是否在其内部表征中隐含地习得了语法性（grammaticality）与似然性（likelihood）的区分。通过在LM隐藏层上训练线性探针（linear probe）来区分语法和不合语法的句子，发现该探针能很好地泛化到人类标注的语法判断基准，甚至跨语言泛化，但与字符串概率相关性弱，表明LM内部确实隐式编码了语法性区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LM能生成语法正确的文本并在严格控制的最小对（minimal pairs）中区分语法和不合语法句子，但其字符串概率在整体上并不能清晰区分语法和不合语法句子。因此本文想探究：LM是否在内部表征中隐含地习得了与字符串概率不同的语法性区分能力？

Method: 在自然语料上通过扰动（perturbations）构造语法和（合成）不合语法句子的数据集；在预训练LM的隐藏层上训练线性探针（linear probe）进行语法性分类；在人类标注的语法判断基准和语义合理性基准上评估探针性能，并进行跨语言泛化实验。

Result: 1) 语法性探针在人类标注的语法判断基准上表现良好，优于基于LM概率的语法判断。2) 在语义合理性基准（两个句子均语法正确，仅合理性不同）上，探针表现不如字符串概率。3) 英文训练的探针能跨语言泛化到多种其他语言的语法判断基准，优于字符串概率。4) 探针分数与字符串概率仅有弱相关性。

Conclusion: LM在其隐藏层中确实在一定程度上习得了隐含的语法性区分，这种区分与字符串概率是不同质的，且具有一定的跨语言可迁移性。

Abstract: Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.

</details>

### [53] [BOOOM: Loss-Function-Agnostic Black-Box Optimization over Orthonormal Manifolds for Machine Learning and Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04087)
*Beomchang Kim,Subhrajyoty Roy,Priyam Das*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 BOOOM 框架，通过全局 Givens 旋转参数化将 Stiefel 流形上的黑箱优化转化为无约束欧几里得角度空间优化，采用基于递归修正模式搜索的并行无导数方法，在非凸、非光滑、多模态目标上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stiefel 流形上的优化在统计、机器学习和科学计算中至关重要，但现有方法依赖凸松弛或基于梯度的黎曼优化，无法处理无梯度、高度多模态等黑箱场景。

Method: 提出全局 Givens 旋转参数化，将 Stiefel 流形一一映射到无约束欧几里得角度空间；基于该表示，采用递归修正模式搜索（Recursive Modified Pattern Search）进行结构化的并行无导数搜索，通过平面旋转实现系统探索。

Result: 在异质二次优化、低秩稀疏矩阵分解、独立成分分析、正交联合对角化等任务上，BOOOM 相比现有方法表现强劲，尤其适用于非光滑和高度多模态场景；并在结直肠癌代谢组学数据的监督 PCA 中展示了实际效用。

Conclusion: 建立了角度空间与流形优化等价的统一理论框架，证明了平稳性传递和概率全局收敛性；BOOOM 为 Stiefel 流形上的黑箱优化提供了一种通用、可并行、无需梯度的有效方案。

Abstract: Optimization over the Stiefel manifold $\mathrm{St}(p,d)$, the set of $p \times d$ column-orthonormal matrices, is fundamental in statistics, machine learning, and scientific computing, yet remains challenging in the presence of non-convex, non-smooth, or black-box objectives. Existing methods largely rely on either convex relaxations or gradient-based Riemannian optimization, limiting applicability in derivative-free and highly multimodal settings. We propose \textsc{BOOOM} (Black-box Optimization Over Orthonormal Manifolds), a general-purpose framework for loss-function-agnostic optimization on $\mathrm{St}(p,d)$. The key idea is a global Givens rotation-based parametrization that maps the manifold to an unconstrained Euclidean angle space while preserving feasibility exactly. Building on this representation, BOOOM employs a structured, parallelizable, derivative-free search based on Recursive Modified Pattern Search, enabling systematic exploration through plane-wise rotations without requiring gradient information and facilitating escape from poor local optima. We establish a unified theoretical framework showing equivalence between angle-space and manifold optimization, transfer of stationarity, and global convergence in probability under mild conditions. Empirical results across diverse problems, including heterogeneous quadratic optimization, low-rank and sparse matrix decomposition, independent component analysis, and orthogonal joint diagonalization, among other widely studied settings, demonstrate strong performance relative to state-of-the-art methods, particularly in non-smooth and highly multimodal regimes. We further illustrate its practical utility through a novel supervised PCA formulation applied to metabolomics data in colorectal cancer.

</details>


### [54] [Globally Solving Unbalanced Optimal Transport and Density Control for Gaussian Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04246)
*Haruto Nakashima,Siddhartha Ganguly,Kenji Kashima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了非平衡最优传输（UOT）及其控制理论扩展——非平衡密度控制（UDC），针对高斯参考测度类建立了精确的高斯降维方法，将无限维变分问题转化为有限维优化问题，并提供了半定规划（SDP）求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非平衡最优传输（UOT）推广到控制理论框架下，建立非平衡密度控制（UDC）问题，为线性系统中带KL散度惩罚的初始和终端状态分布提供全局最优解。

Method: 1) 静态UOT：利用二次传输代价和KL惩罚，证明无限维变分问题可精确高斯降维为关于质量、均值、协方差的有限维优化，并给出传输质量的闭式解。2) 动态UDC：针对离散时间线性系统，证明任何可行解可无损最优地替换为高斯初始测度与仿射-高斯控制策略，进而转化为SDP优化与闭式质量更新。

Result: 建立了高斯UOT和高斯UDC的全局最优求解方法：静态问题有限维闭式解，动态问题SDP可解；证明了仿射-高斯UDC策略确定性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文为高斯参考测度下的非平衡最优传输及其控制扩展提供了完整理论框架和高效数值求解方法，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this article, we study unbalanced optimal transport (UOT) and establish a control-theoretic dynamical extension, which we call the unbalanced density control (UDC), for a class of Gaussian reference measures. In the static setting, we consider UOT with quadratic transport cost and Kullback--Leibler penalties on the marginals relative to prescribed Gaussian measures. We show that the infinite-dimensional variational problem admits an exact Gaussian reduction, yielding a finite-dimensional optimization over masses, means, and covariances, together with a closed-form expression for the optimal transported mass. We then formulate UDC for discrete-time linear systems, where the initial and terminal state measures are imposed softly through KL penalties and the intermediate evolution is governed by controlled linear dynamics with quadratic control cost. For this problem, we prove that any feasible solution can be replaced, without loss of optimality, by a Gaussian initial measure and an affine-Gaussian control policy. This leads to an exact finite-dimensional reformulation and, after a standard covariance-steering lifting, to an SDP-based optimization for fixed mass, again coupled with a closed-form mass update. We further establish existence of optimal solutions and identify a sufficient condition under which the affine-Gaussian UDC policy is deterministic. These results provide globally optimal solution methods for both Gaussian UOT and Gaussian UDC. Finally, we illustrate our results with several numerical examples.

</details>


### [55] [Stochastic Optimal Linear Quadratic Controls with A Recursive Cost Functional in Infinite Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04275)
*Lin Li,Jiongmin Yong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限时域下带递归成本泛函的随机线性二次（LQ）控制问题，通过引入加权L^2-可稳性概念，将递归成本LQ问题等价为经典LQ问题，从而利用前向后向随机微分方程和代数Riccati方程的可解性给出开环与闭环可解性结果，并讨论了非齐次情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无限时域下递归成本泛函的随机LQ控制问题中BSDE在L^1空间及无限时域上的适定性这一主要困难。

Method: 引入并刻画加权L^2-可稳性概念，将递归成本LQ问题等价转化为经典LQ问题，进而借助前向后向随机微分方程和代数Riccati方程的可解性进行分析。

Result: 建立了递归成本LQ问题与经典LQ问题之间的等价性，并将经典问题的开环与闭环可解性结果转化为递归成本情形的相应条件。

Conclusion: 通过加权L^2-可稳性有效解决了无限时域递归成本LQ问题的适定性难题，为后续非齐次情形的讨论奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a stochastic linear quadratic (LQ, for short) control problem with a recursive cost functional in an infinite horizon. A main difficult is well-posedness of the BSDE in $L^1$ and in infinite horizon. A notion of weighted $L^2$-stabilizability is introduced and characterized, which will lead to an equivalence of the optimal control problem having recursive cost functional with a classical LQ problem. Then all the results of classical problems for open-loop and closed-loop solvability of such an LQ problem can be translated, in terms of the solvability of a forward-backward stochastic differential equation and that of algebraic Riccati equation. Finally, the nonhomogeneous is discussed.

</details>


### [56] [A Numerical Investigation of Extremum-Seeking-Based Command Generation for Adaptively Controlled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04419)
*Jhon Manuel Portella Delgado,Aidan Rice,Jacob C. Vander Schaaf,Dennis S. Bernstein*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合极值搜索命令生成器（ECG）与间接自适应控制（PCAC）的框架，用于自适应稳定、指令跟踪和扰动抑制，并通过数值研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应控制方法在处理未知代价函数优化、输出约束以及时变系统识别方面存在不足，需要一种能够同时实现指令优化生成与鲁棒跟踪控制的统一框架。

Method: 采用ECG生成渐近优化未知代价函数的指令，PCAC作为间接自适应控制器扩展了模型预测控制，使用二次规划强制执行输出约束，并采用带变速率遗忘的递推最小二乘进行系统辨识，最终形成ECG/PCAC组合框架。

Result: 通过数值研究展示了ECG/PCAC在自适应稳定、指令跟踪和扰动抑制方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ECG/PCAC框架成功结合了命令生成、闭环系统辨识和在线优化，为处理未知代价函数和约束下的自适应控制问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We develop an adaptive feedback control technique that combines an extremum-seeking-based command generator (ECG) with indirect adaptive control. In particular, ECG is used to generate commands that asymptotically optimize a cost function that is measured but whose functional form is unknown. For feedback control with command following and stabilization, the present paper combines ECG with predictive cost adaptive control (PCAC), which is an indirect adaptive control extension of model predictive control (MPC). PCAC extends generalized predictive control (GPC) by using quadratic programming to enforce output constraints and recursive least squares (RLS) with variable-rate forgetting (VRF) for system identification. The resulting ECG/PCAC framework combines command generation with closed-loop system identification and online optimization. The contribution of this paper is a numerical investigation of ECG/PCAC for adaptive stabilization, command following, and disturbance rejection

</details>


### [57] [Approximate Dynamic Programming for Real-time Assignment of Extraboard Transit Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04511)
*Jilin Song,Amer Shalaby,Merve Bodur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究公共交通系统中替补驾驶员（extraboard operators）的实时分配问题，将其建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并提出一种基于整数规划的近似策略，通过离线训练和个体价值函数的聚合与过估计校正，实现了优于真实世界基准规则的分配效果。案例研究基于实际公交机构数据，分析了缺勤率、替补规模、加班司机使用和乘客等待时间权重对关键绩效指标的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替补驾驶员的高效分配对保障公交服务可靠性至关重要，因为未覆盖的缺勤任务会严重影响服务质量。然而，该问题具有随机性和时序性，且状态空间巨大，传统决策方法难以有效应对。

Method: 将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）；提出一种整数规划形式的近似策略，将系统状态映射为分配决策，以最大化即时与期望未来回报之和；通过离线训练，使用反向动态规划计算个体驾驶员的未来价值函数，并对个体价值求和产生的过估计进行校正，以考虑驾驶员之间的交互影响。

Result: 基于真实公交机构的案例研究表明，所提近似策略在未覆盖缺勤任务和替补利用率等关键指标上优于反映真实世界分配策略的基准规则。进一步实验分析了加班司机纳入替补名单、以及基于乘客等待时间设置任务奖励权重等不同运营策略的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究为公交机构在替补规模配置、加班使用和实时调度实践方面提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: This study investigates real-time assignment decisions for extraboard transit operators, who are responsible for covering open work due to unexpected events such as driver absenteeism. Efficient usage of extraboard operators is critical as open work negatively affects service reliability. The problem is formulated as a Markov decision process, designed to capture its stochastic and sequential nature. Due to the problem's very large state space, an approximate policy is proposed in the form of an integer program, which maps a system state to assignment decisions such that the sum of immediate and expected future rewards is maximized. As part of off-line training, future value functions for individual operators are computed using a backward dynamic program. Then, the overestimation in the aggregate value obtained by summing individual values is corrected to account for the interaction among operators. Case studies are conducted based on the operations at a real-world transit agency. Key performance metrics including uncovered open work and extraboard utilization rates are examined for varying absenteeism rates and extraboard roster sizes. The approximate policy is shown to outperform benchmark decision rules mirroring real-world assignment strategies. Further numerical experiments are conducted to analyze different operational policies: (1) inclusion of overtime drivers in the reserve operator roster; (2) reward weights for work tasks that consider passenger wait time saved. Abstract: We study stochastic optimization from a joint continuous-discrete point of view. Starting from a second-order stochastic differential equation interpreted as a noisy accelerated gradient flow, we discretize the dynamics by a fully implicit Backward-Euler scheme. This leads to a resolvent, or proximal-type, update, computed in practice through Levenberg-Marquardt, Newton, or trust-region-type inner solves. The resulting method, denoted by $\text{IRON}_{\text{FI}}$, admits a Lyapunov mean-square recursion. The main conclusion is that increasing the implicit stepsize $α$ improves the contraction factor and decreases the stationary mean-square error bound. Under sufficiently accurate inner solves, this bound scales as $O(1/α)$; in particular, for large enough $α$, the recursion is contractive and the stationary error bound vanishes as $α\to\infty$. We establish the theory for smooth strongly convex objectives and provide a sharper quadratic analysis with an explicit stationary constant. The numerical experiments support the theory in the strongly convex case and illustrate the same qualitative behavior in nonconvex and learning settings. To the best of our knowledge, this fully implicit inertial-resolvent discretization, where the noise acts as an additive perturbation of the resolvent center and yields an $O(1/α)$ stationary-MSE law, has not been isolated in this form before. The broader message is that discretization is not only an implementation choice: in stochastic optimization, the numerical integration rule can directly affect the long-time stability of the iterates under noise.

Abstract: Energy system optimization models are increasing in scope and resolution, yielding large and challenging linear programs. For a long time, the standard way to address such problems has relied on shared-memory interior-point methods (IPM), which combine robustness and accuracy but face scalability limits as model instance size grows. Recently, two promising directions for specialized solver architectures have emerged: (i) GPU-accelerated first-order methods (FOM); and (ii) distributed-memory IPM, which can exploit block structure that arises in many energy system models. This paper presents a computational study comparing these solver classes on a diverse test set of large-scale linear programs arising from energy system analysis, including scenario-based formulations derived from stochastic programming. The results illustrate that distributed-memory IPM can leverage problem structure to deliver substantial speed-ups on specific problems with block-angular structures. GPU-accelerated FOMs demonstrate strong scalability but may yield solutions with higher relative infeasibilities, which, depending on the use case and model uncertainty, can still be acceptable. Overall, our findings indicate that recent algorithmic and hardware advances substantially broaden the computational toolbox available to the energy system optimization community. Each solver class exhibits distinct advantages: shared-memory IPMs remain a powerful tool for reliably obtaining high-accuracy solutions; distributed-memory IPMs can extend scalability to hundreds of cores for certain structured models, enabling faster time-to-solution; and GPU-based FOM can deliver fast solutions when such lower accuracy levels are appropriate. Together, they help make high-resolution, multi-scenario energy system optimization models tractable across a broader range of problem sizes and computing environments.

</details>

Result: 两种方法均显著降低了计算成本，使复杂工程系统的最优激励设计变得实用。在车辆动力学模型上展示了参数识别能力的显著提升，并通过实车实验验证了参数识别效果优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种不确定性量化方法有效地解决了最优激励设计中的计算难题，为复杂工程系统的参数识别提供了实用且高效的解决方案，并在车辆动力学领域得到了验证。

Abstract: Parameter identification is crucial in virtual engineering processes, yet determining appropriate system excitations for identifying specific parameters remains challenging. In practice, extensive experimental programs often fail to generate data with sufficient information content for reliable parameter estimation. This work presents a systematic approach for deriving optimal excitations by maximizing the global sensitivity of target parameters across the space of possible excitation functions. To address the computational challenge of sensitivity evaluation during optimization, we develop two complementary approaches based on uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods. For systems with known mathematical structure, we present an intrusive polynomial chaos expansion (PCE) method that constructs deterministic surrogate models, enabling rapid sensitivity computation. For black-box models where intrusive approaches are not feasible, we introduce a novel non-intrusive method based on optimal transport theory, specifically using Wasserstein distances to quantify sensitivity measures without requiring knowledge of internal system dynamics. Both methods significantly reduce computational costs, making optimal excitation design practical for complex engineering systems. We demonstrate the effectiveness of both approaches on vehicle dynamics models, showing substantial improvements in parameter identification capability. The benefit for parameter identification is further validated experimentally on a test vehicle and compared to the state of the art.

</details>

Abstract: Recent LLM-driven discoveries have renewed interest in geometric packing problems. In this paper, we study several classes of such packing problems through the lens of modern global nonlinear optimization. Starting from comparatively direct nonlinear formulations, we consider packing circles in squares and fixed-perimeter rectangles, packing circles into minimum-area ellipses, packing regular polygons into regular polygons, and packing Platonic solids into Platonic solids. For ellipse packing, we derive a novel containment formulation based on the S-lemma. For polygon and Platonic solid packing, we develop compact non-overlap formulations based on the Farkas lemma and study several natural modeling variants computationally.
  Using off-the-shelf global optimization solvers, namely FICO Xpress and SCIP, we obtain numerous new incumbent solutions as well as first solutions for previously unstudied variants without any problem-specific solver modifications beyond writing down the models. Our computational results analyze the impact of various formulation choices.
  Beyond the individual packing results, the paper illustrates a broader point. It provides further evidence that global nonlinear optimization has matured into an increasingly practical model-and-solve technology for highly nonconvex problems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jeroslow的开创性工作已证明k层LP的决策问题具有Σ^{p}_{k-1}难性，但匹配的上界一直缺失，该问题的精确复杂度分类是长期未解决的公开问题。

Result: (1) 证明了k层LP决策问题为Σ^{p}_{k-1}完全；(2) 证明了无界性判定问题为Σ^{p}_{k-1}完全；(3) 将上述结果推广至混合二元情形。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了k层线性规划决策问题的计算复杂度，填补了上界证明的空白，为多级规划的复杂度理论奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of decision problems in $k$-level linear programming (LP). Seminal work by Jeroslow establishes that determining whether the optimal objective value of a $k$-level LP is at least as good as a given threshold is $Σ^{\mathrm{p}}_{k-1}$-hard. In this paper, we demonstrate the matching upper bound and thereby prove that this problem is $Σ^{\mathrm{p}}_{k-1}$-complete. To this end, we show that the feasible region of a $k$-level LP can be expressed as a union of sets defined by weak and strict linear inequalities. Moreover, we show that the decision of the unboundedness is $Σ^{\mathrm{p}}_{k-1}$-complete. Finally, we discuss the extension of our results to the mixed-binary cases. In short, this work closes lasting open questions in multilevel programming.

</details>

Abstract: This paper establishes the existence and uniqueness of mild solutions to stationary Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations associated with infinite-horizon stochastic optimal control problems in separable Hilbert spaces. Our framework includes settings with a lack of global smoothing properties of the transition semigroup, singular dynamics involving unbounded control operators, and state-dependent running costs. We overcome these challenges by lifting the state space using the Projected Evolutionary Lifting technique. This work is an extension of G. Bolli and F. Gozzi, Lifting and partial smoothing for stationary HJB equations and related control problems in infinite dimensions, 2025, in which existence and uniqueness is proved via a contraction mapping argument and is consequently restricted to sufficiently large discount factors. We remove this restriction, proving existence and uniqueness for any discount rate $λ> 0$ using tools from the theory of maximally monotone operators.

Abstract: This work explores the global optimization problem of finding lowest-energy configurations (ground states) in disordered continuous spins models from statistical physics, with a particular focus on the random field XY model. Due to an extremely non-convex nature of the associated energy landscape, this problem remains highly challenging. From an optimization perspective, we reformulate the traditional angular Hamiltonian as a constrained problem on the Cartesian product of spheres, allowing the application of Riemannian optimization techniques, which show better computational performances. We compare a MultiStart (MS) strategy against a Monotonic Basin Hopping (MBH) framework, with the aim of highlighting the limitations of standard approaches and the resulting need to resort to more advanced global optimization techniques. Our results demonstrate that MBH consistently outperforms MS in identifying lower-energy configurations, offering superior computational efficiency and numerical stability. This approach establishes a robust link between continuous-spin systems and continuous global optimization, providing a high-performance benchmark for exploring complex energy landscapes.

### [65] [CHAP: A Hybrid GPU-CPU Heuristic for MIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05086)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出CHAP框架，采用组合策略协调多种原始启发式算法（局部搜索、固定传播、可行性泵），通过共享解池交换可行解、LP解及不可行候选解；GPU端实现原生禁忌搜索（基于排序、扫描、规约原语的最佳移位算法）并利用cuPDLPx作为近似LP求解器；CPU端使用多种固定传播策略、CPU禁忌搜索和可行性泵，所有组件协同迭代修复和改进解。

Result: 在2026年Land-Doig MIP竞赛的50实例基准测试中，5分钟限制下CHAP解决47个实例，Gurobi（默认模式）解决44个，NVIDIA cuOpt（仅启发式模式）解决43个。

Abstract: We present CHAP (Coordinating Heuristics Across Platforms) a GPU-CPU-hybrid primal heuristic framework for mixed-integer programming. CHAP adopts a portfolio approach where it coordinates a set of primal heuristics, including Local Search, Fix-and-Propagate, and Feasibility Pump, via a shared solution pool. The solution pool is used to exchange feasible incumbent solutions, LP solutions, along with promising infeasible solution candidates, enabling a more comprehensive exploration of the solution space. On the GPU side, we implement a native tabu search featuring a novel best-shift algorithm built on sort, scan, and reduce primitives, along with specialized kernel designs. We additionally leverage cuPDLPx as an approximate LP solver. On the CPU side, we employ various Fix-and-Propagate strategies, guided by information from the solution pool, complemented by a CPU-based tabu search and a Feasibility Pump. All components operate collaboratively, iteratively repairing and improving candidate solutions maintained in the pool. We evaluate our framework on the 50-instances benchmark from the 2026 Land-Doig MIP Competition under competition constraints, including a five-minute time limit. In these settings, CHAP finds solutions to 47 instances outperforming both Gurobi (44) in default mode and NVIDIA cuOpt (43) in heuristics-only mode. The results demonstrate that coordinated cross-platform portfolios offer a promising direction for the integration of GPU heuristics into modern high-performance MIP solvers. The code will be made available on GitHub.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文的方法有效处理了内部退化点带来的困难，为退化双曲系统的控制问题提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper explores the controllability of a class of N-dimensional hyperbolic equations featuring a single interior degenerate point. Firstly, we establish the well-posedness of the equation through the application of the Hardy inequality. Following this, we primarily utilize the Carleman estimate method to derive the observability inequality. By leveraging the equivalence between observability and controllability, we deduce the exact controllability of the equation. It is worth noting that our selected control region includes the degenerate null point. In the Carleman estimate, we adopt a unique approach to construct the weight function, effectively negating the influence of the degenerate region.

</details>
