*Kris De Asis,Mohamed Elsayed,Jiamin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分时间差分（TD）方法在回合制问题中的局限性，提出了广义差分TD算法，通过证明其保持策略排序不变性，将差分TD扩展到回合制问题，并验证了奖励中心化能提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的差分TD方法依赖奖励中心化（reward centering），这在无限时域问题中有效，但在回合制问题中会改变最优策略，限制了其适用性。作者受流式深度强化学习中归一化作用的启发，研究奖励中心化在回合制问题中的应用。

Method: 提出广义差分TD算法，证明该算法在存在终止条件时保持策略排序不变，从而将差分TD扩展到回合制问题；证明其与线性TD的一种形式等价，继承了后者的理论保证；并将多种流式强化学习算法扩展为差分版本。

Result: 理论证明广义差分TD保持了策略排序；实证表明在多种基算法和环境上，奖励中心化能提升回合制问题的样本效率。

Conclusion: 提出的广义差分TD方法成功将差分学习框架扩展到回合制问题，且奖励中心化作为一种简单有效的归一化手段，可广泛应用于各类流式强化学习算法以提高样本效率。

Abstract: Differential temporal difference (TD) methods are value-based reinforcement learning algorithms that have been proposed for infinite-horizon problems. They rely on reward centering, where each reward is centered by the average reward. This keeps the return bounded and removes a value function's state-independent offset. However, reward centering can alter the optimal policy in episodic problems, limiting its applicability. Motivated by recent works that emphasize the role of normalization in streaming deep reinforcement learning, we study reward centering in episodic problems and propose a generalization of differential TD. We prove that this generalization maintains the ordering of policies in the presence of termination, and thus extends differential TD to episodic problems. We show equivalence with a form of linear TD, thereby inheriting theoretical guarantees that have been shown for those algorithms. We then extend several streaming reinforcement learning algorithms to their differential counterparts. Across a range of base algorithms and environments, we empirically validate that reward centering can improve sample efficiency in episodic problems.

</details>


### [140] [Probing Structural Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Algebraic Trapdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04352)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于SL(3, Z)中子群构造问题的数学推理基准套件，用于评估语言模型的结构性数学推理能力。该基准利用密码学风格的验证者-证明者不对称性，要求模型通过阿什巴赫分类分析或SL(3, Z)中可判定性未知的成员查询来推导群论不变量。实验表明，一个模型在索引变体上花费152分钟推理，识别出核侧成员查询为瓶颈，尝试构造性验证后以"DON'T KNOW"弃权，展示了在开放可判定性边界上的校准元认知。该基准暴露了标准答案评分无法区分的四类模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法有效区分语言模型是依赖内化的代数先验知识还是通用计算能力进行数学推理。作者希望设计一个具有验证者-证明者不对称性的基准，通过子群构造问题中O(1)封闭形式答案与求解者必须进行复杂代数分析之间的差距，来探测模型是否真正理解代数结构、是否具备元认知能力（即在面对可判定性未知的问题时能否正确弃权）。

Method: 构建基于SL(3, Z)子群构造问题的基准套件，每个实例以整数矩阵列表形式给出有限生成子群，要求计算三个算术不变量之一（指数、素数处满射或成员资格）。构造时信息(N, K)能在O(1)封闭形式下确定答案，但求解者缺乏该信息，必须通过阿什巴赫分类分析或SL(3, Z)中可判定性未知的成员查询来推导。基准包含三个变体，分别测试不同层次的代数推理能力。

Result: 对五个代表性推理轨迹进行实证分析。一个模型在索引变体上花费152分钟推理，明确识别出核侧成员查询是瓶颈，尝试构造性验证后，最终以"DON'T KNOW"弃权而非提交计算出的余核候选。这表明该模型展示了校准的元认知能力，能够在开放可判定性边界上做出正确判断。

Conclusion: 该基准能暴露标准答案评分无法区分的四类模型行为：正确提交、错误提交、正确弃权、错误弃权。通过迫使模型在可判定性未知的数学问题中做出决策，可以有效评估模型的元认知能力和真正的代数推理深度，而不仅仅是生成表面正确的答案。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark suite for evaluating structural mathematical reasoning in language models, built on subgroup-construction problems in SL(3, Z) with cryptographic-style verifier-prover asymmetry. Each instance presents a finitely generated subgroup as a list of integer matrices and asks for an arithmetic invariant -- index, surjection-at-prime, or membership -- that the construction-time information (N, K) pins down in O(1) closed form, but that the solver, lacking that information, must derive by either Aschbacher-classification analysis or by a membership query in SL(3, Z) of unknown decidability. The benchmark therefore distinguishes models with internalized algebraic priors (Aschbacher classes, McLaughlin's theorem, Property (T), the congruence subgroup property) from models that rely on general-purpose computation. We report empirical results across five representative reasoning traces from two state-of-the-art models. The headline result: on the index variant, one model spent 152 minutes of reasoning, explicitly identified the kernel-side membership question as the bottleneck, attempted constructive verification, and abstained with "DON'T KNOW" rather than commit to its computed cokernel candidate -- demonstrating calibrated meta-cognition on the open-decidability boundary that the benchmark was designed to probe. We argue that the benchmark exposes a four-way classification of model behavior (commit-correct, commit-wrong, abstain-correct, abstain-wrong) that standard answer-key scoring conflates.

</details>


### [141] [Critical Windows of Complexity Control: When Transformers Decide to Reason or Memorize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04396)
*Sarwan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer的组成泛化（记忆vs推理）在训练过程中存在一个尖锐的关键窗口期，该窗口内施加权重衰减对最终推理能力起决定性作用，且窗口位置取决于初始化尺度，但在小初始化下推理吸引域反而缩小，挑战了"越小初始化越好"的主流观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将复杂度控制视为静态超参数，忽略了训练过程中何时施加这种控制才是关键的问题。本文旨在揭示Transformer的训练动力学中，记忆化与推理能力之间决定性转变发生的时间窗口。

Method: 在受控的组合泛化任务上，系统性地在不同训练阶段施加权重衰减，通过对比OOD准确率来定位关键窗口。实验包括：单窗口权重衰减（仅25%训练步数）、总正则化预算固定但放置位置不同、改变初始化尺度等。

Result: (i) 在25%训练窗口内施加权重衰减可达到与全程衰减相当的OOD准确率(0.93 vs 0.91)；(ii) 将正则化预算放在训练中期比放在早期OOD准确率高5-9倍；(iii) 关键窗口边界极其尖锐，仅移动100步就会使OOD从随机(0.15)跃升至推理区间(0.61)；(iv) 窗口位置依赖于初始化尺度，但小初始化下推理吸引域反而缩小。此外，该现象具有任务特异性，在模算术的grokking任务中不出现。

Conclusion: Transformer的推理能力并非由整体超参数决定，而是由训练中一个特定的关键窗口期内的复杂度控制决定。这一发现挑战了"更小初始化始终更好"的普遍建议，并揭示了复杂度控制的时间依赖性及其任务特异性。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that Transformers' compositional generalization is governed by \emph{complexity control}, initialization scale and weight decay, which steers training toward low-complexity reasoning solutions rather than high-complexity memorization. Existing analyses, however, treat complexity control as a single static hyperparameter choice, leaving open \emph{when} during training this control is actually decisive. We show that the memorization-versus-reasoning fate of a Transformer is determined within a sharp, identifiable window of training. On a controlled compositional task we find that (i)~weight decay applied for a single 25\%-of-training window matches full-training weight decay in out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy ($0.93$ vs $0.91$); (ii)~holding total regularization budget constant, placing it in the middle of training yields $5{-}9\times$ higher OOD accuracy than placing it early; (iii)~the boundary of the critical window is remarkably sharp, window onset shifted by as little as $100$ optimization steps causes mean OOD to jump from chance ($0.15$) to reasoning-regime ($0.61$); (iv)~the window's position depends systematically on initialization scale, but the basin of attraction for reasoning solutions \emph{shrinks} at small initialization, contradicting the prevailing recommendation that smaller initialization is uniformly better. We further show that the critical-window phenomenon is task-specific: it does not appear on grokking with modular arithmetic, where properly tuned constant weight decay matches scheduled weight decay.

</details>


### [142] [FAAST: Forward-Only Associative Learning via Closed-Form Fast Weights for Test-Time Supervised Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04651)
*Guangsheng Bao,Hongbo Zhang,Han Cui,Yanbin Zhao,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAAST提出了一种前向传播的联想适应方法，通过单次前向传递将标注样本解析为快速权重，实现了恒定时间推理，在减少90%以上适应时间和高达95%内存使用的同时，性能与反向传播方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练模型适应方法存在折中：反向传播训练成本高，而基于内存或上下文的方法推理开销大。需要一种既高效又低资源消耗的适应方法。

Method: 提出FAAST（Forward-only Associative Adaptation），一种仅前向传播的联想适应方法，通过解析方式在单次前向传递中将标注样本编译为快速权重，消除了内存或上下文依赖。

Result: 在图像分类和语言建模基准上，FAAST达到或超过基于反向传播的适应方法，适应时间减少90%以上；与基于内存/上下文的适应方法相比，内存使用节省高达95%。

Conclusion: FAAST是一种高效、可扩展的监督任务适应解决方案，尤其适用于资源受限的模型，实现了任务适应与预训练表征的解耦。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models typically involves a trade-off between the high training costs of backpropagation and the heavy inference overhead of memory-based or in-context learning. We propose FAAST, a forward-only associative adaptation method that analytically compiles labeled examples into fast weights in a single pass. By eliminating memory or context dependence, FAAST achieves constant-time inference and decouples task adaptation from pretrained representation. Across image classification and language modeling benchmarks, FAAST matches or exceeds backprop-based adaptation while reducing adaptation time by over 90\% and is competitive to memory/context-based adaptation while saving memory usage by up to 95\%. These results demonstrate FAAST as a highly efficient, scalable solution for supervised task adaptation, particularly for resource-constrained models. We release the code and models at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/faast.

</details>


### [143] [$p$-adic Manifold Learning and Benchmark Tasks from Impartial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04374)
*Tomoki Mihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了p进流形学习，提出了一个解决该问题的算法，并基于无偏博弈设计了基准测试任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流形学习方法主要基于实数和欧几里得空间，但许多数据和结构（如无偏博弈）具有p进数特征，需要新的学习范式来捕捉其层次结构。

Method: 提出了p进流形学习的定义和算法框架，并利用无偏博弈构建了具有p进结构的基准测试任务。

Result: 成功提出了p进流形学习算法并在无偏博弈基准任务上进行了验证。

Conclusion: p进流形学习为处理具有p进数结构的数据提供了新途径，无偏博弈基准任务可用于评估该类方法。

Abstract: We introduce $p$-adic manifold learning, propose an algorithm to solve it, and propose benchmark tasks from impartial games.

</details>


### [144] [Uncertainty-Aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04874)
*Huatian Zhang,Zhendong Mao,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UE-DPO方法，通过token级认知不确定性引导模型主动探索并纠正视觉缺陷，在偏好学习中更合理地分配训练压力，缓解MLLM中的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖模型自评估的视觉敏感性信号来分配训练权重，但该信号存在自我引用偏差：强化已学好的视觉线索而忽略难以感知的关键细节，限制了深度对齐。

Method: 1) 量化模型因无法将token预测锚定到图像而产生的不确定性；2) 基于不确定性感知的探索强度，对偏好样本中视觉缺陷token施加更多学习压力，同时减轻对非偏好样本中有益知识的过度惩罚。

Result: 大量实验证明了UE-DPO方法在缓解MLLM幻觉方面的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过认知不确定性引导的探索性偏好优化，能使模型主动发现并纠正自身视觉感知缺陷，实现更深入的视觉-语言对齐。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has proven to be an effective solution for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by learning from preference pairs. One of its key challenges lies in how to transfer the sequence-level preference into fine-grained supervision on visual fidelity. To safeguard vision-related tokens that are prone to hallucination, existing methods typically allocate training emphasis according to the model's self-assessed visual sensitivity signals. However, such sensitivity, estimated by a model still under training, introduces self-referential bias: reinforcing already well-learned visual cues while neglecting hard-to-perceive but critical details, thereby limiting deeper alignment. In this work, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization (UE-DPO) method for MLLMs, which enables the model to uncover its cognitive deficiencies and actively explore for self-correction, guided by token-level epistemic uncertainty. Specifically, we first quantify the uncertainty from the model's failure to ground token predictions in the given image. Then, based on an uncertainty-aware exploration intensity, we encourage more learning pressure on visually deficient tokens in preferred samples, and alleviate the over-penalization of beneficial knowledge in dispreferred samples. Further, we provide a theoretical justification for our method, and extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [145] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明注意力机制的谱诊断方法存在结构性方向盲区，并提出基于传输容量(φ)和方向(G)的双轴诊断框架，在8B参数模型上实现0.62-0.84的LC-AUROC，验证了极性反转预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的幻觉以可预测的方式出现：注意力路由失败表现为过度集中于少数位置或过度扩散导致相关性稀释，且失败模式携带诊断信号。现有谱方法存在结构性局限。

Method: 1) 证明任何转置不变的谱诊断对注意力算子的方向信息不可区分；2) 建立不对称系数G作为方向唯一控制参数；3) 推导因果架构的闭式二分Cheeger界；4) 提出基于φ(容量)和G(方向)的双轴诊断框架。

Result: 统一因果注意力满足n无关下界φ≥1/5，最差切割在t*/n≈0.32；窗口注意力突破下界达O(w/n)；双轴框架在8B参数模型上LC-AUROC达0.62-0.84，HaluEval与MedHallu间极性反转。

Conclusion: 注意力故障模式不仅数值不同，形状也不同；提出的双轴诊断是可证伪的，传输特征在长度受控评估下保持可解释信号。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways: attention routing fails by over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or by spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport capacity; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally orientation-blind (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a quantitative converse establishing the asymmetry coefficient $G$ as the unique control parameter for direction.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$ with worst cut at $t^\ast/n \approx 0.32$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (LC-AUROC from 0.62 to 0.84) on tested models up to 8B parameters, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [146] [Reinforcement Learning for Compositional Generalization with Outcome-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04920)
*Xiyan Fu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用结果级强化学习（GRPO算法）替代传统的监督微调来提升组合泛化能力，在多个组合基准上验证了其有效性，并发现强化学习通过重塑输出分布、特别是改善复杂组合类型的泛化来避免对频繁组合的过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督微调采用token级训练范式，仅鼓励模型模仿目标输出，无法捕捉全局组合结构，导致模型难以泛化到未见过的组合。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)进行结果级强化学习，探索二元结果奖励和复合奖励（额外提供组合反馈）两种方案。

Result: 在多个组合泛化基准上，强化学习相比监督微调显著提升了组合泛化能力；监督模型倾向于过拟合频繁出现的训练组合，而强化学习通过重塑输出分布改善了泛化，尤其对复杂组合类型效果更明显。

Conclusion: 结果级强化学习是提升组合泛化的有效替代方案，能够克服监督微调在token级别训练上的局限性。

Abstract: Compositional generalization refers to correctly interpret novel combinations of known primitives, which remains a major challenge. Existing approaches often rely on supervised fine-tuning, which encourages models to imitate target outputs. This token-level training paradigm fails to capture the global compositional structure required for generalizing to unseen combinations. In this work, we investigate whether compositional generalization can instead be improved through outcome-level reinforcement learning. We adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization to optimize models based on feedback on their final outputs. Within this framework, we explore both a simple binary outcome reward and a composite reward that provides additional composition feedback. Experiments on multiple compositional benchmarks show that reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization compared to supervised fine-tuning. Further analysis reveals that supervised models tend to overfit frequent training compositions, whereas reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization by reshaping the output distribution, particularly for more complex composition types.

</details>


### [147] [Counterfactual identifiability beyond global monotonicity: non-monotone triangular structural causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04413)
*Pengcheng Tan,Jiang Chen,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非单调三角结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），用机制可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替代全局单调性，实现完全反事实可识别性，并通过CausalInverter架构在非单调机制中取得系统性反事实增益，在MuJoCo Door任务上实现完美事件级反事实恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局单调性假设在具身交互中常被违反（相同外生扰动在不同接触背景下引发相反响应），亟需探索在放弃该假设后仍能保证反事实可识别性的结构条件。

Method: 提出NM-TM-SCM模型，核心条件为机制可逆性（mechanism-wise invertibility）与上下文无关逆传输（context-independent inverse transport），证明其等价于外生同构（exogenous isomorphism）并保证完全反事实可识别性；实例化为CausalInverter架构，包含三角可逆层、方向门控和传输稳定性正则化。

Result: 在合成非单调机制上随非单调性增强获得系统性反事实增益；MuJoCo Door实现完美事件级反事实恢复，连续角度误差低于Transformer基线；MuJoCo Push中非单调性较弱时仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 识别出介于全局单调三角模型与无约束黑箱世界模型之间的更广泛可识别性区间，为具身交互中的反事实推理提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: Structural causal models provide a unified semantics for interventions and counterfactuals, but most identifiability results rely on restrictive assumptions like global monotonicity, which are often violated in embodied interaction, where the same exogenous perturbation can induce opposite responses under different contact contexts. We ask what structure still suffices once global monotonicity is dropped. We introduce non-monotone triangular structural causal models (NM-TM-SCM), which retain triangular recursion but replace global monotonicity with mechanism-wise invertibility and context-independent inverse transport. We prove that these conditions are equivalent to exogenous isomorphism and imply complete counterfactual identifiability, and we give a counterexample showing that local invertibility alone is insufficient. We instantiate the theory in CausalInverter, with triangular invertible layers, orientation gates, and transport-stability regularization. On synthetic non-monotonic mechanisms, the structural bias yields systematic counterfactual gains as non-monotonicity increases. On MuJoCo Door, our model achieves perfect event-level counterfactual recovery, lowers continuous angle error relative to a Transformer baseline, and delivers substantially more stable recovery than Transformer and conditional-flow predictors. On MuJoCo Push, where non-monotonicity is weaker, the same low-data predictors remain competitive or better, consistent with a bias-variance boundary. These results identify a broader identifiable regime between globally monotone triangular models and unconstrained black-box world models.

</details>


### [148] [Conceptors for Semantic Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04980)
*Ilias Triantafyllopoulos,Young-Min Cho,Ren Tao,Miranda Muqing Miao,Sunny Rai,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Neville Ryant,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用概念器（conceptors）作为软投影矩阵，从双极概念的两极激活中估计，保留概念的多维子空间。与单一方向基线相比，双极子空间严格更优。概念器配额提供无参数层选择诊断，在三个指令微调模型和三个语义维度上预测概念可分离性，Pearson相关系数高达r=0.96。概念器支持闭式布尔代数（AND, OR, NOT），在系统评估中匹配或优于加性基线，且产生更少的退化输出，是几何上更合理、可组合且更安全的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于激活的LLM行为控制在推理时有效，但主流范式将每个概念简化为单一方向，其几何结构未得到充分研究。作者旨在超越单一方向范式，探索概念的多维子空间结构，并提供几何上更合理、可组合且更安全的控制方法。

Method: 使用概念器（conceptors）作为软投影矩阵，从双极概念的两极激活池中估计，保留概念的多维子空间。通过几何分析证明双极子空间严格优于单向量基线。概念器配额（conceptor quota）作为无参数层选择诊断工具。概念器支持闭式布尔代数（AND, OR, NOT）用于组合操作。在五个设计轴上进行系统评估。

Result: 双极子空间严格优于单向量基线。概念器配额预测概念可分离性的Pearson相关系数高达r=0.96。概念器在概念子空间为多维度的层上匹配或优于加性基线，同时产生更少的退化输出。

Conclusion: 概念器引导是几何上更合理、可组合且更安全的替代方案，优于从有限对比对中提取的单一方向引导方法。

Abstract: Activation-based steering provides control of LLM behavior at inference time, but the dominant paradigm reduces each concept to a single direction whose geometry is left largely unexamined. Rather than selecting a single steering direction, we use conceptors: soft projection matrices estimated from activations pooled across both poles of a bipolar concept, which preserve the concept's full multidimensional subspace. A geometric analysis shows the bipolar subspace strictly subsumes the single-vector baseline. We further show that the conceptor quota provides a parameter-free layer-selection diagnostic, predicting concept separability with Pearson correlations up to r=0.96 across three instruction-tuned models and three semantic dimensions. Beyond selection, conceptors admit a closed-form Boolean algebra (AND, OR, NOT): we evaluate conceptor compositionality on thematically related sub-concepts. Across a systematic five-axis design-space evaluation, conceptors match or outperform additive baselines at layers where concept subspaces are multi-dimensional while producing substantially fewer degenerate outputs. Conceptor steering is a geometrically principled, compositional, and practically safer alternative to single-direction steering from a limited number of contrastive pairs.

</details>


### [149] [Dream-MPC: Gradient-Based Model Predictive Control with Latent Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04568)
*Jonathan Spieler,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dream-MPC 是一种结合模型预测控制与策略先验的混合方法，通过梯度上升优化少量候选轨迹，并引入不确定性正则化和跨时间步的优化迭代复用，在24个连续控制任务上显著提升策略性能，超越梯度无关MPC和现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的强化学习方法中，混合MPC与策略先验的方法虽具优势，但依赖梯度无关优化，在高维控制任务中计算成本高；而梯度优化方法在实际中表现往往更差，需探索更有效的梯度优化方案。

Method: Dream-MPC 从策略展开生成少量候选轨迹，利用学习的世界模型对每条轨迹进行梯度上升优化，引入不确定性正则化，并通过跨时间步复用先前优化的动作来摊销优化迭代。

Result: 在24个连续控制任务上，Dream-MPC 显著提升了底层策略的性能，并超越了梯度无关MPC及当前最优基线方法。

Conclusion: Dream-MPC 通过梯度优化的方式有效提升了混合MPC方法的性能，为高维连续控制任务提供了一种计算高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches either use gradient-free, population-based methods for planning, learned policy networks, or a combination of policy networks and planning. Hybrid approaches that combine Model Predictive Control (MPC) with a learned model and a policy prior to leverage the advantages of both paradigms have shown promising results. However, these approaches typically rely on gradient-free optimization methods, which can be computationally expensive for high-dimensional control tasks. While gradient-based methods are a promising alternative, recent works have empirically shown that gradient-based methods often perform worse than their gradient-free counterparts. We propose Dream-MPC, a novel approach that generates few candidate trajectories from a rolled-out policy and optimizes each trajectory by gradient ascent using a learned world model, uncertainty regularization and amortization of optimization iterations over time by reusing previously optimized actions. Our results on 24 continuous control tasks show that Dream-MPC can significantly improve the performance of the underlying policy and can outperform gradient-free MPC and state-of-the-art baselines. We will open source our code and more at https://dream-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [150] [Self-Induced Outcome Potential: Turn-Level Credit Assignment for Agents without Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04984)
*Senkang Hu,Yong Dai,Xudong Han,Zhengru Fang,Yuzhi Zhao,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIOP提出了一种无标签的回合级信用分配方法，通过对最终答案进行语义聚类作为潜在未来状态，利用势能函数为LLM agent的中间信息收集步骤提供奖励信号，无需人工标注过程奖励或任务特定的验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM agent依赖中间信息收集步骤，但训练反馈通常仅在最终答案处获得，因为过程级奖励需要高质量的人工标注。现有回合级塑形方法需要答案监督或稳定的任务特定验证器，而无标签RL方法主要在答案或轨迹层面提取自信号，无法将信用分配到中间回合。

Method: SIOP对每个查询采样多个轨迹，将最终答案聚类为语义结果模式，构建一个可靠性感知的目标分布，然后利用可计算的聚类级近似奖励那些增加对未来可靠状态后验支持的中间回合。该方法将信息势能塑形从黄金答案监督泛化到无需任务特定验证器的设置，同时避免了标准GRPO使用的广播式轨迹级优势。

Result: 在七个搜索增强的agentic推理基准上，SIOP在平均性能上优于无验证器的结果级基线，并接近黄金监督的结果基线。

Conclusion: SIOP形式化了无标签回合级信用分配的框架，刻画了其监督黄金答案极限，证明了通过语义聚类实现势能塑形的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents depend on intermediate information-gathering turns, yet training feedback is usually observed only at the final answer, because process-level rewards require high-quality human annotation. Existing turn-level shaping methods reward turns that increase the likelihood of a gold answer, but they require answer supervision or stable task-specific verifiers. Conversely, label-free RL methods extract self-signals from output distributions, but mainly at the answer or trajectory level and therefore cannot assign credit to intermediate turns. We propose Self-Induced Outcome Potential (SIOP), which treats semantic clusters of final answers as latent future outcome states for potential-based turn-level credit assignment. For each query, SIOP samples multiple rollouts, clusters final answers into semantic outcome modes, and builds a reliability-aware target distribution over these states. It then rewards turns for increasing posterior support for reliable future states using a tractable cluster-level approximation. The objective generalizes information-potential shaping from gold-answer supervision to settings without task-specific gold verifiers while avoiding the broadcasted rollout-level advantages used by standard GRPO. We formalize the framework, characterize its supervised gold-answer limit, and show that SIOP improves average performance over verifier-free outcome-level baselines on seven search-augmented agentic reasoning benchmarks while approaching a gold-supervised outcome baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/dl-m9/SIOP.git.

</details>


### [151] [Continual Knowledge Updating in LLM Systems: Learning Through Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05097)
*Andreas Pattichis,Constantine Dovrolis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memini提出了一种基于生物启发的联想记忆系统，通过Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型中的快慢耦合动力学，将外部记忆重构为能自主组织、具有情景敏感性和选择性遗忘能力的学习基质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的外部记忆系统大多采用显式管理方式，缺乏自主适应能力；而生物记忆通过多时间尺度耦合动力学实现高效的知识组织与更新。作者认为外部记忆应借鉴这一原理。

Method: Memini将知识组织为有向图，每条边携带两个耦合的内部变量（一快一慢），遵循Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型。通过快慢变量的耦合动力学，实现情景敏感性、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘。

Result: 论文未在摘要中提供具体实验结果，但声称该机制能将情景敏感性、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘统一为单一动力学机制的多个面向。

Conclusion: 外部记忆应被重新构想为具有自主学习能力的基质，而非静态存储；生物启发的快慢耦合动力学是实现这一目标的有效途径。

Abstract: LLMs are trained once, then deployed into a world that never stops changing. External memory compensates for this, but most systems manage it explicitly rather than letting it adapt on its own. Biological memory works differently: coupled multi-timescale dynamics make new associations immediately usable, strengthen what repetition confirms, and let the rest fade. We argue that external memory should follow a similar principle. In Memini, this view takes the form of an associative memory that organizes knowledge as a directed graph. Each edge carries two coupled internal variables, one fast and one slow, following the Benna-Fusi model of synaptic consolidation. From this coupling, episodic sensitivity, gradual consolidation, and selective forgetting emerge as facets of a single mechanism, reframing external memory as a learning substrate that reorganizes through its own dynamics.

</details>


### [152] [Discovering Sparse Counterfactual Factors via Latent Adjustment for Survey-based Community Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04460)
*Fatima Ashraf,Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Yufang Zhou,Yan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏反事实社区干预的框架，通过潜在表示对齐和最优传输，从交通调查数据中生成政策可行、稀疏且可解释的群体干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通调查分析多为描述性或预测性，缺乏能够生成稀疏、政策可行的干预策略的方法。

Method: 使用固定基非负潜在表示保持可比性；通过Shapley归因识别目标相关潜在因子并映射到可控变量；利用熵正则化最优传输最小化干预后目标分布与参考分布之间的差异，并引入加权ℓ₂,₁范数促进政策杠杆稀疏性。

Result: 在真实交通调查数据集上，所提框架生成了紧凑、可解释的政策可行干预方案，提升了群体转化率并保持了干预稀疏性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从调查数据中有效提取稀疏、可操作的政策干预策略，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Transportation surveys are widely used to understand travel preferences and adoption barriers, yet most survey-based analyses remain descriptive or predictive and rarely provide sparse, policy-feasible intervention strategies. We study sparse counterfactual community intervention from survey responses, where the goal is to shift a target respondent group toward a desired reference group through controllable survey-variable adjustments. We formulate this task as a policy-feasible distributional alignment problem using a fixed-basis nonnegative latent representation that preserves pre/post comparability and provides a stable map from latent factors to original variables. To make latent movement actionable, target-relevant latent factors are identified through Shapley-guided attribution and transferred to controllable variables as intervention priorities. Feasible group-level adjustments are then learned by minimizing an entropy-regularized optimal-transport discrepancy between the post-intervention target distribution and the reference distribution, together with a weighted $\ell_{2,1}$ penalty that promotes shared policy-lever sparsity. Experiments on real-world transportation survey datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact and interpretable policy-feasible interventions with explicit adjustment magnitudes, improves population-level conversion, and preserves intervention sparsity. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/latent-group-alignment.git

</details>


### [153] [Library learning with e-graphs on jazz harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04622)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers,Xinyi Guan,Martin Rohrmeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于库学习的计算模型，通过从和弦进行语料库中搜索由原始和声关系组成的程序，发现简洁生成性解释，并利用基于e-graph的演绎解析与库学习高效搜索程序与库的联合空间，以模拟人类对爵士和声模式的内化学习过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够获取高度结构化的音乐模式直觉理解，但往往需要多次反复聆听和反思才能完全内化。现有模型尚未很好地捕捉这种内化过程，因此需要一种计算模型来模拟人类学习爵士和声模式的方式。

Method: 基于库学习的计算模型：给定和弦进行语料库，模型搜索由原始和声关系组成的程序空间；先枚举每个片段的可能程序，再联合学习一个和声模式库和重构后的程序；为高效搜索程序与库的联合空间，将演绎解析与基于e-graph的库学习相整合。

Result: 通过评估程序和库的直觉性，以及与人编写和声推导的相似性，来检验模型捕捉人类音乐模式学习的能力。

Conclusion: 该模型能够有效模拟人类对爵士和声模式的内化学习过程，生成的程序和库具有良好的直觉性，且与人工编写的和声推导具有相似性。

Abstract: Humans can acquire a highly structured intuitive understanding of musical patterns, yet these patterns often require multiple iterations of reflection and re-listening to internalize fully. To capture such an internalization process, we present a computational model for the learning of jazz harmonic patterns based on library learning. Given a corpus of harmonic progressions, our model searches over a space of programs composed of primitive harmonic relations in order to discover concise generative explanations of the corpus. The model first enumerates possible programs for each piece, and then jointly learns a library of harmonic patterns and refactored programs. To efficiently navigate the vast joint space of programs and libraries, we integrate deductive parsing with library learning on e-graphs. We explore how well our model captures aspects of human musical pattern learning by evaluating the intuitiveness of both programs and libraries, as well as similarities to human-written harmonic derivations.

</details>


### [154] [Automated Formal Proofs of Combinatorial Identities via Wilf-Zeilberger Guidance and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04472)
*Beibei Xiong,Hangyu Lv,Junqi Liu,Yisen Wang,Shaoshi Chen,Jianlin Wang,Zhengfeng Yang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WZ-LLM是一种神经符号框架，将Wilf-Zeilberger机械证明方法与LLM结合，在Lean 4中自动证明组合恒等式，在LCI-Test上达到34%的证明成功率，显著优于DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动化组合恒等式形式化证明时面临长程推理规划困难、搜索空间爆炸的问题；而WZ方法虽能机械化证明但缺乏智能推理能力。将两者结合以互补优势是核心动机。

Method: 1) 将WZ证明方案转化为Lean 4可执行证明草图；2) LLM证明器用于补全机器可检查的子目标；3) 通过Lean内核验证的引导循环训练专用WZ-Prover，辅以专家验证迭代和DAPO强化学习精炼。

Result: 在LCI-Test上达到34%证明成功率，超越DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等基线；在CombiBench和PutnamBench-Comb上持续获得性能增益。

Conclusion: 该框架提供两方面的互补优势：对超出WZ适用范围恒等式的直接证明能力提升，以及WZ草图引导专用证明器时端到端成功率的大幅提高。

Abstract: Automating formal proofs of combinatorial identities is challenging for LLM-based provers, as long-horizon proof planning is required and unconstrained search quickly explodes. Symbolic methods such as the Wilf-Zeilberger (WZ) method can achieve a mechanized proof of combinatorial identities by constructing special auxiliary functions and demonstrating that they satisfy specific recurrence relations. We propose WZ-LLM, a neuro-symbolic framework that turns WZ proof plans into executable proof sketches in Lean 4 and uses an LLM-based prover to discharge the resulting machine-checkable subgoals. We also train a dedicated WZ-Prover via a Lean-kernel-verified bootstrapping loop with expert-verified iteration, followed by DAPO-based refinement. Experiments show that WZ-LLM achieves a 34% proof success rate on LCI-Test (100 classic combinatorial identities), outperforming strong baselines such as DeepSeek-V3 and Goedel-Prover-V2, and delivering consistent gains on CombiBench and PutnamBench-Comb. These results indicate that our framework provides two complementary strengths: improved direct proving for identities beyond the scope of WZ, and substantially higher end-to-end success when WZ sketches guide a specialized prover.

</details>


### [155] [Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement for Incentivizing Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04747)
*Leon Witt,Togrul Abbasli,Kentaroh Toyoda,Wojciech Samek,Lucy Klinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了知识无关的相关一致性（KFCA）方法，用于在联邦学习中奖励客户端贡献，无需依赖真实标签、公共测试集或分布知识。在分类报告和诚实多数假设下，KFCA是严格诚实的，解决了相关一致性（CA）方法面对标签翻转攻击的脆弱性。在联邦大语言模型适配器微调和真实PCB检测任务上的评估表明，KFCA支持高效的实时奖励计算，适用于去中心化和基于区块链的激励机制设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习激励机制中，相关一致性（CA）方法虽然不依赖真实标签，但容易受到标签翻转攻击（label-flipping）的威胁，即恶意客户端可以通过操纵标签来操纵奖励。本文旨在设计一种无需任何知识（无真实标签、无公共测试集、无分布知识）且能抵御标签翻转攻击的诚实激励机制。

Method: 提出知识无关的相关一致性（KFCA）方法。该方法在分类报告场景下，假设诚实客户端占多数，通过设计一致性协议使得诚实客户端报告真实信息可以获得最大奖励，而偏离真实报告会降低奖励，从而保证严格诚实性（strictly truthful）。

Result: 在联邦大语言模型（LLM）适配器微调任务和真实PCB（印刷电路板）检测任务上进行了评估，证明了KFCA能够实现高效的实时奖励计算，并且适用于去中心化和区块链驱动的激励机制场景。

Conclusion: KFCA是第一个无需任何知识且能抵御标签翻转攻击的联邦学习诚实激励机制，为去中心化和区块链场景下的联邦学习提供了可行的奖励分配方案。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement (KFCA) to reward client contributions in federated learning (FL) without relying on ground truth, a public test set, or distribution knowledge. Under categorical reports and an honest majority, KFCA is strictly truthful, addressing the label-flipping vulnerability of Correlated Agreement (CA). We evaluate KFCA on federated LLM adapter tuning and a real-world PCB inspection task, showing efficient real-time reward computation suitable for decentralized and blockchain-based incentive designs.

</details>


### [156] [Data-dependent Exploration for Online Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04477)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Yuting Tang,Jiandong Zhang,Lanjihong Ma,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据依赖的探索方法（DEPO），通过历史数据构建不确定性奖励，在在线RLHF中实现高效探索，理论上提供数据依赖的遗憾界，实验表明能提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RLHF中现有探索策略依赖基于策略的期望来估计探索奖励，但受限于有限的历史偏好数据，这些估计不可靠，导致策略过早放弃未充分探索但可能包含高价值行为的区域。

Method: 提出DEPO方法，利用历史偏好数据构建不确定性奖励，为高不确定性区域提供额外探索奖励，鼓励LLM探索潜在高价值区域。

Result: 理论上给出了数据依赖的遗憾界，比最坏情况界更紧；实验上在多个基准上一致优于强基线，展示了更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: DEPO是一种简单可扩展的在线RLHF探索方法，通过数据驱动的不确定性估计有效提升了探索效率和模型对齐性能。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) by continuously collecting new preference feedback during training. A foundational challenge in this setting is exploration, which requires algorithms that enable the LLMs to generate informative comparisons that improve sample-efficiency in online RLHF. Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estimate reliably from the limited historical preference data available during training; as a result, the policy can prematurely down-weight under-explored regions that may contain high-value behaviors. In this paper, we propose data-dependent exploration for preference optimization (DEPO), a simple and scalable method that leverages historical data to construct an extra uncertainty bonus for high-uncertainty regions, encouraging exploration toward potentially high-value data. Theoretically, we provide a data-dependent regret bound for the proposed algorithm, showing that it adapts to the hardness of the learning task itself and can be tighter than worst-case bounds in practice. Empirically, the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines across benchmarks, demonstrating improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [157] [Cognitive Twins: Investigating Personalized Thinking Model Building and Its Performance Enhancement with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04761)
*Wu-Yuin Hwang,Nur Alif Ilyasa,Muhammad Irfan Luthfi,Yuniar Indrihapsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化思维模型（PTM），这是一种基于Marzano新教育目标分类学的五层层次化可解释学习者表征，利用大语言模型、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类构建学习者的认知孪生模型。在40名参与者为期七周的研究中，通过自动评估（F1≈75%）、用户评分（Likert均值4.26-4.30）和语义对齐验证（主题连贯性从0.436增至0.626）证明了其可接受的信度和语义抽象能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI教育系统中的学习者建模多聚焦于知识状态或技能水平，缺乏对学习者深层思维模式（如认知习惯、元认知倾向、自我系统价值观）的建模与可解释表征，无法真正实现个性化认知支持。

Method: PTM采用五层层次结构（行为实例→行为模式→认知常规→元认知倾向→自我系统价值观），基于Marzano新分类学。构建流水线包括：LLM推理（Gemini 2.5 Pro）提取证据、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类。评估采用三种方法：自动原子信息点匹配、用户Likert量表评分、语义对齐验证（主题连贯性与词汇重叠分析）。

Result: 自动评估F1分数为74.57%（前HITL）和75.48%（后HITL）；用户评分为4.26/5.0（前）和4.30/5.0（后）；主题连贯性从行为层0.436增至核心价值层0.626，词汇重叠从0.114降至0.007，验证了跨层语义抽象模式。

Conclusion: PTM能够以可接受的信度生成学习者思维表征，用户普遍感知模型反映了自身思维，且各层次呈现一致的语义抽象递进规律，为AI教育中的可解释认知建模提供了有效框架。

Abstract: This paper presents the Personalized Thinking Model (PTM), a hierarchical and interpretable learner representation designed for AI supported education. PTM organizes evidence from learner journals into a five-layer structure covering behavioral instances, behavioral patterns, cognitive routines, metacognitive tendencies, and self-system values. PTM is grounded in Marzano's New Taxonomy of Educational Objectives and tries to clone learner's thinking model and build cognitive twin. It was constructed using a pipeline that combines large language model inference (Gemini 2.5 Pro), sentence embeddings, dimensionality reduction, and consensus clustering. This paper evaluates PTM fidelity through three methods applied to 40 participants in a seven-week study. First, automatic evaluation using atomic information point matching yielded an overall F1 score of 74.57% before human-in-the-loop (HITL) refinement and 75.48% after refinement. Second, user evaluation using a Likert scale produced mean ratings of 4.26 and 4.30 on a five-point scale for pre and post-HITL conditions respectively. Third, semantic alignment verification showed that topic coherence increased from 0.436 at the behavioral layer to 0.626 at the core value layer, while lexical overlap with journal vocabulary decreased from 0.114 to 0.007 across those same layers. These results suggest that the PTM produces outputs with acceptable fidelity, was generally perceived by users as reflecting their thinking, and showed a pattern consistent with semantic abstraction across layers.

</details>


### [158] [Quadrature-TreeSHAP: Depth-Independent TreeSHAP and Shapley Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04497)
*Ron Wettenstein,Rory Mitchell,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯-勒让德求积的Quadrature-TreeSHAP，用8个固定求积点实现任意阶Shapley交互值的数值稳定计算，消除树深度依赖，在CPU/GPU上取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Path-Dependent TreeSHAP在运行时间（依赖树深度）、数值稳定性和高阶交互支持之间存在权衡，缺乏同时满足三者需求的统一方法。

Method: 将Shapley值重新表述为加权Banzhaf交互多项式在特征参与概率p上的积分，采用8点Gauss-Legendre求积数值逼近，消除深度依赖并支持SIMD并行，集成至XGBoost并支持CPU/GPU。

Result: 12个XGBoost基准上，Shapley值CPU加速1.06x-10.59x，GPU加速1.84x-6.95x；成对交互CPU加速3.80x-58.11x；高阶交互相比TreeSHAP-IQ加速最高达1200x。

Conclusion: Quadrature-TreeSHAP以固定求积点方案同时解决了数值稳定性、深度依赖和高阶交互扩展问题，是现有Path-Dependent TreeSHAP的全面改进。

Abstract: Shapley values are a standard tool for explaining predictions of tree ensembles, with Path-Dependent SHAP being the most widely used variant. Despite substantial progress, existing methods still exhibit trade-offs between depth-dependent runtime, numerical stability, and support for higher-order interactions. To address these challenges, we introduce Quadrature-TreeSHAP, a quadrature-based reformulation of Path-Dependent TreeSHAP that is numerically stable, naturally extends to any-order Shapley interaction values and is practically insensitive to tree depth. Our implementation supports both CPU and GPU and is integrated into XGBoost.
  Our method is based on a weighted-Banzhaf interaction polynomial, which expresses Banzhaf interaction values as expectations under a feature participation probability $p$. Shapley values and any-order interaction values are then recovered by integrating these polynomials over $p$ from 0 to 1. We evaluate these integrals using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and show that, in practice, only 8 fixed quadrature points are sufficient to reach machine precision. In fact, Quadrature-TreeSHAP with 8 fixed points achieves greater numerical stability than TreeSHAP. This fixed-point formulation removes depth dependence from the inner computation and enables efficient SIMD execution.
  We confirm these advantages empirically. On 12 XGBoost benchmarks, Quadrature-TreeSHAP computes Shapley values 1.06x-10.59x faster than TreeSHAP on CPU and 1.84x-6.95x faster than GPUTreeSHAP on GPU. Shapley pairwise interactions are 3.80x-58.11x faster on CPU, with higher-order interactions achieving speedups of up to 1200x compared to TreeSHAP-IQ.

</details>


### [159] [Gradient Scaling Effects in Adaptive Spectral PINNs for Stiff Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04502)
*Isabela M. Yepes,Pavlos Protopapas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了初始条件（IC）嵌入函数（指数型 vs. 线性门控）对自适应谱PINNs在刚性和振荡动力系统训练中的影响，发现IC门控函数通过显式时间梯度缩放显著改变优化条件，并且在刚度参数变化时，最优门控选择会发生反转（中等刚度下指数门控更优，高刚度下线性门控更优），验证了IC嵌入并非中性设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注谱参数化等表示层面的改进，但初始条件嵌入对自适应谱PINNs优化条件的影响尚未得到充分刻画。

Method: 以非线性刚性弹簧-摆ODE为受控基准，对比指数型和线性IC门控与固定/自适应傅里叶谱骨干的组合，使用相对L²误差和最大逐点误差评估，并采用配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验（Holm校正）进行统计验证。

Result: 刚度依赖的优劣反转：中等刚度(k=20)下指数门控误差更低但异质性高，高刚度(k=60)下线性门控更优；更大刚度下进一步出现反转；结果在两类误差指标及统计检验中一致。

Conclusion: IC嵌入不是中性设计选择，其诱导的梯度缩放显著影响PINNs在刚性区域的优化条件，且对基线与自适应谱模型呈现不同的敏感性模式。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) often struggle to train reliably on stiff and oscillatory dynamical systems due to poor optimization conditioning. While prior work has emphasized representational remedies such as spectral parameterizations, the optimization implications of initial-condition (IC) embeddings in adaptive spectral PINNs have not been well characterized. In this work, we show that the choice of IC gating function induces explicit time-dependent gradient scaling, which interacts with spectral representations during training. Using a nonlinear stiff spring-pendulum ODE as a controlled benchmark, we compare exponential and linear IC gates in combination with fixed and adaptive Fourier spectral trunks. We observe stiffness-dependent changes in relative dominance for adaptive PINNs: at moderate stiffness ($k=20$), exponential gating often yields lower error but exhibits heterogeneous behavior across random seeds, whereas at higher stiffness ($k=60$), linear gating becomes preferable, with additional reversals observed at larger $k$. These trends hold for both relative $L^2$ error and maximum pointwise error and are confirmed by paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. Overall, our results demonstrate that IC embeddings are not a neutral design choice in PINNs: the induced gradient scaling materially shapes optimization conditioning in stiff regimes, with distinct sensitivity patterns in baseline and adaptive spectral models.

</details>


### [160] [FL-Sailer: Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning for Scalable Single-Cell Epigenetic Data Analysis via Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04519)
*Guangyi Zhang,Yi Dai,Yiyun He,Junhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FL-Sailer是首个专为scATAC-seq数据设计的联邦学习框架，通过自适应杠杆评分采样（降维80%）和不变VAE架构（互信息最小化解耦生物信号与技术噪声）两大创新，解决了超高维、极端稀疏和跨机构异质性三大挑战，在收敛保证下不仅实现了多机构协作，还因隐式正则化超越集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scATAC-seq数据因隐私法规和数据规模限制难以实现跨机构共享，联邦学习虽可提供隐私保护方案，但scATAC-seq分析中存在的超高维度、极端稀疏性和跨机构异质性三大根本性障碍使其直接应用面临挑战。

Method: 提出FL-Sailer框架，包含两大创新：(i) 自适应杠杆评分采样，在保持生物学可解释特征的同时将维度降低80%；(ii) 不变VAE架构，通过互信息最小化将生物信号与技术混杂因素解耦。并提供收敛性保证，证明其以有界误差收敛到原高维问题的近似解。

Result: 在合成数据和真实表观基因组数据集上的广泛实验表明，FL-Sailer不仅实现了此前不可行的多机构协作，还通过利用自适应采样作为隐式正则化器抑制技术噪声，从而超越了集中式方法。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在针对领域特定挑战进行定制后，可以成为协作表观基因组研究的优越范式。

Abstract: Single-cell ATAC-seq (scATAC-seq) enables high-resolution mapping of chromatin accessibility, yet privacy regulations and data size constraints hinder multi-institutional sharing. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative, but faces three fundamental barriers in scATAC-seq analysis: ultra-high dimensionality, extreme sparsity, and severe cross-institutional heterogeneity. We propose FL-Sailer, the first FL framework designed for scATAC-seq data. FL-Sailer integrates two key innovations: (i) adaptive leverage score sampling, which selects biologically interpretable features while reducing dimensionality by 80%, and (ii) an invariant VAE architecture, which disentangles biological signals from technical confounders via mutual information minimization. We provide a convergence guarantee, showing that FL-Sailer converges to an approximate solution of the original high-dimensional problem with bounded error. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real epigenomic datasets demonstrate that FL-Sailer not only enables previously infeasible multi-institutional collaborations but also surpasses centralized methods by leveraging adaptive sampling as an implicit regularizer to suppress technical noise. Our work establishes that federated learning, when tailored to domain-specific challenges, can become a superior paradigm for collaborative epigenomic research.

</details>


### [161] [YOTOnet: Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Fault Diagnosis via Domain-Conditioned Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04528)
*Zesen Wang,Zihao Wu,Yue Hu,Yang Gao,Fuzhen Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出YOTOnet架构，通过物理感知不变特征蒸馏、域条件稀疏专家和双头分类系统，实现机械装备故障诊断的"一次训练、跨域部署"，在5个轴承数据集上验证了零样本泛化的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械装备故障诊断中深度学习方法受域偏移限制严重，难以在不同设备和工况间泛化；受基础模型零样本泛化成功启发，探索将基础模型原则引入工业故障诊断领域。

Method: YOTOnet包含三个核心组件：(1)物理感知不变特征蒸馏器，利用多尺度扩张卷积和FFT时频融合提取域无关表征；(2)域条件稀疏专家(DC-MoE)，通过学习门控自适应路由输入到专用处理器；(3)双头分类系统辅以辅助监督。

Result: 在5个公开轴承数据集(CWRU, MFPT, XJTU, OTTAWA, HUST)上，通过30个跨数据协议验证YOTOnet优于现有方法；观察到清晰的缩放效应：平均测试F1从0.5339(1个训练集)提升至0.705(4个训练集)，从3到4个数据集时增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 基础模型原则能够实现工业故障诊断的鲁棒"一次训练、多次部署"方案，为跨域故障诊断提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Mechanical equipment forms the critical backbone of modern industrial production, yet domain shift severely limits the generalization of deep learning based fault diagnosis models across different equipment and operating conditions.Inspired by the success of foundation models in achieving zero-shotgeneralization, we propose YOTOnet (You Only Train Once), a novel architecture specifically designed for cross-domain fault diagnosis in mechanical equipment.YOTOnet comprises three core components: (1) a physics-aware Invariant Feature Distiller that extracts domain-agnostic representations using multi-scale dilated convolutions and FFT-based time-frequency fusion,(2) Domain-Conditioned Sparse Experts (DC-MoE) that adaptively route inputs to specialized processors via learned gating without external meta-data, and (3) a dual-head classification system with auxiliary supervision.Extensive validation on five public bearing datasets (CWRU, MFPT, XJTU,OTTAWA, HUST) through 30 cross-dataset protocols demonstrates the superiority of YOTOnet compared with other state-of-the-art methods. Critically, we observe a clear scaling effect-average test F1 improves from 0.5339(1 training dataset) to 0.705 (4 datasets), with a clear gain when moving from 3 to 4 datasets. These findings provide empirical evidence that foundation model principles can enable robust, train-once deployment for industrial fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [162] [Power Distribution Bridges Sampling, Self-Reward RL, and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04542)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于幂分布（power distribution），揭示了其桥接了采样、自奖励KL正则化强化学习和自蒸馏三者之间的关系。从采样视角看，局部近似无法复现序列级幂分布；从RL视角看，幂分布是KL正则化RL的闭式最优解，由此引出幂自蒸馏方法。实验表明幂自蒸馏能在大幅降低推理成本的同时匹配或超越幂采样的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关于强化学习（RL）是否为大语言模型（LLMs）的强推理能力负责存在争议，同时蒸馏和推理时采样（包括幂采样）被证明有效，但RL、蒸馏和采样三者之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: 证明幂分布是KL正则化RL在模型序列级对数概率作为奖励时的闭式最优解；提出幂自蒸馏（power self-distillation），将幂采样的计算成本摊销到教师样本的监督训练中；在推理任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 幂采样能提升自奖励；真实奖励的提升取决于与自奖励的对齐程度；幂自蒸馏能在大幅降低推理成本的同时匹配或超越幂采样的性能。

Conclusion: 幂分布统一了采样、自奖励KL正则化RL和自蒸馏三个框架；幂自蒸馏作为离线蒸馏替代方法，可实现自奖励锐化，且真实奖励的改进由幂分布下真实奖励与自奖励的协方差决定。

Abstract: Recent analyses question whether reinforcement learning (RL) is responsible for strong reasoning in large language models (LLMs). At the same time, distillation and inference-time sampling, including power sampling, have emerged as effective ways to improve LLM performance. However, the relationship among RL, distillation, and sampling remains unclear. In this study, we focus on the power distribution, the target distribution of power sampling, and show that the power distribution bridges sampling, self-reward KL-regularized RL, and self-distillation. From the sampling perspective, we show that inexpensive local approximations cannot reproduce sequence-level power without information about possible suffixes. From the RL perspective, the power distribution is the closed-form optimizer of KL-regularized RL when the model's sequence-level log-probabilities are used as the reward. This identification leads to power self-distillation, an offline distillation surrogate that shares the same target distribution and amortizes the cost of power sampling into supervised training on teacher samples. We further show that power self-distillation can achieve self-reward sharpening, while improvement in a downstream true reward is governed by the covariance between true reward and self-reward under the power distribution. Experiments on reasoning tasks support our analysis: power sampling raises self-reward, true-reward gains depend on alignment with self-reward, and power self-distillation can match or exceed the performance of power sampling at much lower inference cost.

</details>


### [163] [Event-Based Early Warning of Vineyard Disease Risk from Environmental Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04548)
*Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Danco Davcev,Slobodan Kalajdziski,Kosta Mitreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的葡萄园病害风险早期预警方法，将传统每日分类任务重新定义为预测未来3-7天内疾病风险期的转换，并利用多年代气象数据评估了XGBoost、LSTM和TCN等模型，结果表明事件式建模优于传统的持续性预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将病害预测视为每日存在性分类任务，这倾向于产生持续性驱动的预测，且只能提供有限的短期预警支持，无法满足实际农业生产中对 actionable（可操作）短期预警的需求。

Method: 1）将任务重新定义为预测未来3-7天窗口内标注疾病风险期的转换事件；2）定义新的事件时要求最小无疾病间隔，减少标签分片；3）利用多年代农业气象数据构建输入表示，捕获湿度动态、降雨累积、温度变异以及通过循环时间编码的季节结构；4）评估XGBoost、LSTM和TCN三种代表性方法。

Result: 事件式建模支持实际的短期预警，不同模型在事件召回率、提前时间和误报行为之间表现出不同的权衡。

Conclusion: 问题定义在环境时间序列学习中至关重要，事件式预测方法对葡萄园病害预警系统具有实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental observations is essential for timely intervention and more sustainable crop protection. However, many existing studies formulate disease prediction as daily presence classification, which can favor persistence-driven predictions and provide only limited support for actionable short-horizon warning. In this paper, we present an event-based approach for early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental time series and evaluate it through a vineyard case study. Rather than predicting daily disease status, the task is reformulated to predict transitions into annotated disease-risk periods within a future window of 3-7 days. To reduce fragmentation caused by short interruptions in the binary labels, new events are defined only after a minimum disease-free gap. This formulation encourages models to capture environmental precursors associated with upcoming risk periods instead of merely reproducing temporal persistence. Using multi-year agro-meteorological data, we construct input representations that capture humidity dynamics, rainfall accumulation, temperature variability, and seasonal structure through cyclic temporal encoding. We evaluate representative methods from classical machine learning and deep learning, including XGBoost, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCNs), using both standard classification metrics and an event-oriented early warning protocol. The results show that the event-based formulation supports practical short-horizon warning, while the compared models exhibit distinct trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior. Overall, the study underscores the importance of problem formulation in environmental time-series learning and demonstrates the value of event-based prediction for vineyard disease warning systems.

</details>


### [164] [EP-GRPO: Entropy-Progress Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization with Implicit Process Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04960)
*Song Yu,Li Li,Wenwen Zhao,Zhisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO在推理任务中存在的三种信用分配失败（统一令牌粒度、统一极性、零方差崩溃），提出EP-GRPO框架，通过熵门控调制、隐式过程信号和累积熵映射实现密集自监督引导，在数学推理基准上取得更优准确率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在推理任务中存在三种信用分配失败：统一令牌粒度忽略异构信息价值、统一极性惩罚正确步骤奖励错误步骤、零方差崩溃消除结果驱动梯度，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 提出EP-GRPO框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 熵门控调制，优先处理高熵决策关键点；2) 基于策略散度的隐式过程信号，结合结果优势提供令牌级定向反馈，无需外部奖励模型；3) 累积熵映射实现进度对齐的优势归一化，在零奖励方差下维持梯度流动。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，EP-GRPO相比GRPO及其变体取得了更优的准确率和效率。

Conclusion: EP-GRPO通过挖掘模型内在信息流实现密集自监督引导，有效解决了GRPO的信用分配问题，提升了推理任务的性能与训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has advanced LLM reasoning. However, GRPO suffers from three credit assignment failures: uniform token-level granularity that ignores heterogeneous informational value, uniform polarity that penalizes correct steps and rewards incorrect ones, and zero-variance collapse that erases outcome-driven gradients. We systematically quantify these failures, revealing highly non-uniform token informativeness, widespread step-level polarity misalignment, and substantial training waste.
  To address these limitations, we propose Entropy-Progress Aligned GRPO (EP-GRPO), a framework that mines the model's intrinsic information flow for dense, self-supervised guidance. EP-GRPO integrates entropy-gated modulation to prioritize high entropy decision pivots, implicit process signals from policy divergence anchored to outcome advantages for directional token-level feedback without external reward models, and cumulative entropy mapping that enables progress-aligned advantage normalization, naturally maintaining gradient flow under zero reward variance.
  Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EP-GRPO achieves superior accuracy and efficiency compared to GRPO and its variants. The code will be available.

</details>


### [165] [Reliable Modeling of Distribution Shifts via Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04965)
*Philip Naumann,Jacob Kauffmann,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位移重塑最优传输（ReshapeOT），通过从观测样本位移的二阶矩估计马氏距离来重塑地面度量，从而在输入空间中构建“高速公路”，使传输路径更符合真实位移模式。该方法计算轻量、可无缝集成到任何基于成本矩阵的OT求解器中，实验表明在传输可靠性上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优传输的传输路径仅依赖于输入空间中的欧氏度量，可能违反真实的位移几何结构。作者旨在引入观测到的样本位移信息作为额外知识，使OT解更好地对齐真实的分布变化模式。

Method: 将欧氏度量替换为从位移二阶矩估计的马氏距离，重塑地面度量；该方法可集成到任何基于成本矩阵的OT求解器中，并可通过核方法进一步扩展。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，ReshapeOT在传输可靠性上取得显著提升，并在两个实际用例中证明了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过引入观测位移信息重塑地面度量，ReshapeOT是一种计算轻量、灵活且有效的改进OT方法，能更好地捕捉真实分布变化的几何结构。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) is a central framework for modeling distribution shifts. Because OT compares distributions directly in input space, a well-designed ground metric between observations is essential to ensure that the optimizer does not violate the true geometry of change. We propose Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport (ReshapeOT), a method that reshapes the ground metric by integrating observed sample displacements as an additional source of knowledge. Technically, ReshapeOT replaces the Euclidean metric with a Mahalanobis distance estimated from displacement second moments. This effectively carves expressways through the input space, inviting transport solutions that better align with observed displacements. Our method is computationally lightweight, integrates seamlessly into any OT solver that operates on a cost matrix, and can be kernelized for further flexibility. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that ReshapeOT achieves substantial gains in transport reliability. We further demonstrate our method's usefulness in two practical use cases.

</details>


### [166] [Skill Neologisms: Towards Skill-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04970)
*Antonin Berthon,Nicolas Astorga,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"技能新词"（skill neologisms）方法——将软令牌（soft tokens）集成到模型词汇表中，通过优化提升特定技能能力，无需权重更新即可选择性扩展模型能力。实验表明，预训练LLM已存在与程序知识相关的令牌，技能新词可学习并提升特定技能，且能与分布外技能组合，独立训练的技能新词可零样本组合，为基于技能的持续学习提供了可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展模型能力的方法存在局限：微调和参数高效变体面临灾难性遗忘风险，而基于上下文的方法表达能力有限且受限于模型有效上下文长度。因此需要一种可选择性扩展模型能力、避免遗忘且可组合的新方法。

Method: 提出技能新词（skill neologisms）方法，即在模型词汇表中集成可学习的软令牌，通过优化提升特定技能表现；无需更新模型权重，仅通过令牌表示学习新技能。

Result: 1) 预训练LLM中已存在与程序知识相关的令牌；2) 技能新词可学习并提升特定技能能力；3) 技能新词可与分布外技能组合；4) 独立训练的技能新词可实现零样本组合。

Conclusion: 技能新词为基于技能的持续学习提供了一种可扩展的路径，无需权重更新即可选择性扩展模型能力，且支持技能组合与泛化。

Abstract: Modern LLMs show mastery over an ever-growing range of skills, as well as the ability to compose them flexibly. However, extending model capabilities to new skills in a scalable manner is an open-problem: fine-tuning and parameter-efficient variants risk catastrophic forgetting, while context-based approaches have limited expressiveness and are constrained by the model's effective context. We explore skill neologisms--i.e., soft tokens integrated in the model's vocabulary and optimized to improve capabilities over a specific skill--as a way to selectively extend model capabilities to new skills without weight updates. We first observe that off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs already demonstrate tokens associated with procedural knowledge. We then show that skill neologisms can be learned to improve model capabilities on specific skills while being composable with out-of-distribution skills, and that independently trained skill neologisms can be composed zero-shot. These results suggest that skill neologisms may provide a scalable path towards skill-based continual learning.

</details>


### [167] [Threshold-Guided Optimization for Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04653)
*Jinbin Bai,Yu Lei,Qingyu Shi,Aosong Feng,Yi Xin,Zhuoran Zhao,Fei Shen,Kaidong Yu,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阈值引导的视觉生成模型对齐框架，通过将成对偏好优化转化为非配对标量反馈上的二值决策任务，结合置信度加权策略，在扩散和掩码生成模型上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成对偏好优化方法依赖于标注的偏好对，这在反馈仅为独立标量评分时难以扩展。本文旨在解决如何直接从标量反馈中有效优化视觉生成模型的对齐问题。

Method: 从KL正则化对齐目标出发，推导出最优策略隐式地将每个样本的奖励与一个实例特定的基线进行比较。作者提出用数据驱动的全局阈值替代该不可解的基线，将对齐转化为非配对数据上的二值决策任务，并引入置信度加权项来强调偏离阈值较大的样本。

Result: 在扩散和掩码生成两种范式上，跨越三个测试集和五个奖励模型，该方法在偏好对齐上一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该阈值引导框架为无需成对比较的视觉生成模型对齐提供了一种简洁而有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Aligning large visual generative models with human feedback is often performed through pairwise preference optimization. While such approaches are conceptually simple, they fundamentally rely on annotated pairs, limiting scalability in settings where feedback is collected as independent scalar ratings. In this work, we revisit the KL-regularized alignment objective and show that the optimal policy implicitly compares each sample's reward to an instance-specific baseline that is generally intractable. We propose a threshold-guided alignment framework that replaces this oracle baseline with a data-driven global threshold estimated from empirical score statistics. This formulation turns alignment into a binary decision task on unpaired data, enabling effective optimization directly from scalar feedback. We also incorporate a confidence weighting term to emphasize samples whose scores deviate strongly from the threshold, improving sample efficiency. Experiments across both diffusion and masked generative paradigms, spanning three test sets and five reward models, show that our method consistently improves preference alignment over previous methods. These results position our threshold-guided framework as a simple yet principled alternative for aligning visual generative models without paired comparisons.

</details>


### [168] [Preference-Based Self-Distillation: Beyond KL Matching via Reward Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05040)
*Xin Yu,Liuchen Liao,Yiwen Zhang,Yingchen Yu,Lingzhou Xue,Qinzhen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于偏好的自蒸馏方法PBSD，通过奖励正则化视角重新审视在线自蒸馏，用偏好学习替代传统的KL匹配，在数学推理和工具使用任务上取得更优性能与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏方法依赖固定教师的KL匹配，存在训练不稳定、推理性能退化以及缺乏探索多样性等问题，亟需一种更稳健的自蒸馏范式。

Method: 提出PBSD方法：推导奖励正则化目标函数，其解析最优解为奖励重新加权的教师分布，产生理论上优于原教师的目标策略；通过优化教师与学生样本间的偏好差距来实现，同时保持在线学生采样。

Result: 在数学推理和工具使用基准上，PBSD在多个模型规模下一致取得最强平均性能，相比现有自蒸馏基线提升了训练稳定性，同时保持token效率。

Conclusion: PBSD通过偏好学习与奖励正则化框架，有效克服了传统自蒸馏的稳定性与性能退化问题，为在线自蒸馏提供了新方向。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning, offering dense token-level training signals. However, its reliance on a stronger external teacher has driven recent work on on-policy self-distillation, where the same model serves as both teacher and student under different prompt contexts. Yet, existing self-distillation methods largely reduce learning to KL matching toward the context-augmented teacher model. This approach often suffers from training instability and can degrade reasoning performance over time. Moreover, self-distillation from the same model with prompt augmentation lacks the exploratory diversity provided by a genuine external teacher. To address these limitations, we move beyond fixed-teacher KL matching and propose \textbf{P}reference-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{D}istillation (\textbf{PBSD}), which revisits on-policy self-distillation through a reward-regularized perspective. Instead of directly matching the teacher distribution, we derive a reward-regularized objective whose analytic optimum is a reward-reweighted teacher distribution, yielding a target policy provably superior to the original teacher under this objective. Practically, PBSD optimizes preference gaps between teacher and student samples while maintaining on-policy student sampling. We support this framework with a statistical analysis of the induced preference-learning problem, formally establishing when on policy self-distillation is preferable to learning from an external teacher in our setting. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks across multiple model scales demonstrate that PBSD consistently achieves the strongest average performance among comparable baselines, showing improved training stability over prior self-distillation baselines while preserving token efficiency.

</details>


### [169] [Feature importance analysis for patient management decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04666)
*Michal Valko,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析电子健康记录中4486名心脏术后患者的数据，发现医生的实验室检查和用药决策通常可由少量关键特征准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解临床数据中哪些特征和属性对医生决策（开实验室检查单或处方药）影响最大。

Method: 基于4486名心脏术后患者的电子健康记录，提取335项实验室检查和407项用药决策，通过统计分析和特征重要性评估进行建模。

Result: 许多情况下，医生的决策可由一小部分特征子集很好地预测；报告了335项实验室决策和407项用药决策的汇总统计。

Conclusion: 临床决策可被少量关键特征有效建模，提示存在简化的决策模式或核心影响因素。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to understand what characteristics and features of clinical data influence physician's decision about ordering laboratory tests or prescribing medications the most. We conduct our analysis on data and decisions extracted from electronic health records of 4486 post-surgical cardiac patients. The summary statistics for 335 different lab order decisions and 407 medication decisions are reported. We show that in many cases, physician's lab-order and medication decisions can be well predicted from a small subset of all features.

</details>


### [170] [ITBoost: Information-Theoretic Trust for Robust Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04671)
*Ye Su,Longlong Zhao,Diego Garcia-Gil,Jipeng Guo,Gangchun Zhang,Jinxin Chen,Jinsong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ITBoost，通过MDL原则分析残差演化轨迹来评估样本可靠性，有效提升了梯度提升算法在标签噪声环境下的鲁棒性，同时在干净数据上保持最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升方法在处理含噪标签时性能显著下降，因为算法倾向于强调大梯度样本，却无法区分这些大误差是来自信息量大的困难样本还是不可靠的标签。

Method: 提出信息论信任提升（ITBoost），利用最小描述长度（MDL）原则衡量每个样本残差轨迹的复杂度，残差波动不规则的样本被视为不可靠并被降低权重；同时推导了在标签噪声下更紧的泛化界。

Result: 在多种表格数据基准测试中，ITBoost在噪声环境下优于主流提升方法和深度表格模型，且在干净数据上保持最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 通过追踪残差演化轨迹而非仅依赖瞬时误差来评估样本可靠性，能有效提升梯度提升方法对标签噪声的鲁棒性，同时不牺牲干净数据上的表现。

Abstract: Gradient boosting remains a strong and widely used method for tabular data learning, but its performance often degrades when training labels are noisy. This behavior is largely related to the way boosting algorithms emphasize samples with large gradients, without explicitly accounting for whether such errors originate from informative hard cases or from unreliable labels. We address this issue by reconsidering how sample reliability is evaluated during boosting. Instead of relying on instantaneous error, we examine the evolution of each sample's residuals across iterations. Based on this insight, we propose Information-Theoretic Trust Boosting (ITBoost), which uses the Minimum Description Length principle to measure the complexity of residual trajectories. Samples whose residual patterns fluctuate in an irregular manner are treated as less trustworthy and are down-weighted during learning. Theoretically, we derive a tighter generalization bound for ITBoost under label noise. Empirical results on various tabular benchmarks indicate that ITBoost provides improved robustness in noisy environments over leading boosting and deep tabular models, while retaining best average performance on clean data.

</details>


### [171] [HEXST: Hexagonal Shifted-Window Transformer for Spatial Transcriptomics Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04682)
*Keunho Byeon,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HEXST模型，通过几何对齐的Transformer架构，直接在六边形点坐标上建模空间转录组学数据，结合对比敏感差分目标和单细胞基础模型先验，从常规H&E染色组织切片中预测高保真空间基因表达，在七个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高且通量有限，阻碍大规模应用。现有从H&E图像推断空间基因表达的计算方法大多假设笛卡尔或几何无关的局部性，忽略了广泛使用的六边形点阵采样特性，且逐点回归目标常导致基因表达过度平滑，丢失基因特异性的空间异质性。

Method: 提出HEXST，一种几何对齐的Transformer：1）直接在六边形点坐标上操作，利用定制的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转变换位置编码实现高效的局部到全局上下文建模；2）在逐点回归基础上补充对比敏感差分目标；3）训练时引入预训练单细胞基础模型的转录组先验知识。

Result: 在七个空间转录组数据集上，HEXST一致优于现有最先进模型，提供准确且鲁棒的基因表达预测，同时保留了基因对比度和空间异质性。

Conclusion: HEXST通过几何对齐的六边形建模和对比增强训练策略，有效解决了现有方法中几何失配和过平滑问题，为从常规病理图像推断空间基因表达提供了更精准且实用的方案。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics offers spatially resolved gene expression profiling within tissue sections, but its cost and limited throughput hinder large-scale deployment. To extend this capability to routine practice, recent computational methods aim to infer spatial gene expression directly from ubiquitous hematoxylin and eosin-stained histology slides. However, most existing models assume Cartesian or geometry-agnostic locality, despite the hexagonal sampling of widely used spot-array platforms, and point-wise regression objectives often yield over-smoothed gene expression profiles, obscuring gene-specific spatial heterogeneity. To address these, we propose HEXST, a geometry-aligned Transformer for spatial gene expression prediction from histology. HEXST operates directly on hexagonal spot coordinates to enable efficient local-to-global contextual modeling via tailored shifted-window attention mechanism and hexagonal rotary positional encoding. To enhance gene-wise spatial contrast, HEXST complements point-wise regression with a contrast-sensitive differential objective and transcriptomic priors from a pretrained single-cell foundation model during training. Across seven spatial transcriptomics datasets, HEXST consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, providing accurate and robust spatial gene expression predictions while preserving gene-wise contrast and spatial heterogeneity.

</details>


### [172] [On the Wasserstein Gradient Flow Interpretation of Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05118)
*Arthur Gretton,Li Kevin Wenliang,Alexandre Galashov,James Thornton,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）视角分析了Deng等人（2026）提出的生成式漂移建模（GMD）框架。主要发现：GMD的一种算法对应KL散度WGF的极限点（含Parzen平滑）；实际实现的算法类似Sinkhorn散度WGF的不动点但缺乏理想性质；该思想可推广至MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别函数等其它WGF的极限点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）的理论视角，深入分析新提出的GMD框架，揭示其算法本质与最优传输几何之间的联系。

Method: 将GMD算法映射到WGF框架中，通过理论分析建立GMD与KL散度、Sinkhorn散度等不同WGF之间的关系，并讨论其扩展性。

Result: 1) GMD的一种算法等价于KL散度WGF的极限点（带Parzen平滑）；2) GMD实际实现的算法仅类似Sinkhorn散度WGF的不动点，但不具备后者的良好性质；3) GMD思想可扩展到MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别函数等WGF的极限点。

Conclusion: GMD可视为直接瞄准特定WGF的不动点，为理解该框架提供了理论支撑，并指出了其实际实现与理想形式之间的差距，同时展示了该思路的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Recently, Deng et al. (2026) proposed Generative Modeling via Drifting (GMD), a novel framework for generative tasks. This note presents an analysis of GMD through the lens of Wasserstein Gradient Flows (WGF), i.e., the path of steepest descent for a functional in the space of probability measures, equipped with the geometry of optimal transport. Unlike previous WGF-based contributions, GMD can be thought of as directly targeting a fixed point of a specific WGF flow. We demonstrate three main results: first, that one algorithm proposed by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to finding the limiting point of a WGF on the KL divergence, with Parzen smoothing on the densities. Second, that the algorithm actually implemented by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to a different procedure, which bears some resemblance to the fixed point of a WGF on the Sinkhorn divergence, but lacks certain desirable properties of the latter. Third, the same same idea can be extended to the limiting point of other WGFs, including the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), the sliced Wasserstein distance, and GAN critic functions.

</details>


### [173] [Adaptive Policy Selection and Fine-Tuning under Interaction Budgets for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05123)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Xiaoan Xu,Shangtong Zhang,Miroslav Pajic,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应策略选择与微调方法，用于离线到在线强化学习（O2O-RL），通过上置信界（UCB）方法在有限在线交互预算下高效选择并微调候选策略，解决了传统O2O-RL中离线策略评估不可靠及无法预知策略微调效果的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O2O-RL流程存在两个主要问题：(1) 离线策略评估（OPE）不可靠，仅基于OPE部署策略风险大；而在线评估（OE）虽能识别可行策略但消耗大量交互预算，这些本可用于微调。(2) 无法预先判断预训练策略在部署后微调是否能提升性能，尤其在非平稳环境中。因此，承诺单一部署策略的方法不切实际，而穷举微调所有候选策略又会违反交互预算约束。

Method: 首先使用不同离线RL算法和超参数训练一组候选策略，然后进行OPE获得初始性能估计。接着，基于UCB方法自适应地选择策略进行微调，根据策略的预测性能进行交互预算分配，高效利用在线交互。

Result: 在多种基准测试上，所提方法相较于O2O-RL基线方法获得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在有限在线交互预算下进行策略选择与微调的自适应方法，通过UCB策略有效权衡探索与利用，解决了OPE不可靠和策略微调效果不确定性的问题。

Abstract: In offline-to-online reinforcement learning (O2O-RL), policies are first safely trained offline using previously collected datasets and then further fine-tuned for tasks via limited online interactions. In a typical O2O-RL pipeline, candidate policies trained with offline RL are evaluated via either off-policy evaluation (OPE) or online evaluation (OE). The policy with the highest estimated value is then deployed and continually fine-tuned. However, this setup has two main issues. First, OPE can be unreliable, making it risky to deploy a policy based solely on those estimates, whereas OE may identify a viable policy with substantial online interaction, which could have been used for fine-tuning. Second--and more importantly--it is also often not possible to determine a priori whether a pretrained policy will improve with post-deployment fine-tuning, especially in non-stationary environments. As a result, procedures committing to a single deployed policy are impractical in many real-world settings. Moreover, a naive remedy that exhaustively fine-tunes all candidates would violate interaction budget constraints and is likewise infeasible. In this paper, we propose a novel adaptive approach for policy selection and fine-tuning under online interaction budgets in O2O-RL. Following the standard pipeline, we first train a set of candidate policies with different offline RL algorithms and hyperparameters; we then perform OPE to obtain initial performance estimates. We next adaptively select and fine-tune the policies based on their predicted performance via an upper-confidence-bound approach thereby making efficient use of online interactions. We demonstrate that our approach improves upon O2O-RL baselines with various benchmarks.

</details>


### [174] [Joint Treatment Effect Estimation from Incomplete Healthcare Data: Temporal Causal Normalizing Flows with LLM-driven Evolutionary MNAR Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05125)
*Olivia Jullian Parra,Sara Zoccheddu,David Catalan Cerezo,Tom Forzy,Franziska Ulrich,William Sutcliffe,Jakob Martin Burgstaller,Oliver Senn,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段因果效应估计管道：第一阶段用DAG约束的归一化流（CausalFlow-T）进行精确反事实推理；第二阶段用LLM驱动的演化填补器处理缺失数据。在合成基准和瑞士2型糖尿病真实EHR数据上验证了有效性，估计的GLP-1受体激动剂减重效果与随机对照试验证据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中存在时间依赖性混杂和高达50%-80%的非随机缺失（MNAR）生物标志物，而现有因果效应方法通常分别处理因果估计、缺失和时间结构，缺乏鲁棒性。因此需要一种能同时处理这些挑战的管道。

Method: 提出两阶段管道：(1) CausalFlow-T：DAG约束的归一化流模型，结合LSTM编码的患者历史，实现精确可逆的反事实推理，避免变分推断的近似误差；(2) LLM驱动的演化填补器：利用大语言模型提出可执行的填补算子（而非单个填充值），处理30%-80%的MNAR缺失。使用两个开源和一个商业LLM作为后端。

Result: 在合成和半合成基准上，消融实验表明DAG约束和精确推断各自处理不同的失败模式。填补器在30%-80% MNAR缺失下取得生物标志物和因果指标的最佳综合排名。在瑞士2型糖尿病真实EHR数据上，估计GLP-1受体激动剂相较于SGLT-2抑制剂的减重差异为-0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96]，与随机对照试验证据一致。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段管道能够从现实世界中不完整的纵向EHR数据中鲁棒地估计因果效应，填补了因果推断与缺失数据处理之间的方法论空白，且估计结果得到了随机对照试验证据的验证。

Abstract: Target trial emulation (TTE) enables causal questions to be studied with observational data when randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are infeasible. Yet treatment-effect methods often address causal estimation, missingness, and temporal structure separately, limiting their robustness in electronic health records (EHRs), where time-varying confounding and missing-not-at-random (MNAR) biomarkers can reach 50%--80%. We propose a two-stage pipeline for treatment effect estimation from incomplete longitudinal EHRs. First, CausalFlow-T, a directed acyclic graph (DAG)-constrained normalizing flow with long short-term memory (LSTM)-encoded patient history, performs exact invertible counterfactual inference, avoiding approximation errors from variational inference and separating confounding through explicit causal structure. Ablations on four synthetic and one semi-synthetic benchmark with known counterfactuals show that DAG constraints and exact inference address distinct failure modes: neither compensates for the other. Second, because CausalFlow-T requires completed inputs, we introduce an LLM-driven evolutionary imputer that proposes executable imputation operators rather than individual entries, and evaluate it with three large language model (LLM) backends, including two open-source models. Across 30%--80% MNAR missingness, this imputer achieves the best pooled rank over biomarker and causal metrics, leading in point-wise accuracy and temporal extrapolation while preserving average treatment effect (ATE) recovery as statistical baselines degrade. On Swiss primary-care EHRs from adults with type 2 diabetes initiating a GLP-1 receptor agonist or SGLT-2 inhibitor, the pipeline estimates a per-protocol weight-loss difference of -0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96] favoring GLP-1 receptor agonists, consistent with randomized evidence and obtained from realistically incomplete real-world EHRs.

</details>


### [175] [Ensuring Reliability in Programming Knowledge Tracing: A Re-evaluation of Attention-augmented Models and Experimental Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04727)
*Jaewook Kim,Hyeoncheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了编程知识追踪（PKT）模型，发现其性能提升很大程度上受注意力维度设置、序列构建顺序（忽略时间戳导致时序因果性破坏）等实现细节影响。通过统一超参数选择与交叉验证协议，在CodeWorkout数据集上重新评估发现，注意力增强模型与标准DKT之间的性能差距显著缩小，架构复杂性的增加并不一致地带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PKT模型虽然报告了强劲的实证性能，但其可靠性容易受到微妙实现细节和实验设计选择的影响，需要对这些模型的性能提升进行严格审视和可靠评估。

Method: 通过重新审视代表性PKT模型，识别注意力维度设置问题和序列构建中忽略ServerTimestamp导致的时序因果性违反；采用基于单折网格搜索的超参数选择，并在所有折中统一固定；系统分析最大序列长度和作业级别特征的影响。

Result: 在受控一致设置下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT之间的性能差距显著缩小，增加架构复杂性并不一致地带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 为编程知识追踪提供了可靠且可比较评估的实践指导，强调需要关注实现细节和实验设计对模型性能评估的影响。

Abstract: Programming Knowledge Tracing (PKT) has recently advanced through hybrid approaches that integrate attention-based feature modeling for code representation with RNN-based sequential prediction. While these models report strong empirical performance, their reliability can be sensitive to subtle implementation and experimental design choices. This study revisits representative PKT models and shows that reported gains can be substantially influenced by model configuration and sequence construction practices. We identify issues in attention dimension settings that affect performance estimates, and demonstrate that improper ordering of student attempts, such as ignoring ServerTimestamp, can violate temporal causality and lead to overly optimistic results. To ensure consistent evaluation, hyperparameters are selected via grid search guided by a single designated fold and then fixed uniformly across all folds during cross-validation. We further analyze the role of assignment-wise characteristics and systematically explore the impact of maximum sequence length. Using this protocol, we re-evaluate PKT models on the CodeWorkout dataset. Our results show that, under controlled and consistent settings, the performance gap between attention-enhanced models and standard DKT is significantly reduced, and increased architectural complexity does not consistently translate into superior performance. Beyond individual model comparisons, this work provides practical guidance for reliable and comparable evaluation in programming knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [176] [Superposition Is Not Necessary: A Mechanistic Interpretability Analysis of Transformer Representations for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05151)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对PatchTST进行机制可解释性分析，发现其时序预测中FFN表征并不依赖强叠加（superposition），表征稀疏且对潜在干预不敏感，这解释了为何简单线性模型（如DLinear）在该任务上能与Transformer竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在时序预测中广泛使用，但简单线性模型（如DLinear）依然具有竞争力，其背后的机制原因尚未被解释；本文旨在从机制可解释性角度探究Transformer内部表征是否真的利用了类似NLP中的叠加表征。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对PatchTST模型的FFN中间激活（post-GELU）进行探测，字典大小设置为原生维度的0.5x至4.0x；进行词典扩展实验、因果干预实验分析表征的稀疏性与叠加程度。

Result: 词典扩展对下游性能影响极小（平均仅0.214%），过完备词典中大量特征未被激活；对主导潜在特征进行因果干预仅产生极小的预测扰动；未发现FFN表征依赖强叠加的证据，表征保持稀疏且稳定。

Conclusion: 叠加并非时序预测标准基准上取得竞争性能的必要条件；时序预测任务可能不需要像NLP中那样丰富的组合表征，这解释了简单线性模型的持续竞争力。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have been widely adopted for time series forecasting, yet whether the representational mechanisms that make them powerful in NLP actually engage on time series data remains unexplored. The persistent competitiveness of simple linear models such as DLinear has fueled ongoing debate, but no mechanistic explanation for this phenomenon has been offered. We address this gap by applying sparse autoencoders (SAEs), a tool from mechanistic interpretability, to probe the internal representations of PatchTST. We first establish that a single-layer, narrow-dimensional transformer matches the forecasting performance of deeper configurations across commonly used benchmarks. We then train SAEs on the post-GELU intermediate FFN activations with dictionary sizes ranging from 0.5x to 4.0x the native dimensionality. Expanding the dictionary yields negligible downstream performance change (average 0.214%), with large portions of overcomplete dictionaries remaining inactive. Targeted causal interventions on dominant latent features produce minimal forecast perturbation. Across all evaluated settings, we observe no empirical evidence that the analyzed FFN representations rely on strong superposition.
