Instead, the representations remain sparse, stable under aggressive dictionary expansion, and largely insensitive to latent interventions. These results demonstrate that superposition is not necessary for competitive performance on standard forecasting benchmarks, suggesting they may not demand the rich compositional representations that drive transformer success in language modeling, and helping explain the persistent competitiveness of simple linear models

</details>


### [177] [Using Common Random Numbers for Simulation-based Planning with Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04732)
*Sandarbh Yadav,Frederic J Maliakkal,Harshad Khadilkar,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在基于仿真的规划中使用公共随机数（CRN）的效果，提出了一种在特定深度后使用rollout策略时能够（可证明地）降低相对效用方差的方法，并通过合成任务和实际应用（养老金发放任务和Ludo游戏中的UCT算法）验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机环境中，基于仿真rollout的规划虽广泛应用，但仿真过程中随机噪声会导致对动作效用估计的方差较大，影响决策质量。现有方法未充分探索如何利用公共随机数来降低相对效用估计的方差，从而提升规划性能。

Method: 提出在仿真rollout过程中，当rollout策略执行到一定深度后，引入公共随机数（Common Random Numbers, CRN）机制，以降低不同动作之间相对效用估计的方差。通过理论分析证明该方法在特定条件下可减少方差，并在合成任务以及养老金发放（单步前瞻规划）和Ludo游戏（UCT算法部署）两个实际应用中进行实验验证。

Result: 合成任务实验表明所提方案有效提升了任务性能；在实际应用中，该方法在养老金发放的单步前瞻规划任务和Ludo游戏的UCT算法部署中均展现了显著效果。

Conclusion: 在基于仿真的rollout规划中，适当使用公共随机数可以可证明地降低相对效用方差，从而提升决策性能和任务表现，该方法具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: Simulation-based planning with rollouts is a widely-deployed technique for decision making in stochastic environments. The primary instrument of simulation-based planning is a sampling model, which is repeatedly called to generate trajectories and estimate the utilities of available actions. Among the actions thus explored, one with the maximum estimated utility is then executed. In this paper, we examine the effect of using common random numbers in the simulation process. We obtain a simple recipe for (provably) reducing variance in relative utility when simulations invoke a rollout policy beyond some depth. Experiments on synthetic tasks confirm that our scheme improves task performance. The broader significance of our innovation is apparent from two practical applications: (1) single-step lookahead planning in a pension-disbursement task, and (2) a deployment of the well-known UCT algorithm for the game of Ludo.

</details>


### [178] [OSAQ: Outlier Self-Absorption for Accurate Low-bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04738)
*Zhikai Li,Zhen Dong,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSAQ方法，利用Hessian矩阵的低秩零空间进行加性权重抑制，在不影响任务损失的情况下消除LLM权重异常值，实现低比特量化且无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM参数规模庞大导致资源消耗和延迟高，权重量化虽可缓解，但权重中的系统性异常值严重制约低比特量化性能，现有方法（如缩放和旋转）效果仍不理想。

Method: 观察到Hessian矩阵在不同输入下具有低秩一致性，存在稳定的零空间；利用该零空间中的向量线性组合构造加性权重变换，在不改变任务损失的前提下抑制权重异常值；该变换可离线吸收到权重中，无需层间变换或推理开销；构造过程通过闭式解高效实现。

Result: 在2比特量化中，OSAQ结合GPTQ相比原始GPTQ困惑度降低超过40%，广泛实验验证了其有效抑制异常值和提升低比特量化性能的能力。

Conclusion: OSAQ通过Hessian低秩零空间指导的加性权重抑制，有效解决了LLM权重量化中的异常值问题，实现了高效的低比特量化且无额外推理负担。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities. However, their massive parameter scale leads to significant resource consumption and latency during inference. Post-training weight-only quantization offers a promising solution by reducing model size and accelerating token generation through alleviating the memory-bound issue. Nevertheless, the presence of inherent systematic outliers in weights continues to be a major obstacle. While existing methods, such as scaling and rotation, attempt to address this issue, the performance remains unsatisfactory. In this paper, we propose Outlier Self-Absorption Quantization (OSAQ), which performs additive weight suppression guided by the second-order low-rank property for low-bit weight-only quantization of LLMs. Specifically, we observe that the Hessian exhibits low-rank consistency across different inputs, with certain directions consistently showing vanishing curvature. Leveraging this property, we identify a stable null space of the Hessian and then construct an additive weight transformation by linearly combining the vectors within this null space, thereby suppressing weight outliers without affecting the task loss. This additive transformation can be absorbed into the weights offline, requiring no inter-layer transformations and introducing no inference overhead. Moreover, the construction is efficiently achieved by a closed-form solution, without resource-intensive training or iterative procedures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OSAQ effectively suppresses outliers and enhances low-bit quantization performance. For instance, in 2-bit quantization, OSAQ, when integrated with GPTQ, achieves over 40% lower perplexity compared to vanilla GPTQ.

</details>


### [179] [AxMoE: Characterizing the Impact of Approximate Multipliers on Mixture-of-Experts DNN Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04754)
*Omkar B Shende,Marcello Traiola,Gayathri Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次探索近似计算与混合专家（MoE）架构的交叉领域，提出AxMoE框架，系统评估了近似乘法器对三种MoE变体（Hard MoE、Soft MoE、Cluster MoE）在多种CNN和ViT架构上的影响。实验表明：未经重训练时Dense基线在CNN上最鲁棒；经过近似感知重训练后，ResNet-20可在全部乘法器范围内完全恢复，而VGG在激进近似下不可逆退化，但在ViT-Small上Hard MoE在激进近似下优于Dense基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端DNN推理需要在精度、计算效率和能耗之间取得平衡。近似计算和MoE架构分别被独立研究为高效推理的路径，但二者的交互作用此前完全未被探索。

Method: 提出AxMoE框架，使用EvoApproxLib库中的8种8位有符号乘法器（含一种精确基线），在CIFAR-100上的ResNet-20、VGG11_bn、VGG19_bn和Tiny ImageNet-200上的ViT-Small上评估Hard MoE、Soft MoE、Cluster MoE三种MoE变体。

Result: 无重训练时Dense基线在CNN上最鲁棒；ViT-Small上所有拓扑退化速率相当。近似感知重训练后：ResNet-20可完全恢复；VGG在中等近似可恢复，激进近似下除Cluster MoE on VGG11_bn外均不可逆退化；ViT-Small上Hard MoE在激进近似下优于Dense基线。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来近似MoE的硬件-软件协同设计策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Deep neural network (DNN) inference at the edge demands simultaneous improvements in accuracy, computational efficiency, and energy consumption. Approximate computing and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have each been studied as independent routes towards efficient inference, the former by replacing exact arithmetic with low-power approximate multipliers, the latter by routing inputs through specialized expert sub-networks to enable conditional computation. However, their interaction remains entirely unexplored. This paper presents AxMoE, the first study of the impact of approximate multiplication on MoE DNN architectures. We evaluate three MoE variants: Hard MoE, Soft MoE, and Cluster MoE against dense baselines across three CNN architectures (ResNet-20, VGG11_bn, VGG19_bn) on CIFAR-100 and a Vision Transformer (ViT-Small) on Tiny ImageNet-200 dataset, using eight 8-bit signed multipliers (including one exact baseline) from the EvoApproxLib library. Results show that, without retraining, the Dense baseline is the most resilient topology across all CNN architectures, whereas on ViT-Small, all topologies degrade at comparable rates regardless of routing strategy. After approximate-aware retraining, recovery varies substantially across architectures, topologies, and multipliers. ResNet-20 achieves full recovery across the entire multiplier range, whereas VGG architectures recover at moderate multipliers but fail irreversibly at aggressive ones for all topologies except Cluster MoE on VGG11_bn; on ViT-Small, Hard MoE outperforms Dense under aggressive approximation at equal normalized inference cost. These results pave the way for future approximate MoE hardware-software co-design strategies.

</details>


### [180] [Improving FMQA via Initial Training Data Design Considering Marginal Bit Coverage in One-Hot Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04825)
*Taiga Hayashi,Yuya Seki,Kotaro Terada,Yosuke Mukasa,Shuta Kikuchi,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对FMQA方法中初始采样可能遗漏某些二进制变量的激活问题，提出了通过拉丁超立方采样（LHS）和Sobol序列实现完全边际位覆盖的改进方法（LHS-FMQA和Sobol'-FMQA），并在17和32维机翼优化基准问题上取得了优于基线FMQA的平均最终巡航速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FMQA在基于独热编码处理整数或离散连续变量时，随机初始采样可能导致许多二进制变量在初始训练数据中从未被激活，使得对应的FM参数无法从观测响应中获得直接梯度更新，影响模型质量。

Method: 设计初始训练数据以实现完全边际位覆盖，确保每个经过独热编码的二进制变量至少有一次取值为1；采用两种空间填充采样方法：拉丁超立方采样（LHS）和Sobol序列，分别得到LHS-FMQA和Sobol'-FMQA。

Result: 在17维和32维设计变量的人力飞行器机翼形状优化基准问题上，两种提出的方法均获得了比基线FMQA更高的平均最终巡航速度，且在32维问题上的优势更为明显。

Conclusion: 通过空间填充采样实现完全边际位覆盖能有效改善FMQA在高维优化问题中的性能，特别是在变量维度较高时优势更显著。

Abstract: Factorization machine with quadratic-optimization annealing (FMQA) is a black-box optimization method that combines a factorization machine (FM) surrogate with QUBO-based search by an Ising machine. When FMQA is applied to integer or discretized continuous variables via one-hot encoding, uniform random initial sampling can leave many binary variables never active in the initial training data, and the corresponding FM parameters receive no direct gradient updates from the observed responses. We address this by designing the initial training data to achieve complete marginal bit coverage, namely, ensuring that every binary variable obtained by one-hot encoding takes the value one at least once. We use two space-filling sampling methods, Latin hypercube sampling (LHS) and the Sobol' sequence, yielding LHS-FMQA and Sobol'-FMQA. On the human-powered aircraft wing-shape optimization benchmark with 17 and 32 design variables, both proposed methods achieved numerically higher mean final cruising speeds than the baseline FMQA, with the advantage more pronounced on the 32-variable problem.

</details>


### [181] [Trustworthy Federated Label Distribution Learning under Annotation Quality Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04827)
*Junxiang Wu,Zhiqiang Kou,Hongwei Zeng,Wenke Huang,Biao Liu,Hanlin Gu,Yuheng Jia,Di Jiang,Yang Liu,Xin Geng,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedQual框架，通过质量感知的客户端训练（全局语义锚定校准低质量客户端）和可靠性感知的服务器聚合（基于有效信息而非样本量重加权），解决联邦标签分布学习中客户端标注质量异构导致的信任问题，在四个新基准上验证有效性并提供理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签分布学习依赖高质量标签分布，但获取成本高且数据隔离下客户端标注质量异构，传统基于样本量的聚合方法（如FedAvg）不可靠，亟需一种质量感知的联邦学习框架。

Method: 提出FedQual框架，包含两个耦合机制：(1) 质量自适应客户端训练——利用全局语义锚定校准低质量客户端，同时保持高质量客户端的自主性；(2) 可靠性感知服务器聚合——基于有效可靠信息而非原始样本量重新加权客户端贡献。

Result: 构建了四个Fed-LDL基准数据集（FER-LDL、FI-LDL、PIPAL-LDL、KADID-LDL），通过实验验证了FedQual的有效性，并提供了理论保证：异构标注质量下客户端特定校准严格优于任何统一校准。

Conclusion: FedQual通过质量感知机制有效解决了联邦标签分布学习中的标注异构问题，理论分析和实验均证明了其优越性。

Abstract: Label Distribution Learning (LDL) models supervision as an instance-wise probability distribution, enabling fine-grained learning under inherent ambiguity, but its success relies on high-fidelity label distributions that are costly to obtain and thus often noisy. Motivated by privacy-sensitive applications, we study Federated Label Distribution Learning (Fed-LDL), where data isolation further induces heterogeneous annotation quality across clients, making local updates unevenly reliable and breaking sample-size-based aggregation (e.g., FedAvg). To address this trust dilemma, we propose FedQual, a quality-aware Fed-LDL framework with two coupled mechanisms: (i) quality-adaptive client training guided by a global semantic anchor that calibrates low-quality clients while preserving high-quality autonomy, and (ii) reliability-aware server aggregation that reweights client contributions by effective reliable information rather than raw sample size. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct four new Fed-LDL benchmarks (FER-LDL, FI-LDL, PIPAL-LDL, and KADID-LDL) with controlled annotation quality disparity. We further provide a theoretical guarantee showing that under heterogeneous supervision quality, client-specific calibration is strictly better than any uniform calibration. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of FedQual.

</details>


### [182] [Concurrence of Symmetry Breaking and Nonlocality Phase Transitions in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04830)
*Yifan F. Zhang,Fangjun Hu,Guangkuo Liu,Mert Okyay,Xun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型生成动态中的相变现象，发现非局域性和对称性破缺两种临界时间在现代化扩散Transformer中几乎同时发生，首次在实践中统一了这两种相变概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成过程中存在一个关键时间窗口，其中发生相变。已有两种互补的解释：对称性破缺视角（轨迹分叉进入不同语义极小值）和非局域性视角（局部去噪失效）。此前缺乏对这两种相变是否同时发生的系统性研究。

Method: 通过评估扩散Transformer生成轨迹的动态和结果，分别测量非局域性临界时间和对称性破缺临界时间，分析两者的时间对齐关系。

Result: 观察到非局域性与对称性破缺临界时间在现代化扩散Transformer中几乎同时发生。

Conclusion: 首次在实践中统一了两种相变概念，提供了扩散模型何时以及为何依赖条件作用和全局去噪的具体诊断，为评估模型效率和指导架构与采样方案设计（避免不必要的计算）提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Diffusion models undergo a phase transition in a critical time window during generation dynamics, with two complementary diagnoses of criticality. The symmetry breaking picture views the critical window as when trajectories bifurcate into different semantic minima of the energy landscape, whereas the nonlocality picture views the critical window as when local denoising fails. We study whether two notions of such phase transitions are concurrent in modern diffusion transformers. By evaluating the dynamics and outcomes of the generation trajectory, we observe a near-simultaneous occurrence of the non-locality and symmetry breaking critical times. Our work is the first to unify the two notions of phase transitions in practice: it provides a concrete diagnostic for when and why diffusion models rely on conditioning and global denoising, enabling principled evaluation of model efficiency and guiding the design of architectures and sampling schemes that avoid unnecessary computation.

</details>


### [183] [Replay-Based Continual Learning for Physics-Informed Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04832)
*Yizheng Wang,Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk,Yinghua Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经算子在分布外数据上性能退化的问题，提出了一种基于回放的持续学习策略，应用于物理信息驱动的Transolver架构。该方法无需标签数据，通过蒸馏约束保留旧知识，并利用LoRA实现快速适应新数据。在三个物理问题（达西流、脑肿瘤超弹性、三周期极小曲面）上验证了其有效缓解灾难性遗忘、提升训练效率并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在分布内数据上表现优异，但在面对分布外数据时性能显著下降，缺乏有效的持续学习机制来适应新数据分布同时不遗忘旧知识。

Method: 基于Transolver架构的神经算子，结合回放式持续学习策略：1) 采用物理约束进行训练，无需标注数据；2) 对新OOD数据学习时，通过蒸馏约束回放少量旧数据以缓解灾难性遗忘；3) 引入迁移学习LoRA实现对新数据的快速适应。

Result: 在达西流、二维脑肿瘤超弹性、三维三周期极小曲面三个物理问题上，该方法有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，保持了快速适应新数据的能力，相比传统联合训练策略显著提升训练效率并降低额外内存和计算成本。

Conclusion: 提出的回放式持续学习策略能够有效解决神经算子面对分布外数据时的性能退化问题，在保持旧任务性能的同时快速适应新任务，且训练效率更高、资源消耗更少。

Abstract: Neural operators generally demonstrate strong predictive performance on in-distribution (ID) problems. However, a critical limitation of existing methods is their significant performance degradation when encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) data. To address this issue, this work introduces continual learning into physics-informed neural operators, with particular emphasis on neural operators built upon the Transolver architecture, and proposes a simple yet effective replay-based continual learning strategy. The proposed method is fully physics-informed and does not require labeled data, relying solely on input fields together with physical constraints for training. When new OOD data become available, a small number of past data are incorporated through a distillation-based constraint to preserve previously acquired knowledge and alleviate catastrophic forgetting. Meanwhile, a transfer learning LoRA is employed to enable rapid adaptation to the new data. The proposed framework is systematically validated on three representative physical problems, including the Darcy flow problem in fluid mechanics, a two-dimensional hyperelastic brain tumor problem in biomechanics, and a three-dimensional linear elastic Triply Periodic Minimal Surfaces problem in solid mechanics. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting on previously learned data while maintaining fast adaptability to new data. Compared with conventional joint training strategies, the proposed method significantly improves training efficiency while reducing additional memory usage and computational cost.

</details>


### [184] [Bridging Input Feature Spaces Towards Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04834)
*Moshe Eliasof,Krishna Sri Ipsit Mantri,Beatrice Bevilacqua,Bruno Ribeiro,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALL-IN方法，通过将节点特征投影到共享随机空间并利用协方差统计量构建表示，解决了跨数据集图学习中的输入特征不匹配问题，实现了无需重新训练即可迁移的输入无关图模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图学习缺乏统一的输入空间，不同数据集的输入特征在语义、数值范围和维度上均存在差异，导致图模型无法跨数据集泛化，限制了其作为基础模型的应用。

Method: 将节点特征投影到共享随机空间，基于协方差统计量构建节点表示，通过节点-协方差算子消除对原始特征空间的依赖，并在理论上证明该表示对输入特征排列和正交变换具有不变性。

Result: 在多种节点级和图级任务上，ALL-IN在未见数据集和新输入特征上取得强性能，无需架构调整或重新训练。

Conclusion: ALL-IN为输入无关、可迁移的图模型提供了有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Unlike vision and language domains, graph learning lacks a shared input space, as input features differ across graph datasets not only in semantics, but also in value ranges and dimensionality. This misalignment prevents graph models from generalizing across datasets, limiting their use as foundation models. In this work, we propose ALL-IN, a simple and theoretically grounded method that enables transferability across datasets with different input features. Our approach projects node features into a shared random space and constructs representations via covariance-based statistics, thus eliminating dependence on the original feature space. We show that the computed node-covariance operators and the resulting node representations are invariant in distribution to permutations of the input features. We further demonstrate that the expected operator exhibits invariance to general orthogonal transformations of the input features. Empirically, ALL-IN achieves strong performance across diverse node- and graph-level tasks on unseen datasets with new input features, without requiring architecture changes or retraining. These results point to a promising direction for input-agnostic, transferable graph models.

</details>


### [185] [Regime-Conditioned Evaluation in Multi-Context Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04895)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示迁移贝叶斯优化（transfer-BO）研究中存在"无免费排行榜"问题：98%的论文未将预算/搜索空间比（B/|A|）作为可控变量，导致结论不可泛化。作者提出便携式机制评分（PRS）来预测不同机制下的最优采集策略，并验证了该评分在79个条件下的有效性，最终提出RegimePlanner算法在16个HPO-B搜索空间上全胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有transfer-BO比较研究通常只报告平均处理效应（ATE），但实践者需要针对自身具体先验质量、预算比和指标的**条件处理效应（CATE）**。审计发现98%的论文从未将B/|A|作为控制轴进行变化，导致结论不可推广。

Method: 1) 提出**便携式机制评分PRS=(B/|A|)(1-ρ)**，其中ρ为先验秩相关系数；2) 在79个条件（化学、药物反应生物学、HPO）上建立分层模型验证PRS与采集策略优势的关系；3) 提出**RegimePlanner**算法，在线估计ρ并动态切换采集函数。

Result: 1) 分层模型β=0.50 (p=1.1e-9)，19%条件落在|优势|<0.01的等价区；2) 在5个已发表的反转案例中，PRS从比较前可观测数据预测获胜者；3) RegimePlanner在16个HPO-B搜索空间上B=100时全胜，在GDSC2上超过匹配的单上下文最优方案18%；4) 预注册预测总体准确率27/40=67.5%，EMA先验族内超90%。

Conclusion: 提出"无免费排行榜"命题解释为何无条件排名不稳定：当CATE跨机制变号时，报告的ATE成为基准混合比例的函数。实践协议要求报告B/|A|、ρ、K和指标。

Abstract: Published transfer-BO comparisons often estimate an average treatment effect of acquisition choice over hidden regime variables, while practitioners need the conditional effect for their specific prior quality, budget ratio, and metric. An audit of 40 transfer-BO papers from NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, AISTATS, UAI, TMLR, JMLR, and AutoML-Conf (2022-2025) finds that 98% never vary B/|A| as a controlled axis. On the same GDSC2 benchmark, changing only the budget reverses the ranking: at B=50, Greedy outperforms UCB by 0.050 Hit@1, while at B=100, UCB outperforms Greedy by 0.035. We capture this transition with the Portable Regime Score PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho), where rho is the prior rank correlation and can be estimated from pilot contexts before the main comparison. Across 79 conditions spanning chemistry, drug-response biology, and HPO, a hierarchical model gives beta=0.50 (p=1.1e-9), and 19% of conditions fall in an equivalence zone where |advantage|<0.01 Hit@1. In five published reversal cases, PRS predicts the winner from pre-comparison observables. A No-Free-Leaderboard proposition explains why unconditional rankings are unstable: when CATE changes sign across regimes, the reported ATE becomes a function of benchmark mixture. RegimePlanner, which estimates rho online and switches acquisition accordingly, wins all 16 HPO-B search spaces at B=100 and exceeds the matched {Greedy,UCB} per-context oracle on GDSC2 by 18%. Pre-registered predictions achieve 27/40=67.5% overall accuracy and above 90% within EMA prior families. The practical protocol is simple: report B/|A|, rho, K, and metric alongside any claimed acquisition advantage.

</details>


### [186] [A geometric relation of the error introduced by sampling a language model's output distribution to its internal state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04899)
*Albert F. Modenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现GPT风格的语言模型在预测概率分布分散于多个token的生成点上对单token变化敏感。作者将此敏感性视为几何性质，推导出仅依赖于token嵌入几何的$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式，并证明其曲率具有语义意义：在国际象棋推理任务中，曲率与现成指令调优模型的世界模型耦合，变换按棋盘区域聚类并尊重棋子重要性，表明token空间几何直接反映模型内部问题表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GPT风格语言模型对单token变化的敏感性的几何本质，揭示token空间几何与模型内部语义表征之间的关系。

Method: 从几何角度出发，推导仅依赖于token嵌入几何的$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式，并通过国际象棋推理任务验证其曲率的语义意义。

Result: 曲率与指令调优模型的世界模型耦合，变换按棋盘区域聚类并尊重棋子重要性。

Conclusion: token空间几何直接反映了模型内部如何表征问题，曲率具有语义意义。

Abstract: GPT-style language models are sensitive to single-token changes at generation points where the predicted probability distribution is spread across multiple tokens. Viewing this sensitivity as a geometric property, we derive an $\mathfrak{so}(n)$-valued 1-form that depends only on the geometry of the token embeddings. Despite this purely geometric origin, we show that its curvature is semantically meaningful: On chess reasoning tasks, the curvature couples to the world model of an off-the-shelf instruction-tuned model, with transformations clustering by board region and respecting piece importance. Our findings suggest that token space geometry directly reflects how models internally represent problems.

</details>


### [187] [Breaking the Quality-Privacy Tradeoff in Tabular Data Generation via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04911)
*Xinyan Han,Yan Lu,Xiaoyu Lin,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yuanrui Wang,Xuanyue Li,Wenchao Zou,Xingxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对表格数据合成在小数据场景下质量与隐私之间的权衡问题，提出DiffICL方法，将表格数据生成转化为上下文学习问题，利用预训练的结构先验从有限上下文中推断数据分布，从而同时提升数据质量和隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成模型在小数据场景下存在明显的质量-隐私权衡：提升数据质量往往导致训练样本记忆增强，从而削弱隐私保护。这一权衡源于小训练集使得数据特有生成模型难以区分可泛化结构与样本特有模式。

Method: 提出DiffICL，将表格数据生成构建为上下文学习问题。不从头拟合每个数据集，而是利用从大量数据集合中预训练的结构先验，从有限上下文中推断数据分布，而非记忆单个样本。

Result: 在14个真实数据集上的实验结果表明，DiffICL同时提升了数据质量和隐私保护，生成的合成数据能有效进行数据增强。

Conclusion: 通过更好的训练范式可以改善质量-隐私权衡问题。

Abstract: Tabular data synthesis aims to generate high-quality data while preserving privacy. However, we find that existing tabular generative models exhibit a clear tradeoff in the small-data regime: improving data quality typically comes at the cost of increased memorization of training samples, thereby weakening privacy protection. This tradeoff arises because small training sets make it difficult for dataset-specific generative models to distinguish generalizable structure from sample-specific patterns. To address this, we propose DiffICL, which formulates tabular data generation as an in-context learning problem. Instead of fitting each dataset from scratch,DiffICL leverages pretrained structural priors learned from a large collection of datasets, enabling it to infer data distributions from limited context rather than memorizing individual samples. We evaluate DiffICL on 14 real-world datasets. Results show that DiffICL improves both data quality and privacy, and generate synthetic data that provides effective data augmentation. Our findings suggest that the quality-privacy tradeoff can be improved through better training paradigms.

</details>


### [188] [Koopman Identification of Nonlinear Systems via Reservoir Liftings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04917)
*Weibin Gu,Chen Yang,Lu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-Koopman框架，将储层计算（Reservoir Computing）解释为一种有状态的有限维Koopman字典，通过回波状态属性（ESP）保证提升后Koopman逼近的适定性和良好数值条件，并提出基于相关性的谱半径选择算法来对齐储层记忆与主导系统时间尺度。实验表明，相比EDMD和Hankel方法，该方法在非线性动力学的重构精度与动态稳定性之间取得了更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Koopman算子理论学习非线性动力系统的可解线性表示，通常受限于字典选择、时间记忆编码和数值病态问题。现有方法难以同时兼顾表达能力和计算稳定性。

Method: 将储层计算（Reservoir Computing）范式引入Koopman框架，将储层解释为有状态的有限维Koopman字典，通过谱半径显式控制时间深度；利用回波状态属性（ESP）保证适定性和数值条件；提出基于相关性的谱半径选择算法，使储层记忆与系统主导时间尺度对齐。

Result: 在合成基准测试上，RC-Koopman相比EDMD和Hankel提升方法，在非线性动力学重构精度和动态稳定性之间实现了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: RC-Koopman框架有效克服了传统Koopman方法中字典选择、记忆编码和数值病态的问题，为非线性动力系统的可解线性表示学习提供了一种实用且稳定的方案。

Abstract: Learning tractable linear representations of nonlinear dynamical systems via Koopman operator theory is often hindered by dictionary selection, temporal memory encoding, and numerical ill-conditioning. Inspired by Reservoir Computing (RC) paradigm, this paper introduces the RC-Koopman framework, which interprets reservoir as a stateful, finite-dimensional Koopman dictionary whose temporal depth is explicitly controlled by its spectral radius. We show that the Echo State Property (ESP) guarantees well-posedness and favorable numerical conditioning of the lifted Koopman approximation. A correlation-based spectral radius selection algorithm aligns reservoir memory with dominant system timescales. Analysis reveals how the finite memory of the reservoir determines which Koopman eigenfunctions remain observable from the lifted features. Evaluation on synthetic benchmarks demonstrates that RC-Koopman achieves a favorable balance between reconstruction accuracy of the underlying nonlinear dynamics and dynamical stability, compared to Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) and Hankel-based lifting approaches. Code available at: https://github.com/NEAR-the-future/RC-Koopman.git

</details>


### [189] [Adaptive Inverted-Index Routing for Granular Mixtures-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04952)
*Klaus-Rudolf Kladny,Maximilian Mordig,Bernhard Schölkopf,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR-MoE提出了一种基于向量量化的两级路由架构，通过粗选（VQ码字分配构建候选专家集）和精选（仅对候选集计算精确路由分数）来近似top-k路由，在细粒度专家场景下提升性能并降低路由计算开销，可无缝替换标准路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE（大量小专家）虽能提升性能，但显著增加了路由计算成本，可能主导整体计算量。现有方法对专家参数施加结构约束，限制了灵活性。

Method: 采用两级路由：第一阶段通过向量量化将token分配给VQ码字，构建候选专家集（粗筛选）；第二阶段仅对候选集进行精确路由评分（精选）。该两阶段过程近似top-k路由，无需对专家参数或损失函数做任何修改。

Result: 在细粒度MoE设置下，AIR-MoE相比现有路由方法取得了更优的性能；同时推导了AIR-MoE的召回率下界，揭示了其工作机制。

Conclusion: AIR-MoE是一种高效、即插即用的路由方案，能在不牺牲模型灵活性的前提下解决细粒度MoE的路由计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models enable scalable transformer architectures by activating only a subset of experts per token. Recent evidence suggests that performance improves with increasingly granular experts, i.e., many small experts instead of a few large ones. However, this regime substantially increases routing cost, which can dominate computation. We introduce adaptive inverted-index routing for MoE (AIR-MoE), an inverted-index-inspired routing architecture based on vector quantization (VQ). In a first stage, AIR-MoE performs coarse shortlisting by assigning tokens to VQ codewords to construct a candidate set of experts. In a second stage, fine scoring computes exact routing scores restricted to this shortlist. This two-stage procedure approximates true top-k routing while avoiding full expert scoring and, in contrast to prior work, imposing no structural constraints on expert parameters. AIR-MoE serves as a drop-in replacement for standard routers and requires no modifications to the model architecture or loss function. We further provide a lower bound on the mass recall achieved by AIR-MoE that yields insights into its inner workings. Empirically, we demonstrate that AIR-MoE achieves improved performance compared to existing routing approaches in granular MoE settings.

</details>


### [190] [Order-based Rehearsal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04955)
*Yu-Xuan Tao,Tian-Zuo Wang,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于序结构的避免不良未来（AUF）决策方法，证明序结构足以识别决策影响，并通过信息论方法学习序结构，实验优于基于图结构的方法甚至达到真值图基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有避免不良未来（AUF）的排练学习方法依赖底层图结构，但从观测数据中学习图结构既困难又会产生显著估计误差，因此需要探索更轻量的结构表示来支持AUF决策。

Method: 1) 证明序结构足以从观测数据中识别决策影响；2) 提出信息论方法学习序结构，不限制结构函数形式或噪声分布类型；3) 构建基于序的采样器近似决策影响，结合替代目标最大化决策后成功概率，将AUF任务转化为可微优化问题。

Result: 序学习方法优于现有方法；基于序的AUF方法不仅超越基于学习图或学习序的方法，甚至匹敌或超越给定真实图的oracle基线。

Conclusion: 学习完整图结构并非AUF决策的必要条件，序结构作为一种更弱的结构信息足以支持有效的AUF决策，且更易学习。

Abstract: When a machine learning (ML) model forecasts an undesired event, one often seeks a decision to avoid it, known as the avoiding undesired future (AUF) problem. Many rehearsal learning methods have been proposed for AUF, but they rely on an underlying graph structure; learning such a graph from observational data is challenging and can incur substantial estimation error. In this work, we demonstrate that the order structure can be sufficient for AUF decision-making, and propose the first order-based rehearsal learning method. Although an order is less informative than a graph, it can be sufficient to identify the influence of decisions from observational data, suggesting that learning the entire graph is not always necessary. To learn the order, we develop an information-theoretic method that imposes no restrictions on the form of structural functions or the type of noise distributions. For AUF decision-making, we construct an order-based sampler to approximate the influence of decisions and, combined with a surrogate objective for maximizing the post-decision success probability, reduce the AUF task to a differentiable optimization problem. Experiments show that our order learning method outperforms existing methods, and that our AUF approach not only surpasses methods relying on learned graphs or learned orders, but also matches or even exceeds oracle baselines that are given the true graph.

</details>


### [191] [KernelBench-X: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04956)
*Han Wang,Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Haoxu Wang,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelBench-X通过176个任务、15个类别的系统评估，揭示了LLM生成Triton内核的三个关键发现：任务结构比方法设计更能决定正确性（类别解释方差9.4% vs 方法3.3%）；迭代改进提升编译率（52.3%→68.8%）但降低性能（加速比1.58×→1.44×）；46.6%的正确内核慢于PyTorch基线，量化任务完全失败（0/30成功）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based Triton kernel generation capability breaks down at certain tasks, yet the fundamental empirical question of where and why this breakdown occurs remains unanswered. This paper aims to systematically answer this question through category-aware evaluation.

Method: 提出KernelBench-X基准，包含176个任务、15个类别，对五种代表性方法进行系统比较，评估语义正确性和硬件效率，并通过方差分解分析任务类别与方法设计的贡献。

Result: (1) 任务结构决定正确性：类别解释方差9.4%，远超方法的3.3%；72%的Fusion任务全方法失败，Math任务一致成功。(2) 迭代改进提升编译率（52.3%→68.8%）但降低平均加速比（1.58×→1.44×），新挽救的内核性能显著低于持续正确的内核（1.16× vs 1.58×）。(3) 46.6%的正确内核慢于PyTorch eager基线，跨硬件加速比方差达21.4×；量化任务编译率非零但0/30成功，揭示对数值计算合约的系统性误解。

Conclusion: 未来进展依赖于处理全局协调、显式建模数值精度以及将硬件效率纳入生成过程。

Abstract: LLM-based Triton kernel generation has attracted significant interest, yet a fundamental empirical question remains unanswered: where does this capability break down, and why? We present KernelBench-X, a benchmark designed to answer this question through category-aware evaluation of correctness and hardware efficiency across 176 tasks in 15 categories. Our systematic comparison of five representative methods yields three main findings. First, task structure determines correctness more than method design. Category explains nearly three times more variance in semantic correctness than method (9.4% vs 3.3% explained deviance), and 72% of Fusion tasks fail across all five methods while Math tasks are solved consistently. Second, iterative refinement improves correctness, but not performance. Across GEAK iterations, compile rate rises from 52.3% to 68.8% while average speedup declines from $1.58\times$ to $1.44\times$; newly rescued kernels consistently underperform persistently correct ones ($1.16\times$ vs $1.58\times$ speedup in round~0$\to$1). Third, correctness does not imply efficiency. 46.6% of correct kernels are slower than the PyTorch eager baseline, and cross-hardware speedup variance reaches $21.4\times$. Besides, quantization remains completely unsolved (0/30 successes) despite non-trivial compilation rates, revealing systematic misunderstanding of numerical computation contracts rather than surface-level syntax errors. These findings suggest that future progress depends on handling global coordination, explicitly modeling numerical precision, and incorporating hardware efficiency into generation. The code is available at https://github.com/BonnieW05/KernelBenchX

</details>


### [192] [Delving into Non-Exchangeability for Conformal Prediction in Graph-Structured Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04957)
*Ruichao Guo,Xingyao Han,Luo Wenshui,Zhe Liu,Chen Gong,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图结构多元时间序列的点预测，提出频谱图条件可交换性（SGCE）概念，并基于此设计SCALE方法（图小波变换+自适应门控），在高频成分上应用共形预测，实现有效的不确定性量化。实验表明SCALE在保持有效覆盖的同时，持续改善覆盖-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构多元时间序列中，节点间的跨节点耦合破坏了共形预测所需的可交换性假设，导致传统CP方法不可靠。现有工作缺乏对此类数据的不确定性量化研究。

Method: 提出SGCE概念，将可交换的高频成分条件于保留全局趋势的低频成分上；基于SGCE设计SCALE方法，利用图小波变换分解低/高频成分，通过低频嵌入的自适应门控对高频残差进行共形化。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上，SCALE不仅达到有效的覆盖，而且持续优于最先进的CP方法在覆盖-效率权衡上的表现。

Conclusion: 频谱域分解能有效处理图时间序列中节点耦合对CP的干扰，SGCE为图结构时序数据的不确定性量化提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Point forecasting for graph-structured multivariate time series is a fundamental problem, but rigorous uncertainty quantification for such predictions is still underexplored. Conformal prediction (CP) offers uncertainty estimation with a solid coverage guarantee under the exchangeability assumption, which requires the joint data distribution to be unchanged under permutation. However, in graph-structured time series, inherent cross-node coupling can violate the exchangeability condition, making direct application of CP unreliable. Inspired by the spectral graph theory, such coupling resides in global trends and can be characterized by the low-frequency components, while high-frequency components are nearly exchangeable. Therefore, we propose a novel concept named Spectral Graph Conditional Exchangeability (SGCE), which conditions exchangeable high-frequency components on low-frequency ones to preserve global trends and enable effective CP in the spectral domain. Based on SGCE, we further propose Spectral Conformal prediction via wAveLEt transform (SCALE). SCALE uses graph wavelets to decompose low/high-frequency components and conformalizes high-frequency residuals via adaptive gating over a low-frequency embedding. Experimental results on real-world traffic datasets show that SCALE not only achieves valid coverage but also consistently improves the coverage-efficiency trade-off over the state-of-the-art CP methods.

</details>


### [193] [DualTCN: A Physics-Constrained Temporal Convolutional Network for 2 Time-Domain Marine CSEM Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04997)
*Khaled Ahmed,Ghada Omar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualTCN是一个用于海洋可控源电磁（MCSEM）瞬态数据反演的深度学习框架。它通过TCN编码器、晚时分支和辅助海底深度头，直接回归四个地层参数（σ₁, σ₂, d₁, d₂），并以可微soft-step解码器重构电导率-深度剖面。该框架在379K参数下实现25.3%的损失降低，反演速度3.5ms/样本（A100 GPU），对噪声具有高鲁棒性，在±2%随机振幅误差下平均R²为0.858（无增强时为0.363）。相比传统局部优化方法，DualTCN以低21000倍的计算成本实现平均R²=0.877（对比0.129-0.439）。框架还通过MC Dropout实现不确定性量化，但短偏移距（200m）下d₂存在覆盖不足问题（PICP90=0.572），可通过温度缩放或分裂共形预测缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MCSEM数据反演依赖局部优化方法（如Levenberg-Marquardt、L-BFGS-B），计算成本高且易陷入局部最优。本文旨在开发首个基于深度学习的端到端反演框架，实现高精度、快速且鲁棒的MCSEM瞬态数据反演，并集成不确定性量化。

Method: 提出DualTCN框架：① 采用TCN编码器提取时序特征；② 设计晚时分支和辅助海底深度头增强参数回归能力；③ 使用可微soft-step解码器从四个回归参数（σ₁, σ₂, d₁, d₂）重构连续电导率-深度剖面；④ 通过课程式振幅增强提升噪声鲁棒性；⑤ 集成MC Dropout进行不确定性量化，并引入温度缩放和分裂共形预测校准。

Result: ① 损失降低25.3%，σ₂预测R²=0.898，反演速度3.5ms/样本；② ±2%振幅噪声下，有/无增强的平均R²分别为0.858和0.363；③ 三层扩展模型中基底电导率R²≈0.88，薄层分辨率受限（R²≈0.23）；④ 对比传统方法：平均R²=0.877 vs 0.129-0.439，计算成本降低21000倍；⑤ 不确定性量化：σ₁的PICP90=0.944，d₂的PICP90=0.572（短偏移距限制）。

Conclusion: DualTCN作为首个用于MCSEM瞬态数据反演的深度学习框架，在精度、速度和鲁棒性上均显著超越传统优化方法，且具备不确定性量化能力。尽管薄层分辨率和短偏移距下的覆盖不足是物理固有的局限性，但后处理校准策略可部分缓解。该框架为地球物理电磁反演提供了高效且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: DualTCN is the first deep-learning framework for inverting time-domain marine controlled-source electromagnetic (MCSEM) transient data. Moving away from traditional subsurface discretization, the framework regresses four earth-model parameters -- $σ_1$, $σ_2$, $d_1$, $d_2$ -- and reconstructs conductivity-depth profiles using a differentiable soft-step decoder. The optimized architecture (379K parameters) features a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) encoder paired with a late-time branch and an auxiliary seafloor-depth head. This design achieves a 25.3\% loss reduction over baseline models, with high predictive accuracy ($R^2 = 0.898$ for $σ_2$) and an inversion speed of 3.5~ms per sample on an A100 GPU.
  The framework demonstrates high robustness to noise through curriculum-based amplitude augmentation, maintaining a mean $\bar{R}^2$ of 0.858 at $\pm2\%$ random amplitude error, compared to $0.363$ without augmentation. DualTCN generalizes effectively to three-layer extensions (seawater/resistive layer/basement), accurately resolving basement conductivity ($R^2 \approx 0.88$), though thin-layer resolution remains a physical limitation ($R^2 \approx 0.23$).
  In comparative benchmarks, DualTCN significantly outperforms traditional local optimization methods like Levenberg-Marquardt and L-BFGS-B, yielding a mean $\bar{R}^2 = 0.877$ versus 0.129-0.439 for multi-start baselines, while operating at up to 21,000$\times$ lower computational cost. Finally, the framework incorporates uncertainty quantification via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout. While well-calibrated for $σ_1$ (PICP90 = 0.944), inherent signal limitations at short offsets (200m) lead to under-coverage for $d_2$ (PICP90 = 0.572), which can be mitigated through post-hoc temperature scaling or split conformal prediction.

</details>


### [194] [Learned Neighbor Trust for Collaborative Deployment in Model-Agnostic Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05009)
*Michael Lanier,Luise Ge,Sastry Kompella,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LNTrust方法，让分布式去中心化蒸馏中的每个节点学习一个轻量信任函数，在训练时控制辅助蒸馏，在推理时定义集成，实现训练到部署的无缝协作迁移，显著提升异构设备上的部署精度并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化蒸馏方法仅在训练时协调，推理时各节点独立部署，忽略了推理时更强邻居仍可用的事实。在IoT等异构、数据稀缺且分布偏差的场景中，这种设计是不完备的。

Method: 提出LNTrust：在无服务器、模型无关的协议下，节点间仅交换查询和软预测。每个节点从本地验证证据中学习一个轻量信任函数，该函数在训练时控制辅助蒸馏的门控，在推理时定义部署集成，使训练时学到的协作直接迁移到部署阶段。

Result: 在多种数据集和拓扑结构上，LNTrust相比最强的仅输出基线大幅提升部署精度，同时通信量显著低于先前方法。

Conclusion: 节点应训练使其预测在部署时能良好组合，并学习信任谁。LNTrust通过信任函数的训练-推理一致性机制，有效解决了异构节点协作蒸馏中的部署泛化问题。

Abstract: Many decentralized distillation methods are designed around training-time coordination, yet deploy each node in isolation even when more capable neighbors remain available at inference time. This is an incomplete objective for settings such as IoT, where devices are heterogeneous, data is scarce and skewed, and a node's strongest neighbors may far exceed its own local capacity. We study how nodes should train so that their predictions compose well at deployment, and how each node should learn whom to trust. Under a server-free, model-agnostic protocol where nodes exchange only queries and soft predictions, we propose Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust) wherein each node learns a compact trust function over its neighborhood from local validation evidence. This trust function gates auxiliary distillation during training and defines a deployment ensemble at inference, so that collaboration learned during training transfers directly to deployment. Across datasets and topologies, LNTrust improves deployed accuracy over the strongest output-only baseline by large margins while using significantly less communication than previous methods.

</details>


### [195] [Graph-SND: Sparse Aggregation for Behavioral Diversity in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05020)
*Shawn Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-SND，通过用任意图G上的加权平均替代完整图平均，将多智能体强化学习中的系统神经多样性（SND）度量计算复杂度从O(n²)降低到O(|E|)。实验证明在保持SND语义不变的前提下，稀疏图（如伯努利-0.1采样）可实现约10倍加速且偏差极小，随机d-正则扩展图在Θ(n log n)边数下SND比值稳定在[0.9987, 1.0013]。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始SND度量每次调用需计算所有O(n²)智能体对的成对距离，计算复杂度随团队规模二次增长，成为大规模多智能体强化学习中的性能瓶颈。作者旨在消除这一聚合瓶颈而不改变度量的语义。

Method: 将完整图K_n上的平均替换为任意图G边上的加权平均，定义三种机制：完整图恢复原始SND；固定稀疏图实现局部多样性度量；随机边采样构造无偏Horvitz-Thompson估计器。理论分析包括：稀疏图的转发索引失真界（扩展图）、低秩距离结构的谱改进、随机d-正则图的Õ(D_max/√n)概率界。

Result: VMAS上验证了恢复性、无偏性、集中性和墙钟加速：n=100的PPO运行中伯努利-0.1采样加速约10倍；GPU时序符合O(n²)/|E|理论加速比；随机d-正则扩展图SND比值在[0.9987, 1.0013]；n=50的DiCo多样性控制中伯努利-0.1采样保持设定点跟踪，每调用成本降低约9.5倍。

Conclusion: SND的聚合瓶颈可以在不改变度量语义的情况下被消除，Graph-SND作为即插即用的稀疏替代方案，能够扩展到完整图SND无法企及的规模，同时支持被动测量和闭环多样性控制。

Abstract: System Neural Diversity (SND) measures behavioral heterogeneity in multi-agent reinforcement learning by averaging pairwise distances over all $\binom{n}{2}$ agent pairs, making each call quadratic in team size. We introduce Graph-SND, which replaces this complete-graph average with a weighted average over the edges of an arbitrary graph $G$. Three regimes follow: $G=K_n$ recovers SND exactly; a fixed sparse $G$ defines a localized diversity measure at $O(|E|)$ cost; and random edge samples yield an unbiased Horvitz-Thompson estimator and a normalized sample mean with $O(1/\sqrt{m})$ concentration in the sampled edge count $m$. For fixed sparse graphs we prove forwarding-index distortion bounds for expanders and a spectral refinement under low-rank distance structure; for random $d$-regular graphs we prove an unconditional probabilistic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(D_{\max}/\sqrt{n})$ bound. On VMAS we verify recovery, unbiasedness, concentration, and wall-clock scaling, with a PettingZoo TVD panel checking non-Gaussian transfer. In a 500-iteration $n=100$ PPO run, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND tracks full SND while reducing per-call metric time by about $10\times$, and frozen-policy GPU timing up to $n=500$ follows the predicted $\binom{n}{2}/|E|$ speedup. Random $d$-regular expanders empirically achieve $\mathrm{SND}_{G}^{\mathrm{u}}/\mathrm{SND} \in [0.9987, 1.0013]$ at $Θ(n \log n)$ edges. In DiCo diversity control at $n=50$, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND preserves set-point tracking with paired reward differences indistinguishable from zero across nine matched cells while cutting per-call metric cost by ${\sim}9.5\times$. Together, these results show that the SND aggregation bottleneck can be removed without changing the metric's semantics, yielding a drop-in sparse alternative that scales beyond complete-graph SND and supports both passive measurement and closed-loop diversity control.

</details>


### [196] [CuBridge: An LLM-Based Framework for Understanding and Reconstructing High-Performance Attention Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05023)
*Xing Ma,Yangjie Zhou,Wu Sun,Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yun Lin,Shixuan Sun,Minyi Guo,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CuBridge是一个基于LLM的框架，通过"提升-转换-降低"工作流，从专家编写的CUDA注意力核出发，将其适配到不同注意力变体和GPU平台，生成正确且高性能的CUDA代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制实现存在性能与灵活性之间的权衡：专家手写核效率高但难以适配，框架和编译器灵活但性能不足，而LLM生成核的方法在不稳定性和性能差距问题。

Method: CuBridge采用结构化三阶段工作流：1) 提升(lift)：将专家编写的CUDA注意力核转化为可执行的中间表示(IR)，显式化执行编排并抽象底层语法；2) 转换(transfer)：根据用户提供的PyTorch规范生成并验证目标IR程序；3) 降低(lower)：通过参考引导将IR重构为优化的CUDA代码。

Result: 在多种注意力变体和GPU平台上，CuBridge一致地生成正确的核函数，并且在性能上显著优于通用框架、编译器方法和基于LLM的先前方法。

Conclusion: CuBridge证明了结合专家知识与LLM的结构化工作流可以有效弥合注意力机制实现的性能与灵活性鸿沟，为GPU核的自动化适配提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Efficient CUDA implementations of attention mechanisms are critical to modern deep learning systems, yet supporting diverse and evolving attention variants remains challenging. Existing frameworks and compilers trade performance for flexibility, while expert-written kernels achieve high efficiency but are difficult to adapt. Recent work explores large language models (LLMs) for GPU kernel generation, but prior studies report unstable correctness and significant performance gaps for complex operators such as attention.
  We present CuBridge, an LLM-based framework that adapts expert-written attention kernels through a structured lift-transfer-lower workflow. CuBridge starts from expert-written CUDA attention kernels and lifts them into an executable intermediate representation that makes execution orchestration explicit while abstracting low-level CUDA syntax. Given a user-provided PyTorch specification, CuBridge generates and verifies a target IR program, then reconstructs optimized CUDA code via reference-guided lowering. Across diverse attention variants and GPU platforms, CuBridge consistently produces correct kernels and substantially outperforms general frameworks, compiler-based approaches, and prior LLM-based methods.

</details>


### [197] [Full-chip CMP modelling based on Fully Convolutional Network leveraging White Light Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05062)
*Jules Exbrayat,Renan Bouis,Elie Sezestre,Viorel Balan,Arnaud Cornelis,Damien Hebras,Catherine Euvrard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合白光干涉仪（WLI）和原子力显微镜（AFM）两种表面分析技术，训练基于CNN的深度学习模型，以纳米级精度预测全芯片CMP后的纳米形貌，从而加速IC制造中的可制造性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在IC行业中，上市时间至关重要，加快版图可制造性验证十分必要。CMP受版图依赖效应影响显著，现有DSH模型校准耗时且硬件资源需求高，因此需要一种更高效、准确的CMP预测模型。

Method: 提出结合WLI和AFM两种表面分析技术的优势，训练基于CNN的深度学习模型。模型采用两步流水线架构，分别对两种技术的数据进行独立训练，最终生成详细的全芯片CMP模型。

Result: 该深度学习模型能够以纳米级精度预测全芯片后CMP纳米形貌，实现了全芯片级别的精细预测。

Conclusion: 通过结合WLI和AFM技术并利用深度学习，可以在CMP前准确预测表面形貌，减少流片失败风险，加速设计周期，降低制造成本。

Abstract: As time-to-market is crucial in the Integrated Circuit (IC) industry, speeding up layout manufacturability verifi-cation is essential. Chemical-Mechanical Polishing (CMP) plays a vital role in IC fabrication but is significantly influenced by Layout-Dependent Effects (LDE). An accurate and efficient CMP model enables design teams to correct surface unevenness before fabrication, reducing costs and accelerating the design phase. However, existing models often rely on Density Step Height (DSH) modeling, which is time-consuming for calibration and requires substantial hardware resources for fine-grained predictions. In this paper, we propose combining the advantages of two surface analysis techniques, White Light Interfer-ometry (WLI) and Atomic Force Microscopy (AFM), to train a deep learning model. This model aims to predict full-chip post-CMP nanotopography with nanometer-scale accuracy. Our deep learning model is based on a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and follows a two-step pipeline. The model is trained on each technique separately, resulting in a detailed full-chip CMP model.

</details>


### [198] [Order Matters: Improving Domain Adaptation by Reordering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05084)
*Andrea Napoli,Paul White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORDERED是一种新颖的无偏随机方差缩减技术，通过优化训练数据的采样顺序来降低域差异估计误差，从而提升无监督域自适应（UDA）的性能。实验表明该方法在方差缩减和目标域准确率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督域自适应中，域差异估计在随机设置下存在高方差问题，这抑制了理论优势的发挥。现有方法未能有效降低估计误差。

Method: 提出ORDERED方法，通过优化训练数据的采样顺序来减少域差异的随机估计误差。针对相关对齐（CORAL）和最大均值差异（MMD）两种损失函数，将估计误差建模为采样顺序的函数，并设计了实用优化算法。

Result: 模拟实验显示了相比相关方法更低的方差；在两项域迁移图像分类基准上，目标域准确率得到提升。

Conclusion: ORDERED通过数据采样顺序优化有效降低了域差异估计的方差，验证了其在UDA任务中的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Domain shift remains a key challenge in deploying machine learning models to the real world. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) aims to address this by minimising domain discrepancy during training, but the discrepancy estimates suffer from high variance in stochastic settings, which can stifle the theoretical benefits of the method. This paper proposes Optimal Reordering of Data for Error-Reduced Estimation of Discrepancy (ORDERED), a novel unbiased stochastic variance reduction technique which reduces the discrepancy estimation error by optimising the order in which the training data are sampled. We consider two specific domain discrepancy losses (correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy), formulate their stochastic estimation error as a function of the data sampling order, and propose a practical optimisation algorithm. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on two domain shift image classification benchmarks show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [199] [Gated Multimodal Learning for Interpretable Property Energy Performance Prediction and Retrofit Scenario Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05088)
*Yunfei Bai,Aaron Tesfa Tsion,Raul Rosales,Barbara Shollock,Wei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种门控多模态模型，通过整合EPC表格变量、评估师文本和GIS空间特征来预测建筑能效与环境影响评分，在西敏寺案例中实现MAE 4.39和R² 0.75以上，并展示了其在改造筛查与净零住房转型中的可扩展应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅建筑脱碳是实现可持续城市的关键，但现有EPC依赖现场检查，难以支撑城市尺度的及时评估。需要一种可扩展的方法来预测能效评分，以支持法规制定和改造规划。

Method: 构建门控多模态模型，融合三类特征：EPC表格变量、评估师自由文本、GIS空间特征（足迹几何、高度、面积、朝向）。采用样本级门控学习属性特定的模态权重，辅以辅助频带分类头稳定训练。

Result: 西敏寺案例中SAP和EI评分预测MAE分别为4.03和4.76，R²为0.757和0.748；多模态融合优于单模态和双模态基线；门控权重显示对评估师文本依赖强；SHAP突出主燃料、建筑形式和建造年代；文本遮挡优先关注屋顶和墙体字段；空间归因以高度和足迹面积为主。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展地提供建筑级改造证据，支持改造筛选、干预优先级排序和净零住房转型，并展示了墙体保温、屋顶保温和窗户玻璃升级等改造情景下的效果预测能力。

Abstract: Achieving resilient and sustainable cities requires scalable approaches to decarbonising residential buildings, which account for about 20% of UK greenhouse gas emissions and 25% of energy-related emissions in the European Union. Energy Performance Certificates (EPCs) support regulation and retrofit planning, but their reliance on on-site inspections limits timely city-scale assessment. This study introduces a gated multimodal model to predict Standard Assessment Procedure (SAP) energy efficiency and Environmental Impact (EI) scores by integrating EPC tabular variables, assessor-written free text, and Geographic Information System (GIS)-derived spatial features describing footprint geometry, height, area, and orientation. Sample-wise gating learns property-specific modality weights, while an auxiliary band classification head stabilises training. In a Westminster, London case study, the model predicts SAP and EI scores with MAEs of 4.03 and 4.76 points and R2 values of 0.757 and 0.748, respectively, achieving a mean MAE of 4.39. Ablation results show that full multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal and bimodal baselines for both score prediction and band-level classification. Interpretability analyses provide decision-relevant evidence: gating weights indicate strong reliance on assessor text; SHAP highlights main fuel, built form, and construction age band; text occlusion prioritises roof and wall fields; and spatial attribution is dominated by height and footprint area, with sensitivity to footprint shape. The validated framework is further applied to retrofit scenarios for wall insulation, roof insulation, and window glazing upgrades, indicating projected improvements in SAP, EI, annual energy cost, and equivalent CO2 emissions. Overall, the framework provides scalable property-level evidence for retrofit screening, intervention prioritisation, and net-zero housing transitions.

</details>


### [200] [Unified Framework of Distributional Regret in Multi-Armed Bandits and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05102)
*Harin Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架研究随机多臂老虎机和情节强化学习中的遗憾分布，提出一种带有特定探索奖励的UCBVI风格算法，并推导出通用分布遗憾界，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári关于多臂老虎机遗憾分布的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾分析大多关注期望值，缺乏对遗憾完整分布特征的刻画，尤其是不同置信水平下尾部行为的统一理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供跨所有置信水平的概率保证。

Method: 提出UCBVI-style算法，采用探索奖励项 $\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$（N为访问计数，$c_{1,k},c_{2,k}$为用户指定参数），并推导任意参数序列下的通用间隙无关和间隙相关分布遗憾界。

Result: 在极小极大和实例依赖两种机制下达到期望遗憾与分布遗憾之间的最优权衡。特别地，对于A臂T时域的多臂老虎机，得到分布遗憾界 $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/\delta))$，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文建立了遗憾分布的完整理论框架，揭示了参数如何控制期望表现、尾部风险和实例依赖行为之间的权衡，为随机多臂老虎机和情节强化学习提供了统一的分布遗憾分析。

Abstract: We study the distribution of regret in stochastic multi-armed bandits and episodic reinforcement learning through a unified framework. We formalize a distributional regret bound as a probabilistic guarantee that holds uniformly over all confidence levels $δ\in (0,1]$, thereby characterizing the regret distribution across the full range of $δ$. We present a simple UCBVI-style algorithm with exploration bonus $\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$, where $N$ denotes the visit count and $(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})$ are user-specified parameters. For arbitrary parameter sequences, we derive general gap-independent and gap-dependent distributional regret bounds, yielding a principled characterization of how the parameters control the trade-off between expected performance, tail risk, and instance-dependent behavior. In particular, our bounds achieve optimal trade-offs between expected and distributional regret in both minimax and instance-dependent regimes. As a special case, for multi-armed bandits with $A$ arms and horizon $T$, we obtain a distributional regret bound of order $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/δ))$, confirming the conjecture of Lattimore & Szepesvári (2020, Section 17.1) for the first time.

</details>


### [201] [Rollout Pass-Rate Control: Steering Binary-Reward RL Toward Its Most Informative Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05112)
*Tianshu Zhu,Wenyu Zhang,Xiaoying Zuo,Lun Tian,Haotian Zhao,Yucheng Zeng,Jingnan Gu,Daxiang Dong,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prefix Sampling (PS) 方法，通过重放轨迹前缀将采样组的通过率控制在50%这一信息最丰富的工作点，从而提升SWE-bench风格智能体强化学习的训练效率，在Qwen3-14B和32B上分别取得2.01倍和1.55倍的端到端加速，并提升SWE-bench Verified峰值从0.273到0.295。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench风格智能体强化学习中，基于二元奖励的智能体强化学习在有状态轨迹上浪费了大量计算资源，原因是采样组的通过率偏斜导致对比信号弱。本文旨在解决这一效率瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将效率问题建模为通过率控制问题，理论证明50%通过率是信息最丰富的工作点（最大化奖励熵、组过滤存活概率、RLOO优势能量及正负对比结构）。2) 提出Prefix Sampling（PS）：重放轨迹前缀来引导偏斜组向50%通过率靠拢——对多数失败组使用成功前缀作为"头启动"，对多数通过组使用失败前缀作为" handicap"。3) 在有状态智能体环境中，通过重放重建前缀状态，重放token不计入损失，优化仅限于当前策略生成的续接部分。

Result: 在SWE-bench风格智能体RL上：Qwen3-14B端到端加速2.01倍，Qwen3-32B加速1.55倍；14B的SWE-bench Verified峰值从0.273提升至0.295。在AIME 2025数学推理实验上验证了相同的通过率控制模式。

Conclusion: 通过率控制是一个有效的效率优化原则，Prefix Sampling通过重放前缀将采样组引导至最优信息点，显著加速智能体强化学习训练，同时保持或提升最终性能。

Abstract: SWE-bench-style agentic reinforcement learning relies on expensive stateful trajectories, yet substantial compute is wasted on sampled rollout groups with skewed pass rates, where binary rewards provide a weak contrastive signal. We frame this inefficiency as a pass-rate control problem and show that a 50% pass rate is the most informative operating point: it maximizes reward entropy, the probability of surviving group filtering, RLOO advantage energy under GRPO, and success--failure contrastive structure. Guided by this principle, we propose Prefix Sampling (PS), which replays trajectory prefixes to steer skewed groups toward this regime: successful prefixes serve as head starts for mostly failing groups, while failing prefixes serve as handicaps for mostly passing groups. In stateful agent environments, prefix states are reconstructed through replay while replayed tokens are excluded from the loss, restricting optimization to continuations generated by the current policy. On SWE-bench-style agentic RL, PS delivers end-to-end wall-clock speedups of 2.01x on Qwen3-14B and 1.55x on Qwen3-32B while preserving or improving final verified performance. For 14B, the SWE-bench Verified peak rises from the baseline peak of 0.273 to 0.295 under PS. Additional mathematical reasoning experiments on AIME 2025 show the same pass-rate control pattern and decompose the gains into replay, bidirectional coverage, and adaptive control.

</details>


### [202] [How Long Does Infinite Width Last? Signal Propagation in Long-Range Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05113)
*Mariia Seleznova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性递归模型在有限宽度下的信号传播，推导了精确的有限宽度公式，并识别出三个联合深度-宽度缩放区间：亚临界(t=o(√n))、临界(t~c√n)和超临界(t≫√n)，揭示了无限宽度理论在长程线性递归中失效的精确深度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信号传播理论主要依赖无限宽度极限，但在递归模型中，递归深度t与宽度n同时增长时，该近似何时保持准确尚不清楚。这对处理长输入序列的现代递归序列模型尤为重要。

Method: 在复高斯初始化下，推导了线性递归中隐藏状态信号能量的精确有限宽度公式，并据此识别出联合深度-宽度缩放区间。

Result: 确定了三个缩放区间：亚临界(t=o(√n))无限宽度近似有效；临界(t~c√n)出现显著偏差且存在非平凡联合缩放极限；超临界(t≫√n)有限宽度效应占主导。标准初始化方案（如Glorot）在超临界区间变得不稳定。

Conclusion: 有限宽度效应在递归模型中的积累速度比前馈模型更快，导致定性地不同的信号传播行为。研究结果精确指出了无限宽度理论在长程线性递归中失效的深度尺度。

Abstract: We study signal propagation in linear recurrent models at finite width. While existing signal propagation theory relies predominantly on the infinite-width limit, it remains unclear for how long that approximation remains accurate when recurrent depth $t$ grows jointly with width $n$. This question is especially relevant for modern recurrent sequence models, whose natural operating regime involves long input sequences, i.e., large $t$. We derive exact finite-width formulas for the hidden state signal energies in linear recurrences under complex Gaussian initialization. Using these formulas, we identify the joint depth-width scaling regimes that govern signal propagation: (i) a subcritical regime $t=o(\sqrt n)$, in which the infinite-width approximation remains valid; (ii) a critical regime $t\sim c\sqrt n$, in which non-negligible deviations from infinite-width predictions appear and a nontrivial joint scaling limit emerges; and (iii) a supercritical regime $t\gg \sqrt n$, in which finite-width effects dominate. Thus, our results pinpoint the precise recurrent depth scale at which infinite-width theory breaks down in long-range linear recurrences. In turn, this shows when standard initialization schemes, such as Glorot, become unstable. More broadly, our results demonstrate that finite-width effects accumulate more rapidly with depth in recurrent models than in feedforward ones, leading to qualitatively different signal propagation behavior.

</details>


### [203] [On the Hardness of Junking LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05116)
*Marco Rando,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型中的"自然后门"问题，形式化为"junking问题"——寻找最大化有害响应目标前缀生成概率的令牌序列。通过贪心随机搜索方法发现：该问题比标准越狱攻击更难，但简单策略仍能以高成功率解决；且发现的令牌序列位于模型分布的低概率区域，支持其隐含产生于训练过程的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期观察到即使没有对抗性提示，仅靠优化的令牌序列也能诱发有害行为，暗示存在自然后门（训练中自然出现的触发不安全输出的令牌序列）。但该设置尚未被充分探索，尤其缺乏对寻找自然后门困难程度的评估。

Method: 将问题形式化为junking问题——寻找最大化有害响应目标前缀生成概率的令牌序列；提出贪心随机搜索方法。

Result: junking问题比标准越狱攻击更难，表明提示中的语义信息对攻击性能至关重要；但简单策略仍能以高成功率解决，说明自然后门存在且易于恢复；发现的令牌序列位于模型分布的低概率区域。

Conclusion: 该工作为自然后门的存在和可恢复性提供了初步概念验证，支持其隐含产生于训练过程的假设。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to be vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, which typically rely on carefully designed prompts containing explicit semantic structure. These attacks generally operate by fixing an adversarial instruction and optimizing small adversarial components (e.g., suffixes or prefixes). In this setting, prompt structure is fundamental for performance, and recent results show that even simple random search can achieve strong performance when combined with sophisticated prompt design. Recently, it has been observed that harmful behaviors can be elicited even without the adversarial prompt, relying solely on optimized token sequences. This suggests the existence of natural backdoors, i.e., token sequences naturally emerged during LLMs training that trigger unsafe outputs without any meaningful instruction. However, despite these observations, this setting remains largely unexplored, and in particular the hardness of finding natural backdoors has not been assessed yet. In this work, we provide a first proof-of-concept study investigating the hardness of this task, which we refer to as the junking problem. We formalize it as the problem of finding token sequences that maximize the probability of generating a target prefix of harmful responses, propose a greedy random-search method to assess is such sequences can be discovered easily. Our results show that this problem is harder than standard jailbreak attacks, confirming the importance of semantic information in prompt design. At the same time, we find that our simple strategy is sufficient to solve it with a high success rate, suggesting that natural backdoors are present and easily recoverable. Finally, through perplexity analysis, we observe that the discovered token sequences lie in low-probability regions of the model distribution, supporting the hypothesis that they emerged implicitly from the training process.

</details>


### [204] [Physiologically Grounded Driver Behavior Classification: SHAP-Driven Elite Feature Selection and Hybrid Gradient Boosting for Multimodal Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05120)
*Sahar Askari,Mohammad Mahdi Mirza Ali Mohammadi,Fatemeh Ensafdoust,Amin Golnari,Saeid Sanei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态生理信号（EEG、EMG、GSR）的可解释且可扩展的驾驶行为解码框架，通过SHAP特征选择、贝叶斯优化和加权软投票集成（XGBoost + LightGBM），在大型数据集上实现了80.91%的测试准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，消融实验验证了多模态融合相比单模态带来8%的性能提升，且SHAP分析表明EEG贡献了主要预测权重，GSR和EMG在高唤醒和运动密集型操作中提供关键判别信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶行为解码研究多依赖单一模态或黑箱模型，缺乏可解释性与多模态融合的系统性框架，难以全面捕捉驾驶过程中的生理状态变化。

Method: 基于EEG、EMG、GSR三种模态的生理信号，经预处理后提取时域、频域及导出生理指标特征；采用SHAP进行精英特征选择（保留Top 250）；通过Optuna对XGBoost和LightGBM进行贝叶斯超参数优化；最终构建加权软投票集成模型。

Result: 集成模型测试准确率80.91%，宏F1分数0.79；相比最优单模态（EEG）提升8%；SHAP分析显示EEG贡献主要预测权重，GSR和EMG在高唤醒与运动密集型操作中提供关键判别信息。

Conclusion: 多模态生理信号融合结合可解释性分析能够有效解码驾驶行为，所提框架兼具性能优势与生理可解释性，验证了多模态融合的必要性。

Abstract: An interpretable and scalable framework for decoding driving behaviors from multimodal physiological signals is proposed in this study. We utilize multimodal physiological driving behavior large-scale dataset comprising synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and galvanic skin response (GSR) signals. Our approach involves rigorous preprocessing followed by a domain-specific feature extraction pipeline targeting time-domain, frequency-domain, and derived physiological indices. To address high dimensionality, we employ SHAP-based elite feature selection, retaining the top 250 features to reduce computational overhead while preserving predictive power. Hyperparameter optimization for extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) and light gradient boosting machine (LightGBM) models is conducted using Bayesian optimization via Optuna. Finally, a weighted soft-voting ensemble is constructed to leverage the complementary strengths of both gradient boosting frameworks. The results demonstrate that the proposed ensemble achieves a test accuracy of 80.91% and a macro-F1 score of 0.79, significantly outperforming single-modality baselines and traditional machine learning models. Ablation studies confirm an 8% performance gain over the best single modality (EEG), validating the necessity of multimodal fusion. SHAP analysis further validates the physiological plausibility of the model, revealing that the EEG contributes the majority of predictive weight, GSR and EMG features provide critical discriminatory signals for high-arousal and motor-intensive maneuvers.

</details>


### [205] [Conditional outlier detection for clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05124)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Shyam Visweswaran,Iyad Batal,Gilles Clermont,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于电子健康记录数据的异常检测方法，用于识别术后患者管理中可能存在的错误，经专家评估表明该方法具有合理的低误报率，且异常强度与警报率正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录系统中存储的大量患者数据未被充分利用来检测潜在的管理错误，传统的规则驱动警报系统可能存在局限性，因此需要一种数据驱动的异常检测方法来识别不寻常的患者管理行为，这些行为可能暗示潜在错误。

Method: 利用4,486名心脏手术后患者的电子健康记录数据，开发了一种数据驱动的异常检测方法，通过比较当前患者的管理行为与历史患者的相似性来判断是否异常，并基于专家小组的意见进行评估。

Result: 异常驱动的警报系统具有合理的低误报率，且异常强度越强，警报率越高，两者之间存在正相关关系。

Conclusion: 基于异常的警报方法在患者管理错误检测中具有可行性，能够以较低的误报成本实现有效的警报，为临床决策支持系统提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We develop and evaluate a data-driven approach for detecting unusual (anomalous) patient-management actions using past patient cases stored in an electronic health record (EHR) system. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to past patients may be due to a potential error and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. We evaluate this hypothesis using data obtained from the electronic health records of 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients. We base the evaluation on the opinions of a panel of experts. The results support that anomaly-based alerting can have reasonably low false alert rates and that stronger anomalies are correlated with higher alert rates.

</details>


### [206] [Low-Cost Black-Box Detection of LLM Hallucinations via Dynamical System Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05134)
*Dan Wilson,Mohamed Akrout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Koopman算子理论的单样本幻觉检测方法，将LLM视为黑箱动力系统，通过嵌入模型投影响应序列，学习事实与幻觉两种状态下的转移算子，并利用差分残差分数进行检测，同时引入偏好校准机制。该方法无需二次采样或外部知识，在三个基准测试上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉检测方法通常依赖于计算昂贵的基于采样的一致性检查或外部知识检索，缺乏轻量级、高效的检测手段。

Method: 将LLM视为黑箱动力系统，通过嵌入模型将响应投影到高维流形；利用Koopman算子理论分别学习事实和幻觉状态下的转移算子；定义基于预测误差的差分残差分数；引入偏好感知校准机制优化分类阈值。

Result: 在三个数据基准上，该方法以更低的资源开销达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 提出的方法实现了低成本、单样本通路的幻觉检测，避免了二次采样或外部知识依赖，具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently generate plausible but non-factual content, a phenomenon known as hallucination. While existing detection methods typically rely on computationally expensive sampling-based consistency checks or external knowledge retrieval, we propose a new method that treats the LLM as a black-box dynamical system. By projecting LLM responses into a high-dimensional manifold via an embedding model, we characterize the resulting vector sequences as observable realizations of the model's latent state-space dynamics. Leveraging Koopman operator theory, we fit the transition operators for both factual and hallucinated regimes and define a differential residual score based on their respective prediction errors. To accommodate varying user requirements and domain-specific sensitivities, we introduce a preference-aware calibration mechanism that optimizes the classification threshold based on a small set of demonstrations. This approach enables low-cost hallucination detection in a single-sample pass, avoiding the need for secondary sampling or external grounding. Extensive testing across three data benchmarks demonstrates that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with reduced resource overhead.

</details>


### [207] [Human-AI Co-Mentorship in Project-Based Learning: A Case Study in Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05144)
*Freyaa Chawla,Ahan Chawla,Rishi Singh,Joe Germino,Grigorii Khvatskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了一个由高中生和低年级本科生组成的团队在研究生导师和AI工具辅助下开展的研究项目。项目强调通过AI工具加速工作流程，使学生在有限背景下仍能专注于高阶问题定义与解决方案，最终成功构建了具有实际应用的金融模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI工具如何帮助缺乏AI和金融背景的高中生绕过传统课堂式学习，直接聚焦于高阶问题定义与方案设计，实现高效的研究型学习。

Method: 采用工作流驱动的方法：学生先识别解决问题的步骤序列，再利用AI工具执行每个步骤；通过每日站会进行调试和概念答疑；学生与AI迭代交互生成代码。

Result: 高中生成功开发了技术市场分析和ETF价格预测模型；每位学生能深入各自兴趣领域（计算机科学或金融）；团队在2025年夏季取得了显著进展。

Conclusion: AI工具能有效赋能技术背景有限的高中生，使其聚焦于问题定义与概念理解；每日站会在保持研究方向聚焦上起关键作用；该模式是重要的教学法实践，展示了AI在指导高中生研究中的潜力。

Abstract: This paper reflects on a AI research project carried out by a team of high-school and early-undergraduate students under the mentorship of graduate researchers and ably assisted by AI tools. We share our experience in not only on the learning experience for the high school students, but also on how AI tools accelerated the process that enabled the high school students to focus on higher order problem formulation and solution. Although the participants entered the project with limited background in both AI and finance, they showed strong enthusiasm for technical market analysis and ETF price prediction. Traditional learning settings would first teach the necessary methods in a classroom setting and only later let students apply them. In contrast, our project emphasized workflow design: students identified the sequence of steps needed to address the problem and then used AI-driven tools to execute each step.
  We note that the high school students developed the necessary code through iterating with the AI tools, and we used our daily stand-ups to debug and answer conceptual questions. Each of the student was able to dig deeper into their area of interest whether computer science or finance, while collaboratively making a significant advance over the summer of 2025. This project was an important pedagogical exercise on how AI tools can be used for mentoring high school students, allowing them to focus on their specific interests and using the daily stand-ups to focus on problem definition and conceptual understanding. Despite their limited technical qualifications, the students were able to leverage AI tools to build meaningful models with real-world application.

</details>
